Method: 作者采用可解析的两层线性网络，并在非线性网络中开展数值模拟，系统考察网络表征与功能之间的关系，并检验输入噪声鲁棒性、参数噪声鲁棒性以及泛化误差水平对表征的约束作用。

Result: 研究发现：功能与表征是解耦的——可以存在表征相似而无功能相似，也可以功能相似而无表征相似；对输入噪声的鲁棒性和泛化误差水平均不能将表征约束到任务本身；相反，对参数噪声鲁棒的网络其表征灵活性受限，必须采用任务特定的表征。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，表征对齐反映的是超越单纯功能对齐的计算优势，这对理解和比较连接主义系统的表征具有重要启示。

Abstract: A foundational principle of connectionism is that perception, action, and cognition emerge from parallel computations among simple, interconnected units that generate and rely on neural representations. Accordingly, researchers employ multivariate pattern analysis to decode and compare the neural codes of artificial and biological networks, aiming to uncover their functions. However, there is limited analytical understanding of how a network's representation and function relate, despite this being essential to any quantitative notion of underlying function or functional similarity. We address this question using analysable two-layer linear networks and numerical simulations in non-linear networks. We find that function and representation are dissociated, allowing representational similarity without functional similarity and vice versa. Further, we show that neither robustness to input noise nor the level of generalization error constrain representations to the task. In contrast, networks robust to parameter noise have limited representational flexibility and must employ task-specific representations. Our findings suggest that representational alignment reflects computational advantages beyond functional alignment alone, with significant implications for interpreting and comparing the representations of connectionist systems.

</details>


### [105] [Synchronous Multi-view Neural Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39019)
*Yongquan Shi,Weijun Huang,Yueyang Pi,Wendi Zhao,Yiqing Shi,Shiping Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出同步多视图神经扩散（SynMDiff），将多视图特征空间视为由扩散过程驱动的统一动力系统，实现视图内与视图间信息的同步自适应融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多视图融合策略将视图内融合与视图间融合视为相互独立的阶段，未同时考虑视图内的演化与视图间的依赖关系。这种异步融合范式受限于各视图特有的结构归纳偏置冲突，制约了跨视图交互，导致信息流在中间路径上易被扭曲和压缩，使模型局限于有限的解空间。

Method: 作者提出SynMDiff，将多视图特征空间建模为由扩散过程驱动的统一动力系统，通过在联合空间中对任意二元特征交互的扩散流建模，实现并发的、自适应的视图内与视图间信息融合。针对该同步机制直接实现计算成本过高的问题，进一步引入基于能量的拓扑采样策略和Ego-Net式中心化训练架构，以保证学习与推理的效率与可扩展性。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上的评估表明，SynMDiff大幅超越基线方法。

Conclusion: SynMDiff凭借其概念上的简洁性与计算高效性，为多视图学习提供了一种同步融合的新范式，并在实验中验证了其优越性能。

Abstract: Multi-view learning seeks to learn more comprehensive representations by exploiting the complementarity and consistency across diverse modalities or views. However, existing multi-view fusion strategies treat intra- and inter-view fusion as independent stages, without simultaneously considering the evolution within views and the dependency across views. Such an asynchronous fusion paradigm inevitably constrains cross-view interactions due to conflicting view-specific structural inductive biases. As a result, information flow is prone to distortion and compression along intermediate pathways, confining the model to learn within a restricted solution space. To address this, we propose Synchronous Multi-view Neural Diffusion (SynMDiff), which conceptualizes the multi-view feature space as a unified dynamical system driven by a diffusion process. By modeling the diffusion flow across arbitrary dyadic feature interactions in a joint space, SynMDiff enables the concurrent and adaptive intra- and inter-view information fusion. While a direct implementation of this synchronized mechanism incurs prohibitive computational costs, we further introduce an energy-based topological sampling strategy and an Ego-Net style centralized training architecture, ensuring both efficiency and scalability during learning and inference. Due to its conceptual elegance and computational efficacy, evaluations on real-world datasets demonstrate that SynMDiff outperforms the baselines by a large margin.

</details>


### [106] [A Rank Graduation metric for Algorithmic fairness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39025)
*Dalia Atif,Paolo Giudici*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于秩的公平性评估框架，通过模型预测误差分布将公平性、预测准确性与可解释性联系起来，并在模拟与HMDA抵押贷款数据上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统公平性评估多依赖群体层面的均等性指标（parity measures），这类指标难以揭示哪些个体遭遇不公平，以及哪些解释性因素导致了不公平。因此需要一种能连接公平性、预测准确性与可解释性的新框架。

Method: 提出基于秩的框架：结合秩晋级公平性（Rank Graduation Fairness, RGF）、其集成测度AURGF、中心化Cramér–von Mises置换检验，以及用于公平性可解释性的特征移除程序。使用逻辑回归、随机森林、梯度提升和多层感知机进行模拟研究与HMDA抵押贷款数据实证。

Result: 模拟显示受保护群体不平衡会反转描述性公平性比较，而所提推断程序能正确区分公平与不公平机制。HMDA数据应用中，模型排序不同于经典公平性准则的结果；树模型在预测准确性与秩公平性上表现最优组合；四个模型的公平性零假设均被拒绝。跨统计、bagging、boosting与神经网络设定下差异持续存在，结合特征移除结果，表明不公平并非源于单一算法或预测变量，而是与贷款数据特征中固有的群体差异相关。

Conclusion: 该框架支持一种更广泛的值得信赖的人工智能路径，将预测准确性、公平性测量、统计推断与可解释性结合起来。

Abstract: Fairness assessment in algorithmic decisions that affect individuals, such as credit scoring, often relies on parity measures calculated at the aggregate group level. Such measures may not reveal which individuals experience unfairness or which explanatory factors contribute to it. In this paper, we propose a rank-based framework that evaluates fairness through the distribution of model prediction errors, thereby linking fairness assessment with predictive accuracy and explainability. The framework combines Rank Graduation Fairness (RGF), its integrated measure AURGF, a centered Cramer--von Mises permutation test, and a feature removal procedure for fairness explainability.
  We evaluate the methodology using logistic regression, random forest, gradient boosting, and a multilayer perceptron. The simulation study shows that protected-group imbalance can reverse descriptive fairness comparisons, whereas the proposed inferential procedure correctly distinguishes fair from unfair mechanisms. Its application to HMDA mortgage data produces model rankings that differ from those obtained with classical fairness criteria. Tree-based models, rather than logistic regression, provide the strongest combination of predictive accuracy and rank-based fairness, while the fairness null hypothesis is rejected for all four models. The persistence of disparity across statistical, bagging, boosting, and neural network specifications, together with the feature removal results, indicates that the observed unfairness is not specific to a single algorithm or predictor, but is associated with group differences embedded in the characteristics of the lending data. These findings support a broader approach to trustworthy artificial intelligence that combines predictive accuracy, fairness measurement, statistical inference, and explainability.

</details>


### [107] [Switching Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39034)
*Hyun Dong Lee,Xavier Gonzalez,Nicolas Zucchet,E. Kelly Buchanan,Emily B. Fox,Scott W. Linderman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 SwiLA（Switching Linear Attention），一种新型序列层，在保持线性注意力固定大小循环状态的同时增强表示能力，缩小了与 softmax 注意力的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 softmax 注意力性能优异但 KV 缓存随序列长度线性增长，限制可扩展性；线性注意力推理高效且内存恒定，但表示能力弱导致建模性能较差。本文旨在弥合二者之间的差距。

Method: 从测试时回归框架推导 SwiLA 循环，将状态更新规则建模为线性回归混合模型中的在线期望最大化（EM）。测试时，每个输出维度根据输入在多个线性注意力组件间动态选择。

Result: 在联想回忆、上下文语言学习和语言建模基准上，SwiLA 表现强劲，缩小了与 softmax 注意力的差距，并在若干设置中实现超越。

Conclusion: SwiLA 通过切换机制兼顾高效推理与强表示能力，是线性注意力与 softmax 注意力之间的有效折中方案。

Abstract: Designing expressive sequence layers with efficient inference remains a central challenge in modern machine learning. Standard softmax attention achieves excellent sequence modeling performance through rich nonlinear token interactions, but it requires a key-value cache that grows linearly with sequence length, limiting its scalability. Linear attention enables efficient recurrent computation with a constant memory footprint, yet its reduced expressivity often yields inferior modeling performance. We introduce Switching Linear Attention (SwiLA), a novel sequence layer that bridges this gap by enhancing representational capacity while retaining the fixed-size recurrent state of linear attention. We derive the SwiLA recurrence from the test-time regression framework, casting the state update rule as online expectation-maximization in a mixture of linear regressions model. At test time, each output dimension dynamically selects among multiple linear attention components based on the input. Across associative recall, in-context language learning, and language modeling benchmarks, SwiLA shows strong performance and narrows the gap to softmax attention, even surpassing it in several settings.

</details>


### [108] [Cycle-Aware Autoencoder with Cross-SignalConsistency for Railway Door Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39035)
*Ammar Bouketta,Smail Niar,Hamza Ouarnoughi,Eva Mutuzo Brindle*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出时序循环感知注意力自编码器（TCAA-CS），将列车车门状态监测建模为循环级无监督异常检测，仅用正常循环训练，在真实工业数据上取得93.8%召回率、97.3%精确率和0.5%误报率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车门是轨道车辆的安全关键子系统，但实际运行中故障稀少、种类多样且常无标签，导致异常行为检测困难；同时需满足车载实时部署需求。

Method: 将每次完整的"开门—保持—关门"循环作为监测单元，构建循环级无监督异常检测框架。模型采用双流编码器，分别通过独立1D-CNN分支处理连续物理量（位置、电流、电压）与二值逻辑状态（门关、门锁）；结合LSTM编码器与时间注意力池化；设计融合重构误差、潜空间偏差和相位感知跨信号一致性的三重混合异常评分，以捕捉单信号看似合理但信号间物理或逻辑关系不一致的情形。

Result: 在商业运营客运列车的真实工业数据上，TCAA-CS达到93.8%召回率、97.3%精确率和0.5%误报率，优于代表性无监督基线；在NVIDIA Jetson AGX Xavier上的系统级评估验证了实时车载部署的可行性。

Conclusion: TCAA-CS为铁路车门状态监测提供了高精度、低误报且可车载实时运行的无监督异常检测方案，具备实际工程部署价值。

Abstract: Passenger access doors are safety-critical subsystems in railway vehicles, yet detecting abnormal door behavior in real operation is challenging because faults are rare, diverse, and often unlabeled. This paper addresses railway door condition monitoring as a cycle-level unsupervised anomaly detection problem, where each complete opening-dwell-closing cycle is treated as a single monitoring unit. We propose the Temporal Cycle-Aware Attention Autoencoder with Cross-Signal Consistency (TCAA-CS), trained exclusively on nominal cycles. It combines a dual-stream encoder that processes continuous physical measurements (position, current, voltage) and binary logical states (door-closed, door-locked) through separate 1D-CNN branches, an LSTM encoder with temporal attention pooling, and a triple hybrid anomaly score fusing reconstruction error, latent-space deviation, and phase-aware cross-signal consistency. The consistency term helps identify cases where individual signals appear plausible but their inter-signal relationships become physically or logically inconsistent. On real industrial data from a passenger train in commercial service, TCAA-CS achieves 93.8% recall, 97.3% precision, and a 0.5% false-alarm rate, outperforming representative unsupervised baselines. System-level evaluation on an NVIDIA Jetson AGX Xavier supports the feasibility of real-time onboard deployment.

</details>


### [109] [Hard-Gate Candidacy in a Deployed Validator Suite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39037)
*Xin Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文对一个已部署生成式智能体中的13个验证器进行筛查，用边际分离度J=TPR−FPR评估其区分好坏构建的能力，发现多数验证器无法与零区分，且执行系统本身存在偏向性，导致检测上限约为84%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证器要被提升为部署流水线的硬性关卡，必须先证明其触发能有效区分对用户可用与不可用的输出。作者希望以实证方式检验这一前提是否成立，并揭示将验证器当作门禁时的系统性缺陷。

Method: 在已部署的生成式智能体上对13个验证器进行筛查，使用550个运行时构建与350个静态构建，按下游结果标注；计算每个检查的边际分离度J=TPR−FPR，并采用Newcombe区间与Fisher精确检验；此外进行四次运行共1867个构建、10个不同运行时检查的复现实验，并分析人类标定的空白构建检测器及大规模评审记录。

Result: 两个检查通过多重比较校正，两个仅名义显著，其余九个与零不可区分（其中三个从未触发）。执行本身与被门禁的属性不随机相关且可复现：895个损坏构建中144个被跳过探测，972个可接受构建中仅1个被跳过（每轮比率15.6%–16.6% vs 至多0.3%）。由于跳过的检查记为通过，检测上限约为84%。针对空白输出的检测器在90个人工标注空白构建上触发0次（灵敏度95%上界3.3%），其近似全局统计量AUC仅0.59。在大规模评审中，32.5%的拒绝没有任何记录问题。

Conclusion: 评估记录必须区分“已运行并通过”与“未运行”的检查，必须携带拒绝的证据；仅有一份检查清单并不能构成关于门禁有效性的证据。

Abstract: Before a validator can be promoted to a hard gate on a deployment pipeline, it has to be shown that its firing separates outputs that reach users in working order from those that do not. We run that screen on 13 validators in a deployed generative agent, against 550 runtime and 350 static builds labelled by downstream outcome, and report each check's marginal separation $J=\mathrm{TPR}-\mathrm{FPR}$ with Newcombe intervals and Fisher exact tests. Two checks survive correction for multiple comparisons, two more are nominal only, and the remaining nine are not distinguishable from zero, three of them because they never fired on any sampled build. Execution itself is not random with respect to the property being gated, and this replicates: across four runs covering 1,867 builds and ten distinct runtime checks, probes were skipped on 144 of 895 broken builds and 1 of 972 acceptable builds (per-run rates 15.6% to 16.6% against at most 0.3%), every skip carrying the same unsafe-to-probe reason. Because a skipped check is recorded as a pass, this imposes a ceiling that no check quality can lift: a check that needs a live artifact cannot operationally detect more than about 84% of broken builds in this harness. For the one check with construct-specific labels, a detector built for blank output fires on 0 of 90 human-labelled blank builds (95% upper bound on sensitivity 3.3%), and the global frame statistic it approximates separates the classes only weakly (AUC 0.59), so the gap is not a threshold that needs tuning. The same gap appears one layer up: on a census of tens of thousands of judge-scored builds, 32.5% of rejections carry no recorded issue at all. We argue that evaluation records must distinguish a check that ran and passed from one that did not run, must carry the evidence for a rejection, and that an inventory of checks is not evidence about a gate.

</details>


### [110] [Structure-aware Reinforcement Learning for Protein Directed Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39048)
*Zikun Nie,Suyuan Zhao,Yizhen Luo,Siqi Fan,Zaiqing Nie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出StructEvo，一种结构感知的强化学习蛋白质定向进化框架，通过delta结构融合编码器与分层动作网络，在优化基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习辅助定向进化方法主要依赖序列特征，忽略了蛋白质结构中的空间约束与共进化相互作用；但直接整合结构信息面临可靠突变体结构稀缺的难题。

Method: StructEvo采用delta结构融合编码器，通过特征差异近似突变体结构特征，实现空间知识的动态引入；并利用结构对齐的分层动作网络将庞大突变空间分解为可管理的子空间，同时引入几何约束稳定delta特征学习。

Result: 在两个具有挑战性的优化基准上，分别比此前最先进方法提升9.2%和16.3%，并在GFP中识别出经实验验证的上位性模式。

Conclusion: 结构引导对有效的蛋白质定向进化具有重要意义，StructEvo验证了结构感知方法在克服突变结构稀缺问题上的有效性。

Abstract: Protein optimization remains a longstanding goal in life sciences. Existing machine learning-assisted directed evolution (MLDE) methods primarily rely on sequence-only features, overlooking the critical spatial constraints and co-evolutionary interactions encoded in protein structures. However, directly integrating structural information remains challenging due to the scarcity of reliable mutant structures. To address these issues, we propose StructEvo, a novel structure-aware reinforcement learning framework for protein directed evolution. StructEvo employs a delta-structure fusion encoder to approximate mutant structure features via feature differences, enabling dynamic incorporation of spatial knowledge. The vast mutation space is then decomposed into manageable subspaces through a structure-aligned hierarchical action network, while a geometric constraint further stabilizes delta feature learning. Our approach outperforms prior state-of-the-art methods by 9.2% and 16.3% on two challenging optimization benchmarks, and further identifies an experimentally validated epistasis pattern in GFP, highlighting the importance of structural guidance for effective protein directed evolution.

</details>


### [111] [The Missing Coefficients: Bayesian Pairwise Merging for Model Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39055)
*Yaling Shen,Tongtong Wu,Siyuan Yan,Gholamreza Haffari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Bayesian Pairwise Merging (BPM)，通过后验推断将成对偏好转化为奖励权重系数，实现无需训练的用户个性化专家合并。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户更自然地以成对选择表达偏好，而非指定数值权重；现有方法需给定权重向量才能合并专家，且反馈有限时存在歧义。挑战在于把成对选择转为合并所需系数并处理不确定性。

Method: 将未知奖励权重视为隐变量，从成对选择与奖励分数差推断后验，直接以后验均值作为合并系数；该方法称为 BPM，其后验还能刻画哪些奖励权衡仍合理。

Result: 在放射报告摘要、图像描述、故事生成上评测（含文本到文本与图像到文本）。每人设 100 条反馈时，对均匀合并的宏观已决胜率为 91.7%、77.1%、64.3%；六对模拟人设各自偏好基于自身反馈拟合的模型，人类概念验证也观察到该模式。仅 10 与 25 次比较时，名义 90% 区间在温度缩放奖励权重上的任务平均边际覆盖率为 88.9% 与 89.2%。

Conclusion: BPM 无需逐用户策略训练即可实现基于成对反馈的个性化，同时刻画有限反馈导致的系数歧义。

Abstract: How can we personalize a shared expert library from a user's pairwise choices? Prior work can realize different reward trade-offs by merging reward-specialized experts, given a vector of trade-off weights. In practice, users can more naturally choose between outputs than specify numerical weights. The challenge is therefore to turn these choices into the coefficients required for merging, while accounting for ambiguity when feedback is limited. Our key idea is to treat the unknown reward weights as latent variables: infer a posterior over them from pairwise choices and reward-score differences, and use its mean directly as the merge coefficients. We instantiate this idea as Bayesian Pairwise Merging (BPM), whose posterior also characterizes which reward trade-offs remain plausible given the feedback. We evaluate BPM on radiology summarization, image captioning, and story generation, spanning text-to-text and image-to-text generation. With 100 feedback per simulated persona, BPM achieves macro decided win rates of 91.7%, 77.1%, and 64.3% against uniform merge. For six pairs of simulated personas, each prefers the model fitted to its own feedback, a pattern also observed in a human proof-of-concept. In simulations under BPM's model and prior, its nominal 90% intervals for temperature-scaled reward weights achieve task-averaged marginal coverage of 88.9% and 89.2% with only 10 and 25 comparisons, respectively. BPM thus enables personalization from pairwise feedback without per-user policy training, while characterizing the coefficient ambiguity left by limited feedback.

</details>


### [112] [SparseEngine: Sparse-First Inference Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39068)
*Jitai Hao,Quansheng Gu,Qiang Huang,Jun Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 SparseEngine，一个稀疏优先的高效推理引擎，通过统一生命周期契约支持 15 种稀疏注意力方法，并引入 Chain Cache 与 Prefix-Cache Pruning，实现超 10 倍吞吐提升与 2 倍以上端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文 LLM 智能体累积的交互历史会加重 KV-cache 内存与注意力计算负担。稀疏注意力虽能降低成本，但异构的缓存表示与工作流难以与现有推理引擎集成，且既有稀疏服务抽象仅支持特定布局或工作流，缺乏通用性。

Method: 提出从底层设计的稀疏优先推理引擎 SparseEngine，其共享生命周期契约允许各方法自主控制 KV 表示与计算，同时与通用服务基础设施协调状态转换。引擎支持四大类共 15 种方法，并通过 Chain Cache 从保留历史中恢复 KV 淘汰方法、通过可控 Prefix-Cache Pruning 在保留逻辑前缀匹配的前提下移除选定历史区域的 KV，实现跨请求状态管理。

Result: 在保持方法质量的前提下，SparseEngine 在 KV 淘汰下吞吐量提升超 10 倍，在相同并发下解码速度比 vLLM 快 2.5 倍以上，并在智能体基准上实现 2 倍以上的端到端加速。

Conclusion: SparseEngine 以稀疏优先架构和统一生命周期契约解决了稀疏注意力与现有推理引擎的集成难题，兼顾通用性与方法质量，显著提升吞吐与解码效率，代码已开源。

Abstract: Long-context LLM agents accumulate interaction histories that strain KV-cache memory and attention computation. Although sparse attention reduces these costs, heterogeneous cache representations and workflows hinder integration with existing inference engines, while prior sparse-serving abstractions support only specific layouts or workflows. We present SparseEngine, a ground-up, sparse-first inference engine whose shared lifecycle contract lets each method control its KV representation and computation while coordinating state transitions with common serving infrastructure. SparseEngine supports 15 methods across four categories and enables cross-request state management through Chain Cache, which resumes KV-eviction methods from retained history, and controllable Prefix-Cache Pruning, which removes KV from selected history regions while preserving logical-prefix matching. While maintaining method quality, SparseEngine delivers over 10x higher throughput with KV eviction, over 2.5x faster decoding at matched concurrency than vLLM, and over 2x end-to-end speedup on agent benchmarks. The code is available at https://github.com/CURRENTF/SparseEngine.

</details>


### [113] [HO-FL: Hybrid-Order Federated Learning for Heterogeneous Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39074)
*Qiyuan Chen,Xian Wu,Yanan Ma,Xianhao Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HO-FL混合阶联邦学习框架，用零阶优化训练模型底层、一阶优化训练顶层，让内存受限设备灵活选择阶数边界，并给出收敛分析与采样优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在内存受限的边缘设备上存在两难：一阶（反向传播）优化需要大量内存，而零阶优化虽省内存但收敛速度严重下降。需在内存与收敛效率间取得平衡。

Method: HO-FL框架：模型底部用零阶优化、顶部用一阶优化训练；各设备按内存预算灵活选择阶数边界，同时参与同一全局模型训练。通过收敛分析揭示新权衡——一阶训练段更大的客户端更新更精确，但偏重它们会使其他客户端数据代表性不足。将该权衡与多步本地更新的偏差-方差联系起来，转化为采样优化问题，并给出实用的维度感知近似与直接模型平均。

Result: 在语言任务上考察任务性能、客户端内存与数据异构下的采样。结果显示混合阶本地训练能在显著降低客户端内存需求的同时保留大部分全一阶性能。代码已开源。

Conclusion: HO-FL通过混合阶训练有效缓解内存与收敛的两难，并给出了关于采样偏差-方差的新理论视角，为内存受限边缘设备上的联邦学习提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) on memory-constrained edge devices faces a dilemma: first-order (FO) optimization (i.e., backpropagation) demands substantial memory, whereas zeroth-order (ZO) optimization suffers from severe convergence slowdown. To resolve this dilemma, we introduce HO-FL, a hybrid-order FL framework that trains a model's bottom segment with ZO optimization and its top segment with FO optimization. Each device can flexibly select its order boundary according to its memory budget while participating in the training of the same global model. Moreover, our convergence analysis reveals a new, fundamental trade-off: clients with larger FO-trained segments can provide more accurate updates, but favoring them can underrepresent other clients' data. We connect this trade-off to the bias and variance of actual multi-step local updates, yielding a sampling optimization problem and a practical dimension-aware approximation with direct model averaging. Experiments on language tasks examine task performance, client memory, and sampling under data heterogeneity. The results show that hybrid-order local training can retain much of the full-FO performance with substantially lower client memory requirements. Our code is available at https://github.com/HKU-WILL-Lab/HO-FL.

</details>


### [114] [Parameter symmetries determine representational geometry in overparameterized nonlinear networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39078)
*Marvin Theiss,Lukas Braun,Andrew M. Saxe,Erin Grant*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究神经网络中"函数约束表征"的程度问题，揭示参数对称性如何通过三种基本特征变换作用于表征，并给出表征几何的闭式分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习、心理学和神经科学常通过表征推断生物与人工系统的计算，但这种推断预设了表征几何与计算之间存在有意义的联系。对于人工神经网络，函数在多大程度上约束表征尚不清楚，其中一个关键障碍是网络存在参数对称性——即改变参数化能精确保持函数不变，却重塑了表征几何。

Method: 作者分析了一类广泛的参数对称性，证明它们仅通过三种基本特征变换作用于表征：加法（addition）、复制（duplication）和缩放（scaling）。基于这一特征级刻画，推导出表征几何的闭式分解，将其分为本质（essential）与辅助（auxiliary）两个成分。

Result: 该分解精确刻画了在函数保持不变的情况下，表征几何的简并性（degeneracy）如何随过度参数化而增长。作者进一步表明，实现层面的选择规则可以消解这种简并性，产生可识别的几何——其中特征按其对该网络函数的贡献程度加权。

Conclusion: 研究结果明确了表征在何时能够支持关于计算的推断、何时不能，为表征分析与计算推断的适用边界提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Representations are routinely used across machine learning, psychology, and neuroscience to draw inferences about the computations of biological and artificial systems. Such inferences presume a meaningful link between representational geometry and the computation being performed. For artificial neural networks, however, the extent to which function constrains representation remains unclear. One key obstacle is that these networks admit parameter symmetries: changes in parameterization that preserve function exactly while reshaping representational geometry. Here, we show that a broad class of parameter symmetries acts on representations through just three primitive feature transformations: addition, duplication, and scaling. This feature-level characterization yields a closed-form decomposition of representational geometry into essential and auxiliary components, which makes precise how degeneracy in representational geometry can grow with overparameterization even when function is held fixed. Finally, we show that implementation-level selection rules can resolve this degeneracy, yielding identifiable geometries in which features are weighted according to their contributions to the network's function. Together, our results delineate when representations can support inferences about computation, and when they cannot.

</details>


### [115] [Shared Phase and Retention Control for Efficient Adaptive Spectral Recurrence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39082)
*Wentao Wang,Hengyu Zhong,Yunhan Jiang,Jialiang An,Meng Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 SPARC，仅用两个输入相关的标量信号即共享相位与保留控制，统一协调高维复频记忆模式的衰减与旋转，在保持常数内存推理的同时实现高效自适应谱循环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序列模型需随新证据更新记忆及其对预测的影响。Transformer 的计算与缓存成本随上下文增长，定状态循环模型虽可常数内存推理，但线性与谱循环依赖静态转移，无法动态修正记忆的衰减或旋转；近期选择性架构虽引入输入相关转移，却为每个记忆模式分配独立控制，使控制成本与状态容量耦合。

Method: 作者论证高维谱记忆不需要高维控制，提出 SPARC（Shared Phase and Retention Control for Efficient Adaptive Spectral Recurrence）。SPARC 仅使用两个输入相关的标量信号，跨异构复数模式协调记忆保留与相位旋转，同时保留各模式的基线时间尺度与频率。其对角仿射循环支持序列级 BPTT 的并行关联扫描，以及用于在线信用分配的精确结构化实时循环学习（RTRL）。

Result: 在部分可观测连续控制、POPGym 与序列分类任务上，SPARC 相比次优方法在 Walker-P 上取得 9.09% 的相对回报提升，在 FordA 上取得 1.36% 的相对准确率提升。在 NVIDIA Blackwell GPU 上，其实现将固定 token 工作负载的循环混合器训练延迟降低 18.2%–34.2%，扫描速度相比优化的 RG-LRU 基线加速 3.1x–4.7x。

Conclusion: 两个共享控制信号即可在在线与全序列设定下高效治理自适应谱记忆，表明高维谱记忆无需高维控制即可实现动态自适应，兼顾性能、内存与计算效率。

Abstract: As new evidence arrives, a sequence model must update what it remembers and how memory influences predictions. While Transformers incur computation and cache costs scaling with context length, fixed-state recurrent models offer constant-memory inference. However, linear and spectral recurrences traditionally rely on static transitions, failing to dynamically revise how stored representations decay or rotate. While recent selective architectures introduce input-dependent transitions, they assign independent controls to every memory mode, coupling control cost to state capacity. We show that high-dimensional spectral memory does not require high-dimensional control, and introduce Shared Phase and Retention Control for Efficient Adaptive Spectral Recurrence (SPARC). SPARC employs just two input-dependent scalar signals to coordinate memory retention and phase rotation across heterogeneous complex modes, while preserving mode-specific baseline timescales and frequencies. Its diagonal affine recurrence supports parallel associative scans for sequence-level BPTT as well as exact structured Real-Time Recurrent Learning (RTRL) for online credit assignment. Across partially observable continuous control, POPGym, and sequence classification, SPARC achieves a 9.09% relative return improvement on Walker-P and a 1.36% relative accuracy gain on FordA over second-best methods. On an NVIDIA Blackwell GPU, our implementation reduces recurrent-mixer training latency by 18.2%-34.2% in fixed-token workloads and accelerates scans by 3.1x-4.7x over an optimized RG-LRU baseline. These results show that two shared control signals can efficiently govern adaptive spectral memory across online and full-sequence settings. Code is available at https://github.com/Botwwt/sparc.

</details>


### [116] [Learning Infinite-Horizon Average-Reward CMDPs via State Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39093)
*Kihyun Yu,Seoungbin Bae,Dabeen Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个在弱通信假设下、针对无限视界平均奖励约束马尔可夫决策过程（CMDP）的计算高效算法，能以高概率同时达到 $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(\sqrt{T})$ 的遗憾和累积约束违反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对该设定（弱通信无限视界平均奖励CMDP）的高概率保证，要么依赖计算效率低下的算法，要么在交互次数 $T$ 上的依赖是次优的。作者希望同时实现计算高效与最优的 $T$ 依赖。

Method: 将累积约束违反纳入状态，并通过 Huber 势函数的差分定义重塑奖励。新增状态决定对进一步违反的惩罚，而奖励函数在增广状态空间上保持固定。由于新增状态具有已知的确定性动态，只需估计原始转移核。Huber 势函数的有界斜率使每步奖励有界，势差可望远镜求和，将重塑回报与原累积奖励及末端势联系起来，从而可应用有限视界近似与带裁剪的乐观值迭代。

Result: 首个计算高效算法，在表格设定下以高概率同时达到 $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(\sqrt{T})$ 遗憾与累积约束违反；该 $\sqrt{T}$ 依赖在忽略对数因子意义下是最优的。

Conclusion: 通过状态增广与 Huber 势重塑奖励，可在不恶化 $T$ 的遗憾速率前提下，复用无约束平均奖励 MDP 中的有限视界近似与乐观值迭代技术，实现高效的约束强化学习。

Abstract: We study infinite-horizon average-reward constrained Markov decision processes (CMDPs) under the weakly communicating assumption. Existing high-probability guarantees for this setting either require computationally inefficient algorithms or have suboptimal dependence on the number of interactions $T$. We propose, to the best of our knowledge, the first computationally efficient algorithm that achieves $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(\sqrt{T})$ regret and cumulative constraint violation with high probability in the tabular setting. The $\sqrt{T}$ dependence is optimal up to logarithmic factors. Our approach incorporates cumulative constraint violation into the state and defines a reshaped reward through differences of a Huber potential. The added state determines the penalty on further violations while the reward function remains fixed on the augmented state space. Since the added state has known deterministic dynamics, only the original transition kernel needs to be estimated. The bounded slope of the Huber potential keeps the per-step reward bounded, and the potential differences telescope to relate the reshaped return to the original cumulative reward and the terminal potential. These properties allow us to apply finite-horizon approximation and optimistic value iteration with clipping, as used in unconstrained average-reward MDPs, without worsening the regret rate in $T$.

</details>


### [117] [A Generalisation Signal Need Not Be a Model-Selection Signal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39099)
*Aditya Nagarsekar,M P Ashish Bhat,Aadi Nesarkar,Vrishti Godhwani,Rahul Yedida,Aditya Challa,Danda Sravan,Snehanshu Saha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文检验了"用网络自身几何性质（Hessian范数等）替代验证数据来挑选模型"这一想法，发现在分子性质、蛋白适应性和药物反应任务上，几何代理指标虽与泛化差距相关，却不能可靠地选出部署最优模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算生物学中模型选择常依赖来自训练分布内的验证数据，但部署场景往往落在该分布之外，此时验证集可能无法正确区分哪个模型最好。因此作者想探索一种替代方案：仅利用已训练网络自身的性质（如Hessian范数所启发的"前向代理"指标）对候选模型进行排序，并检验验证相关性恶化时几何指标是否更有用。

Method: 作者提出一种新的、仅前向传播的代理指标（受Hessian范数启发），并与常见Hessian度量（Hessian迹、最大特征值）一起，在分子性质预测、蛋白适应度预测和药物反应三类任务上进行评测；比较几何指标与验证Spearman相关性、泛化差距及部署最优模型识别之间的关系。

Result: 与假设相反，几何指标并未在验证Spearman相关性变差时变得更有用：增强验证对某些分布偏移有帮助，但对另一些则显著有害。更意外的是，即便Hessian迹和最大特征值的关系很弱甚至反转，该代理指标在多数任务上仍与泛化差距相关，但这种信号无法可靠识别部署最优模型。曲率界不必然保持跨模型排序，低几何分数甚至可能偏向退化的（坍缩）预测器。

Conclusion: 研究结论是：泛化信号不等于模型选择信号。一个指标能反映泛化差距，并不意味着它能可靠地用于跨模型排序和模型选择；几何性质在部署分布外进行模型选择时并不比验证数据更可靠。

Abstract: Model selection in computational biology often relies on validation data drawn from the training regime, even when deployment lies outside it. When validation no longer preserves which model is best, a natural alternative is to rank candidates using properties of the trained network itself. We test this idea using a novel, forward-only proxy motivated by the norm of the Hessian, alongside common Hessian measures, across molecular property, protein fitness, and drug-response tasks. Contrary to our hypothesis, geometry does not become more useful as validation Spearman correlation deteriorates: augmenting validation helps some shifts but significantly harms others. More surprisingly, the proxy still correlates with generalisation gap on most tasks even when Hessian trace and top-eigenvalue relationships are weak or reversed, yet this signal does not reliably identify the deployment-best model. A curvature bound need not preserve cross-model rankings, and low geometric scores can even favour collapsed predictors. Thus, a generalisation signal need not be a model-selection signal.

</details>


### [118] [T-Router: Learning Thalamic Routing for Reasoning with Parameter-Efficient Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39109)
*Liuxian Ma,Jiale Dai,Jiaqi Li,Lu Mi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Thalamic Router (T-Router)，将参数高效强化学习的适配集中于已完成计算的复用，在 8.95B 主干上仅训练 0.466% 参数即超越全参 GRPO 与 LoRA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数高效强化学习希望以紧凑的可训练接口提升预训练模型的推理能力，但如何在不改动主干参数的前提下有效利用已有计算尚缺乏具体计算形式。

Method: T-Router 维护一个压缩、可寻址的块变化库，保存各块的变换；由深度循环控制器决定其选择与相对缩放回写，从而学习接收层使用哪些早前贡献及其影响强度。正确性奖励仅训练该接口，保持主干参数与层序不变。

Result: 在 8.95B 主干上，T-Router 仅分配 41.73M 参数（0.466%），GSM8K RL 后 MathAvg 达 83.64±1.16，优于全参 GRPO 的 73.79±1.83；在相同参数预算与匹配重试下超过 LoRA 的 77.28±1.95，三个任务族全部提升，AIME 平均准确率由 48.33 升至 60.56。

Conclusion: 容量受控的对比表明可寻址块变化与循环上下文更优；独立搜索训练可将接口扩展到工具中介推理。结果确立受控计算复用是参数高效推理强化学习的有效路径。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient reinforcement learning aims to improve reasoning with a compact trainable interface to a pretrained model. We introduce the Thalamic Router (T-Router), which concentrates adaptation on the reuse of completed computations. A compressed, addressable bank preserves block changes; a depth-recurrent controller conditions their selection and relative-scale writeback. This coupling gives thalamic context-dependent routing a concrete computational form: learn which earlier contributions a receiving layer uses, and with what influence. Correctness rewards train the interface while preserving backbone parameters and layer order. On an 8.95B-parameter backbone, T-Router allocates 41.73M parameters (0.466% of the backbone) and achieves 83.64 +/- 1.16 MathAvg after GSM8K RL, compared with 73.79 +/- 1.83 for full-parameter GRPO across three evaluation rounds. At a comparable parameter budget and with matched retries, it exceeds LoRA's 77.28 +/- 1.95 MathAvg, improving all three task families and raising mean AIME accuracy from 48.33 to 60.56. Capacity-controlled comparisons favor addressable block changes and recurrent context; separate search training extends the interface to tool-mediated reasoning. These results establish controlled computation reuse as an effective route to parameter-efficient reasoning reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [119] [The Row Normalization Puzzle in Muon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39114)
*Jiayu Zhang,Tianyi Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究行归一化对 Muon 优化器的影响，揭示 NorMuon 的最坏情况保证与实际表现之间的差距，并提供算法相关的下界与匹配的上界分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NorMuon 在大语言模型预训练中逐渐流行且表现良好，但其最坏情况收敛保证仍缺乏深入理解。核心问题是：行归一化是否带来可证明的收敛增益，尤其是通过与近似极坐标计算和指数移动平均动量的交互。

Method: 作者在算子范数几何下分析行归一化对迭代复杂度的影响，建立与算法相关的下界和确定性场景下的匹配上界，并将上界分析扩展到随机场景；上界分析均允许近似极坐标计算。

Result: 行归一化在最坏情况迭代复杂度中引入了维度相关因子，该因子即使使用精确极坐标计算和任意固定动量参数也依然存在。实验显示 NorMuon 在基于最坏情况构造的合成问题上比 Muon 更慢，但在 LLM 预训练中优于 Muon。

Conclusion: 这些发现深化了"为何行归一化在实践中有效"这一谜题，并补充了 Dewulf 等人的近期研究。

Abstract: This paper examines how row-wise renormalization affects Muon, focusing on the gap between NorMuon's worst-case guarantees and its practical performance (Li et al.). Despite its growing adoption and promising performance in large language model (LLM) pretraining, NorMuon's worst-case guarantees remain poorly understood. One fundamental question is: Does row normalization yield provable convergence gains, potentially through its interaction with approximate polar computation and exponential moving-average momentum? Our results show that row normalization introduces a dimension-dependent factor in the worst-case iteration complexity under the operator-norm geometry, which persists even with exact polar computation and any fixed momentum parameters. Indeed, we establish an algorithm-dependent lower bound and a matching upper bound in deterministic settings, and extend our upper bound analysis to stochastic settings. Both upper-bound analyses allow approximate polar computation. Experiments show that NorMuon is slower than Muon on synthetic problems inspired by our worst-case construction, yet outperforms Muon in LLM pretraining. These findings sharpen the puzzle of why row normalization helps in practice and complement the recent findings of Dewulf et al.

</details>


### [120] [Prequential E-Values for Selected-GP Near-Optimality Certificates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39123)
*Ami Tavory,Noa Cohen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出用prequential e-values对GP-UCB停止规则中的置信包络选择进行可审计化，从而在自适应调参并认证时保持anytime-valid性，显著降低误认证风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在昂贵的黑盒函数顺序优化（如超参数优化）中，人们希望在最优值被认证在全局最优ε范围内时停止。这需要两部分：所选值的下置信界与定义域上的上置信包络（通常由高斯过程GP提供）。GP-UCB式停止规则在核函数与常数事先固定时有效，但实践中常从同一自适应评估数据中调整包络并当作固定值来认证，这会破坏有效性，导致误认证。

Method: 使用prequential e-values使包络选择可审计：从预先声明的、完全指定的GP/RKHS包络集合出发，用每个候选自身的一步预测e-process进行检验，被否定的候选被删除，认证时采用幸存候选中最大的上界。在有有效的选中点下界、且至少一个声明候选具有有效潜在覆盖与噪声校准的情况下，该规则是anytime-valid的。

Result: 在512-seed噪声RBF压力扫描中，与"先拟合再认证"相比，该方法在功率相当的情况下将误认证风险大致减半。在光滑的d=3、4目标上，相对于随机固定GP预承诺，每增加一次误认证分别伴随3.0和13.5次额外正确认证。

Conclusion: 通过prequential e-values对包络候选进行逐步检验与淘汰，可在自适应选择下维持停止规则的anytime-valid性，在保持认证能力的同时显著降低误认证风险，为昂贵黑盒优化提供了可审计的停止认证方案。

Abstract: When optimizing an expensive black-box function sequentially, as in hyperparameter optimization, we may want to stop once the best evaluated value is certified within $\varepsilon$ of the global optimum. Such a certificate needs two ingredients: a lower confidence bound for the selected value and an upper confidence envelope over the domain, typically supplied by a Gaussian process (GP). GP-UCB-style stopping rules are valid when the kernel and constants defining this envelope are fixed before the run, but the practical temptation is to tune the envelope from the same adaptive evaluations and then certify as if it had been fixed. We use prequential e-values to make this selection auditable: starting from a predeclared set of fully specified GP/RKHS envelopes, each candidate is tested by its own one-step-ahead e-process, contradicted candidates are deleted, and certification uses the largest upper bound among the survivors. With a valid selected-point lower bound and one declared candidate having valid latent coverage and noise calibration, the rule is anytime-valid. On a 512-seed noisy RBF stress sweep, it roughly halves false-certification risk at comparable power versus fit-then-certify. Relative to random fixed GP precommitment on smooth $d=3,4$ objectives, each additional false certificate is accompanied by 3.0 and 13.5 additional correct certificates, respectively.

</details>


### [121] [CDMD: A Cross-Dataset Mixed-Type Diffusion Model for Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39124)
*Mohamed Amine Ketata,Maximilian Schambach,Stephan Günnemann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CDMD，一种直接在混合类型特征空间上进行扩散的表格扩散模型，可跨异构数据集联合训练，实现知识迁移并减少模型存储。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格生成模型通常针对每个数据集单独训练，限制了知识迁移，并需要存储大量专用模型。现有跨数据集表格扩散模型多在连续表示空间操作，存在局限。

Method: CDMD直接在混合类型特征空间定义扩散并端到端训练。为适应异构类别域，提出模式受限的反向过程参数化用于掩码扩散，输出空间动态适配各特征词表；将数值与类别特征级扩散组合为模式相关的行级过程；使用共享模式感知Transformer去噪器捕获特征依赖并参数化反向过程。

Result: 在七个真实数据集上，单个联合训练的CDMD取得最高平均生成质量，优于强单数据集与跨数据集基线，且总参数量显著减少。在337个数据集语料上预训练，可在有限目标数据与有限适配轮次下提升未见数据集上的生成效果。

Conclusion: 结果表明直接混合类型扩散在共享与可迁移表格数据生成方面具有潜力。

Abstract: Generative models for tabular data are typically trained separately for each dataset, limiting knowledge transfer and requiring the storage of many specialized models. In this paper, we introduce CDMD, a tabular diffusion model trained jointly across heterogeneous datasets with different schemas and variable numbers of numerical and categorical features. Unlike existing cross-dataset tabular diffusion models that operate in continuous representation spaces, CDMD defines diffusion directly over the mixed-type feature space and is trained end-to-end. To accommodate heterogeneous categorical domains, we introduce a schema-restricted reverse-process parameterization for masked diffusion models, in which the output space dynamically adapts to each feature's vocabulary. We then compose numerical and categorical feature-level diffusion processes into a schema-dependent row-level process. A shared schema-aware Transformer denoiser captures dependencies between features and parameterizes the reverse process across varying schemas. On seven real-world datasets, a single jointly trained CDMD achieves the highest average generation quality among strong single-dataset and cross-dataset baselines, while using substantially fewer total parameters than the collection of separately trained models. Furthermore, pre-training on a corpus of 337 datasets improves generation on previously unseen datasets under both limited target data and limited adaptation epochs. These results demonstrate the potential of direct mixed-type diffusion for shared and transferable tabular data generation. Our code is available at https://github.com/ketatam/cdmd.

</details>


### [122] [ID Balancing: Stable Training of Extremely Sparse MoE via PID-Based Load Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39137)
*Peng Jin,Zihan Qiu,Zekun Wang,Bo Zheng,Yang Xu,Tian Xie,Xiao Li,Huaqing Zhang,Haoran Lian,Rui Men,Dayiheng Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 ID Balancing，一种基于控制理论视角的专家负载均衡方法，用于解决稀疏 MoE 模型中的专家负载不均衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE 通过稀疏路由扩展参数规模，但更稀疏的路由会加剧专家负载不均衡，降低参数利用率与训练效率，并影响训练稳定性，成为可靠扩展的瓶颈。

Method: 作者将两种无辅助损失方法统一为不完整的 PID 控制器：DeepSeek 的无损失方法视为固定步长积分控制器，Kimi K3 的分位数均衡视为广义比例控制器。在此基础上提出 ID Balancing（积分-微分控制器），其积分项随负载误差缩放，微分项仅在失衡恶化时激活。

Result: 在 768 个专家的 Top-10、Top-5、Top-3 路由设置下评测，Top-3 时相比最佳基线，最差情况骨干 MaxVio 降低超 50%，训练平均骨干 MinVio 降低超 12%。参数从 18.9B 增至 69.9B（Top-10-of-768）时，ID Balancing 的最差情况骨干 MaxVio 几乎不变，且比辅助损失基线低约 89.6%。

Conclusion: ID Balancing 保持了有竞争力的语言建模与下游性能，其优势随稀疏度增加而增强，是扩展更大、更稀疏 MoE 模型的有前景方案。

Abstract: Scaling Large Language Models (LLMs) via Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) enables massive parameter growth with nearly constant per-token computation. However, further scaling the parameter count requires increasingly sparse routing, where expert load imbalance becomes more severe. This imbalance reduces parameter utilization and training efficiency, and can undermine training stability, becoming a bottleneck to reliable scaling. In this work, we unify two representative auxiliary-loss-free methods as incomplete Proportional-Integral-Derivative (PID) controllers: DeepSeek's loss-free method acts as a fixed-step integral controller, while Kimi K3's Quantile Balancing functions as a generalized proportional controller. Building on this control perspective, we propose ID Balancing, an Integral-Derivative controller. It scales its integral term with load error and activates its derivative term only when imbalance worsens, enabling stronger corrections for large or worsening errors and smaller updates near balance. Evaluated across Top-$10$, Top-$5$, and Top-$3$ routing over $768$ experts, ID Balancing reduces worst-case backbone MaxVio and training-average backbone MinVio by over $50\%$ and $12\%$, respectively, relative to the best baselines in the Top-$3$ setting. When the total parameter count increases from $18.9$B to $69.9$B (Top-$10$-of-$768$), ID Balancing's worst-case backbone MaxVio remains nearly unchanged and is approximately $89.6\%$ lower than that of the auxiliary-loss baseline. ID Balancing also maintains competitive language-modeling and downstream performance. The advantages of ID Balancing grow as sparsity increases, making it a promising solution for scaling larger, sparser MoE models.

</details>


### [123] [Sharp Stationary Gaussian Approximation for Constant-Stepsize SGD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39144)
*Junghoon Seo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文证明了常步长SGD（噪声由均匀遍历马尔可夫链生成）不变分布的尖锐高斯逼近，误差在1-Wasserstein距离下为O(√α)，并用四状态例子给出匹配下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究常步长随机梯度下降（SGD）迭代的不变分布能否用高斯分布很好地逼近，尤其是在噪声由马尔可夫链生成而非独立同分布的情形下，需要刻画逼近的精确阶数。

Method: 针对光滑、强凸且Hessian Lipschitz的目标函数与非退化长期噪声协方差，结合分块高斯比较（blockwise Gaussian comparison）与长期收缩（long-run contraction）技术进行证明；并以四状态例子分析高阶矩的影响。

Result: 中心化并用步长平方根归一化后的迭代，在1-Wasserstein距离下与极限高斯分布相差O(√α)；四状态例子给出匹配下界，尽管单时刻噪声边缘分布对称且所有非零滞后自协方差为零，其相邻三阶混合矩产生了主导修正项。

Conclusion: 常步长SGD不变分布的高斯逼近阶数是尖锐的O(√α)，且马尔可夫噪声的高阶混合矩（而非二阶自协方差）可能是逼近误差的主导来源。

Abstract: We prove a sharp Gaussian approximation for the invariant law of constant-stepsize SGD with bounded additive noise generated by an exogenous uniformly ergodic Markov chain. For a smooth, strongly convex objective with a Lipschitz Hessian and nondegenerate long-run noise covariance, the centered iterate normalized by the square root of the stepsize is $O(\sqrtα)$-close in 1-Wasserstein distance to its limiting Gaussian. The proof combines blockwise Gaussian comparison with long-run contraction. A four-state example gives a matching lower bound although the one-time noise marginal is symmetric and every nonzero-lag autocovariance vanishes. In this example, an adjacent third-order mixed moment produces the leading correction.

</details>


### [124] [QuanVI: Score-based Variational Inference via Quantum Maximally Mixed States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39164)
*Yuchen Cong,Zerui Tao,Chao Li,Zhe Sun,Qibin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QuanVI：结合混合态密度算符与量子张量网络（MPO）参数化的可扩展量子启发式评分变分推断算法，解决高维下参数爆炸与简并子空间特征向量不唯一的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于评分的变分推断通过最小化Fisher散度替代KL型VI，其先前的特征值形式将优化转化为特征值问题，用低能本征态构造变分分布。但该形式面临两个高维障碍：一是参数数量随维度指数增长，二是简并或近简并低能子空间中单个本征向量不唯一。

Method: 提出QuanVI：将混合态密度算符形式与量子张量网络（QTN）参数化结合，采用矩阵乘积算符（MPO）结构。用最大混合态表示简并低能子空间，避免依赖不唯一的单个本征向量；用QTN参数化压缩密度算符，避免参数指数增长。

Result: 实验与消融表明，QuanVI在低维情形下与精确解一致，并可扩展到高维合成数据与贝叶斯后验近似基准，包括具有挑战性的非高斯目标。

Conclusion: QuanVI通过密度算符与MPO张量网络参数化的结合，有效缓解了评分变分推断在高维下的参数爆炸与简并子空间不唯一问题，展现出良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Score-based variational inference (VI) provides an alternative to Kullback--Leibler (KL)-based VI by minimizing the Fisher divergence between the variational distribution and the target. A prior score-VI approach formulates this optimization as an eigenvalue problem, with the variational distribution constructed from low-energy eigenstates. However, this eigenvalue-based formulation faces two high-dimensional obstacles: an intractably large parameter count due to exponential scaling and non-uniqueness of individual eigenvectors in degenerate or nearly degenerate low-energy subspaces. We propose QuanVI, a scalable quantum-inspired algorithm that combines a mixed-state density-operator formulation with a quantum tensor network (QTN) parameterization using the matrix product operator (MPO) structure. In degenerate low-energy subspaces, the density-operator formulation represents the subspace by its maximally mixed state rather than relying on a non-unique individual eigenvector, while the QTN parameterization compresses the density operator to avoid exponential parameter growth. Experiments and ablations show that QuanVI agrees with exact solutions in low dimensions and scales to high-dimensional synthetic and Bayesian posterior-approximation benchmarks, including challenging non-Gaussian targets.

</details>


### [125] [Whitening Improves Robustness to Spurious Correlations in Linear Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39177)
*Floris Holstege,Bram Wouters,Noud van Giersbergen,Cees Diks*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究线性探针依赖虚假特征的问题，发现最大间隔分类器偏好协方差矩阵大特征值方向，白化可消除此偏好，从而提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络倾向于依赖简单但可能是虚假的特征，导致泛化失败。作者希望在不需预先知道虚假相关性存在、也不需要标注数据的情况下，减少这种依赖。

Method: 在（预训练）模型表示上拟合广义线性探针，利用其与最大间隔分类器的联系进行分析；提出将白化作为预处理步骤，通过均衡协方差矩阵的特征值，消除模型对大特征值方向的偏好。

Result: 在合成数据生成过程和标准虚假相关基准上检验白化效果，发现白化提升鲁棒性；将白化加入现有方法时同样能提升鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 白化是一种无需先验知识或标注数据、可有效减少对虚假相关性依赖的预处理手段，且可与现有方法兼容叠加使用。

Abstract: Deep neural networks tend to rely on simple features that may be spurious and thus fail to generalize. We study this problem in the setting of linear probes, where a (generalized) linear model is fitted on the representations of a (pretrained) model. We use the connection of these models to the max-margin classifier, and show they favor directions associated with large eigenvalues of the covariance matrix. Whitening removes this preference by equalizing the eigenvalues of the covariance matrix. This observation motivates whitening as a preprocessing step that can reduce reliance on spurious correlations without requiring prior knowledge of their presence or labeled data. We examine the effect of whitening on a synthetic data-generating process and standard spurious correlation benchmarks, and find that it improves robustness. We also find that whitening can improve robustness when added to existing approaches.

</details>


### [126] [Low-Discrepancy Dither for Quantized Recurrent State Caches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39185)
*Snigdha Chandan Khilar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出用确定性的黄金比例Weyl抖动（无需随机数）替代随机舍入，作为Mamba类与混合语言模型循环状态低精度缓存的舍入规则，在多种模型、存储格式和长解码场景下都更接近全精度模型，且无额外开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mamba类与混合语言模型将历史压缩进固定大小的循环状态，并在每生成一个token时重写。低精度存储可节省内存带宽，但每次舍入误差会反馈进下一次更新，在长序列生成中不断累积。工业系统采用随机舍入，本文追问这类缓存应采用何种舍入规则。

Method: 研究并比较不同舍入规则：确定性黄金比例Weyl抖动（不需随机数）、随机舍入、以及舍入到最近值（round-to-nearest）；在纯模型与混合模型、不同存储格式、长解码时间跨度下进行评估，并通过差异（discrepancy）分析解释排序原因，同时记录会悄然抹除收益的实现陷阱。

Result: 确定性黄金比例Weyl抖动在纯模型与混合模型、各种存储格式和长解码跨度下，始终比随机舍入让量化模型更接近全精度模型，且无额外成本。舍入到最近值因丢弃小更新导致误差持续增长，短期评估中看似最优，但长生成中大幅落后。

Conclusion: 循环状态低精度缓存应采用确定性黄金比例Weyl抖动而非随机舍入；差异分析解释了不同舍入规则的优劣排序；实现中存在的陷阱会无声地消除该方法的收益，需加以注意。

Abstract: Mamba-style and hybrid language models compress their past into a fixed-size recurrent state that is rewritten at every generated token. Storing this state in low precision saves memory bandwidth, but every rounding error is fed back into the next update and can accumulate over long generations. Production systems round the state stochastically; we ask which rounding rule such caches should use. We find that a deterministic golden-ratio Weyl dither, which needs no random numbers, consistently brings the quantized model closer to the full-precision one than stochastic rounding, across pure and hybrid models, storage formats, and long decoding horizons, at no extra cost. Round-to-nearest behaves differently: because it discards small updates, its error keeps growing, so it can look best in short evaluations yet falls far behind over long generations. A discrepancy analysis explains this ordering, and we document implementation pitfalls that silently remove the benefit.

</details>


### [127] [Attention Function as an Intrinsic Inductive Bias: How Models' Behavior Diverges in Novel Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39188)
*Dong Gyun Kang,Megha Thukral,Kwangsoo Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出MoFA（Mixture of Function Attention），在多注意力头中固定softmax与sigmoid头比例，发现该比例在同分布下影响可忽略，但在零样本分布偏移下影响显著回升，且最优比例沿单一领域结构轴变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展心理学认为某些先验是婴儿先天具备而非从数据归纳的，其影响在强约束条件下被抑制、在弱约束条件下重新显现。作者希望检验Transformer是否具有类似原理：注意力头所配的激活函数能否充当一种内在归纳偏置。

Method: 提出MoFA，一种对多头注意力无参数的修改方式，在训练前固定softmax头与sigmoid头的比例。实验使用124M参数GPT-2模型、5种比例、5个随机种子，在分布内及15个分布外领域上评估。

Result: 分布内不同比例差异统计上可忽略，即使极端比例差异也小；但在零样本分布偏移下差异急剧放大，多个领域困惑度差距扩大一个数量级以上。最优比例沿单一领域结构轴变化，将短文本、非正式文本（偏好softmax）与技术性长文本（偏好sigmoid）区分开，解释78.3%的领域响应方差。头层面可见sigmoid头熵随比例上升加速下降，softmax头响应较温和，形成稳定的分工。

Conclusion: 激活函数选择可作为一种"给定先验"，其影响在同分布下被掩盖、在分布外重新显现，为Transformer中的归纳偏置研究提供了新视角。

Abstract: Developmental psychology holds that certain priors are given to infants prior to experience rather than induced from data, and that the influence of such priors is suppressed under strong, well-constrained conditions but reasserts itself under weak ones. We ask whether an analogous principle holds for the Transformer: can the activation function given to attention heads serve as an intrinsic inductive bias? We propose Mixture of Function Attention (MoFA), a parameter-free modification to multi-head attention that fixes a ratio of softmax and sigmoid heads before training. Across five ratios, a 124M-parameter GPT-2 model, and five seeds, we find that this given ratio has little effect in-distribution -- differences between ratios are statistically negligible for moderate mixtures and remain small even at the extremes -- but its influence re-emerges sharply under zero-shot distribution shift across 15 out-of-distribution domains. Perplexity gaps between ratios widen by more than an order of magnitude on several domains, and the best-performing ratio tracks a single axis of domain structure, separating short, informal text (softmax-favoring) from technical, long-form text (sigmoid-favoring), that explains 78.3% of the variance in domain response. This reorganization is visible at the head level: sigmoid heads show an accelerating drop in attention entropy as their ratio increases, while softmax heads respond more modestly, yielding a consistent division of labor between the two head types. Our results suggest that activation choice functions as a given prior whose influence is masked in-distribution and re-emerges out-of-distribution.

</details>


### [128] [Dynamics to decision: A mathematical theory of Lyapunov spectra and decision boundaries in deep classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39190)
*Shirin Panahi,Amirhossein Nazerian,Ali Pezeshki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将前馈分类器建模为有限非自治离散动力系统，以层为时间步，研究数据轨迹的有限时间最大Lyapunov指数(FTMLE)，揭示决策边界几何特征如何随深度演变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度分类器不仅由决策结果定义，还由形成该决策的逐层变换序列定义。将这一过程视为跨层的动力系统，可自然地追问：决策几何如何随深度涌现，以及能在多大深度上追溯边界的动力学特征。

Method: 将前馈分类器建模为有限非自治离散动力系统，层对应离散时间步；计算样本轨迹的FTMLE以度量邻近轨迹的收敛/发散速率；将观测端点从概率回退到logits再到隐藏表示；针对高斯类别给出概率层FTMLE与决策边界法向对齐的证明，并分析logits层与隐藏表示层的条件性联系；提出几何感知微调与高隐藏FTMLE在边界附近集中化的条件。

Result: 高斯类别下，概率层FTMLE具有清晰的边界几何特征，其主导方向与边界法向一致；回退到logits层时该关系不再普遍成立，而关键依赖于训练方式尤其是损失函数选择；进一步回退到隐藏表示时，边界相关FTMLE仅在可识别的结构条件下才能保持；数值实验验证了理论结果的普遍性与有效性。

Conclusion: 将样本视为穿越分类器各层的过程，为识别边界相关敏感性的涌现位置以及设计层感知正则化策略提供了原则性基础。

Abstract: A deep classifier is defined not only by the decision it produces, but also by the sequence of transformations through which that decision is formed. Treating this evolution as a dynamical system across layers provides a natural framework for asking how decision geometry emerges through depth and how far back we can trace a boundary's dynamical signature. We model a feed-forward classifier as a finite, nonautonomous discrete dynamical system, with layers playing the role of discrete time steps. We study the Finite-Time Maximum Lyapunov Exponent (FTMLE) of the data samples' dynamical trajectory through depths of the classifier. The FTMLE measures the rate of convergence/divergence of nearby trajectories. We move the observation endpoint backward from probabilities to logits and then to hidden representations. For Gaussian classes, we prove that probability-level FTMLE carries a clear geometric signature of the decision boundary, with its dominant direction aligned with the boundary normal. Moving one step backward to the logits, we prove this relationship is no longer universal but depends critically on how the classifier is trained, particularly on the choice of loss function. Moving further backward to the hidden representation, the connection becomes more conditional: boundary-related FTMLE can persist, but only under identifiable structural conditions. We propose geometry-aware fine-tuning for restructuring the classifier's hidden FTMLE, and propose conditions for guaranteed concentration of high hidden FTMLE near the decision boundary. Through our numerical results, we show the generality and validity of our theoretical results. Understanding the evolution of data samples as traveling through the layers of classifier provides a principled foundation for identifying where boundary-relevant sensitivity emerges and for developing layer-aware regularization strategies.

</details>


### [129] [Importance-Aware Feature Sparsification for Wireless Split Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39194)
*Bumjun Kim,Yoon Huh,Wan Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出重要性感知的类别平衡稀疏化(ICS)方法,通过服务器端Grad-CAM评分排序特征通道并生成类别平衡的重要性向量,客户端复用该向量保留top-N通道,在不增加客户端计算开销的前提下缓解无线分割学习的通信瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线分割学习(SL)虽将上层卸载至服务器以降低端侧计算,但每次迭代传输高维中间特征仍构成主要通信瓶颈。现有客户端侧特征选择方法依赖幅度、统计量或聚类等任务无关准则,一方面增加客户端处理负担,另一方面在非i.i.d.客户端数据下常导致精度下降。

Method: 服务器利用反向传播中真实类别logit得到的Grad-CAM分数对特征通道排序;将各类别分数聚合为类别平衡、与标签无关的重要性向量,以缓解标签偏斜下的头部类别偏差;客户端在下一轮复用该向量保留top-N特征通道,无需额外的客户端前向或反向传播。此外推导了非渐近收敛界以分离稀疏化误差,并分析了通信与计算开销,同时将方法扩展至并行分割学习和Transformer模型。

Result: 实验表明ICS持续优于基线方法,在严重的非i.i.d.数据划分下增益更为显著。

Conclusion: ICS是一种轻量、任务感知且类别平衡的分割学习特征稀疏化方案,在不增加客户端计算的同时有效降低通信开销,并具备理论收敛保证与跨架构(CNN/Transformer、串行/并行SL)的通用性。

Abstract: Wireless split learning (SL) reduces on-device computation by offloading upper layers to a server, yet transmitting high-dimensional intermediate features at each iteration remains a major communication bottleneck. Existing methods select features at the client side using task-agnostic criteria such as magnitude, statistics, or clustering, which increases client-side processing and often degrades accuracy under non-independent and identically distributed (non-i.i.d.) client data. We propose importance-aware class-balanced sparsification (ICS), a lightweight approach in which the server ranks feature channels using Grad-CAM-based scores obtained from the true-class logit during backpropagation. The per-class scores are aggregated into a class-balanced, label-agnostic importance vector that mitigates head-class bias under label skew, and each client reuses this vector in the next round to retain the top-$N$ feature channels, incurring no additional client-side forward or backward passes. We further derive a non-asymptotic convergence bound that isolates the sparsification-induced error and characterizes how the sparsification ratio and mini-batch size jointly affect convergence under a fixed communication budget, and we analyze the communication and computational overhead of ICS against representative baselines. Beyond sequential CNN-based SL, we extend ICS to parallel split learning and to transformer-based models. Experiments show that ICS consistently outperforms the baselines, with larger gains under severe non-i.i.d. partitions.

</details>


### [130] [In a Streaming World, Should You Stand Still? A Comprehensive Benchmark of Anomaly Detection in Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39215)
*Magali Parrino,Antoine Ajenjo,Emmanuel Remy,Pierre Stephan,Pierre Senellart,Paul Boniol*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次大规模实验比较流式与静态时间序列异常检测方法，发现在多数流式场景下静态方法显著优于流式方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式TSAD方法多源自流式离群点检测文献，忽视时间序列异常的核心特性；且实验多在合成或小规模基准上，难以判断流式方法在真实TSAD场景中是否真有优势。

Method: 构建统一的流式评测基准，设定初始批次数据用于训练、随后在线评估检测精度与计算效率；并提出基于真实时间序列的分布漂移数据集TSB-drift，用于隔离流式更新理论上成立的场景。

Result: 在多数流式设置下，静态TSAD方法显著优于流式方法，与常见假设相悖。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了现有流式方法设计与现代TSAD需求之间的关键差距，呼吁重新思考如何将流式能力整合进TSAD。

Abstract: Time series anomaly detection (TSAD) is increasingly deployed in streaming settings, where data arrive sequentially and may exhibit non-stationarity. As a result, several works from the recent literature propose streaming anomaly detection methods that rely on incremental updates to adapt over time. However, most of these approaches originate from the streaming outlier detection literature and largely ignore core characteristics of time series anomalies. Moreover, their empirical evaluation is typically conducted on synthetic or small-scale benchmarks with limited diversity, making it unclear whether streaming methods are truly advantageous in realistic TSAD scenarios. In this work, we carry out the first large-scale experimental study comparing streaming and static TSAD methods under a unified streaming evaluation benchmark. We consider a realistic setting in which an initial batch of data is available for model training, followed by online evaluation of both detection accuracy and computational efficiency. In addition, we propose a distribution-drift dataset of real time series, called TSB- drift, to isolate scenarios where streaming updates are theoretically justified. Our results show that, contrary to common assumptions, static TSAD methods significantly outperform streaming approaches in most streaming settings. Such finding highlights a critical gap between the design of existing streaming methods and the requirements of modern TSAD, and calls for a rethinking of how streaming capabilities should be integrated into TSAD.

</details>


### [131] [QATFactory: A Versatile, Deployment-Aligned Framework for Quantization-aware Training and Distillation of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39223)
*Weili Xu,Jisen Li,Yuqing Jian,Chenxi Li,Zhizhou Sha,Yifan Yu,Qingyang Wu,Chenfeng Xu,Zhongzhu Zhou,Tianyi Zhang,Ben Athiwaratkun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QATFactory是一个开源框架，通过在BF16中模拟部署量化以实现量化感知蒸馏与强化学习，支持NVFP4、MXFP4、Q4_K等格式，可直接导出至vLLM和llama.cpp，在8B至230B模型上显著提升部署质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理正转向低精度以发挥硬件加速器吞吐，但激进的训练后量化（PTQ）会损害模型质量；同时许多训练硬件（如H100）缺少目标格式（如FP4）的原生支持，导致量化感知训练难以开展。

Method: 提出QATFactory框架，在BF16下进行矩阵乘法、模拟部署时量化，使模型适应量化噪声而无需原生支持目标格式的硬件；支持NVFP4、MXFP4及llama.cpp的Q4_K格式，覆盖稠密与混合专家模型、全参数与LoRA训练，并可将检查点直接导出至vLLM和llama.cpp而无需有损转换或额外推理开销。

Result: 在8B至230B模型上于生产推理引擎中评估：QAD在各模型和格式上均优于强PTQ基线。Qwen3.5-9B在NVFP4下平均准确率68.9%（PTQ最佳65.4%），MXFP4下66.0%（PTQ最佳56.4%）。发现最佳训练策略依赖格式：NVFP4仅在训练时量化权重更优，MXFP4则需同时量化权重与激活；在固定token预算下，用更少32K序列训练比更多4K序列平均准确率高1.9分。

Conclusion: QATFactory验证了部署对齐的量化感知蒸馏/强化学习能有效提升低精度部署模型质量，且训练策略需随目标格式调整；框架与检查点均已开源。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) inference is increasingly moving toward lower precision to realize the throughput of hardware accelerators, but aggressive post-training quantization (PTQ) can degrade model quality. We present QATFactory, an open-source framework for deployment-aligned quantization-aware distillation (QAD) and reinforcement learning (QARL). QATFactory simulates deployment-time quantization while performing matrix multiplications in BF16, allowing models to adapt to quantization noise without requiring training hardware that natively supports the target format; for example, it supports NVFP4 training on H100 GPUs, which lack FP4 Tensor Cores. The framework supports NVFP4, MXFP4, and llama.cpp's Q4_K format; dense and mixture-of-experts models; and both full-parameter and LoRA-based training. It exports checkpoints directly to vLLM and llama.cpp without an additional lossy conversion step or added inference overhead. With QATFactory, we conduct extensive experiments on models ranging from 8B to 230B parameters and evaluate exported checkpoints in production inference engines. Across models and formats, QAD consistently improves deployed-model quality over strong PTQ baselines. On Qwen3.5-9B, QAD achieves average benchmark accuracies of 68.9% under NVFP4 and 66.0% under MXFP4, outperforming the best PTQ results of 65.4% and 56.4%, respectively. Through our experiments, we found that although both FP4 formats quantize weights and activations at deployment, the best training strategy is format-dependent: NVFP4 generally performs better when only weights are quantized during training, whereas MXFP4 benefits from quantizing both weights and activations. At a fixed training token budget, training on fewer 32K sequences improves average accuracy by 1.9 points over training on more 4K sequences. We release the complete QATFactory training code and the resulting checkpoints.

</details>


### [132] [What Streaming Anomaly Detection Finds (and Misses) in Industrial Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39232)
*Magali Parrino,Antoine Ajenjo,Emmanuel Remy,Pierre Stephan,Paul Boniol*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EDF在真实核电站数据上对比了流式方法与在线部署的最新时间序列异常检测（TSAD）模型，结果表明在线TSAD一致性更高，集成策略鲁棒性强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EDF需要对电厂进行持续监测以便第一时间发现异常，但在无监督场景下不存在通用最优的流式方法，因此需要实证比较不同方法的在线表现。

Method: 在真实核电站数据集上，将流式方法与在线部署的最先进TSAD模型进行对比评测，并在流式场景下评估自动化异常检测。

Result: 在线TSAD表现出更高的一致性，集成（ensembling）策略展现出很强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 在无监督流式异常检测中，在线TSAD与集成策略是较为可靠的选择。

Abstract: EDF relies on continuous monitoring of its power plants to detect anomalies as soon as they occur. Given the absence of a universally optimal streaming method in unsupervised settings, we compare streaming methods with state-of-the-art TSAD models deployed online on a real nuclear power plant dataset. This work also evaluates Automated Anomaly Detection in a streaming context. Results show higher consistency for online TSAD and strong robustness from ensembling strategies.

</details>


### [133] [A differentiability framework for zigzag persistent homology via linear interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39242)
*Enrico Maria Ferrari,Clemens Bannwart,Matteo Biagetti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为锯齿持久性（zigzag persistence）开发了可微分框架，使其能嵌入学习流程，并在传感器网络覆盖优化与动态图分类任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久同调已可被微分并融入学习流程，但当底层拓扑结构随时间非单调演化时所需的锯齿持久性，尚缺乏类似的可微分框架。

Method: 针对固定复形上对时变滤波值取阈值所得单纯复形序列，将持久图端点赋予线性插值滤波值穿越阈值的实值时刻，从而把滤波值的连续性传递到图点上；由此得到持久图值映射的局部光滑提升，并在滤波值参数化的温和正则条件下几乎处处导出微分。进一步证明在显式的零测度排除集之外具有局部Lipschitz连续性。

Result: 证明了在明显零测度排除集外具有局部Lipschitz连续性，因此标准随机次梯度收敛保证不能直接适用；但认为该排除集在实践中足够小，仍可进行有效优化。在传感器网络覆盖优化和动态图分类两个实验中进行了实证验证。

Conclusion: 尽管缺乏标准收敛保证，该可微分锯齿持久性框架在实践中仍能支持有效优化，为处理非单调演化拓扑的学习任务提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Persistent homology can be differentiated and incorporated into learning pipelines, but no analogous framework exists for zigzag persistence, which is needed when the underlying topological structure evolves non-monotonically over time. We develop such a framework for sequences of simplicial complexes obtained by thresholding time-dependent filtering values on a fixed complex. By assigning persistence diagram endpoints the real-valued times at which linearly interpolated filtering values cross the threshold, we transfer the continuity of the filtering values to the diagram points. This yields smooth local lifts of the resulting persistence-diagram-valued map, from which we derive differentials almost everywhere under mild regularity conditions on the parametrization of the filtering values. We prove local Lipschitz continuity outside an explicit measure-zero exclusion set; standard stochastic subgradient convergence guarantees therefore do not apply directly. We argue that, even without such guarantees, this exclusion set is small enough in practice to allow effective optimization. We test this empirically in two experiments: sensor network coverage optimization and dynamic graph classification.

</details>


### [134] [Right Answer, Wrong Mechanism: Detecting Pernicious Divergence in Causal Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39243)
*Beiming Liu,Minjie Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究因果干预（激活修补、DAS）在神经网络机制解释中是否依赖"隐藏通路"：通过在预训练语言模型中植入静默通路，证明现有分布距离指标无效（AUROC 0.35-0.47），提出 HPC 测试在单元级越界条件下有效（AUROC≥0.99），并发现优化干预会主动搜寻隐藏通路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果干预常将表征推离模型的自然分布，这种偏离有时无害、有时有害（会招募模型在自然输入下从不使用的通路，导致"错误机制得出正确答案"）。然而现有方法无法区分这两种情况，作者需要让这个问题变得可检验。

Method: 在预训练语言模型内部植入"隐藏通路"，这些通路在所有基准提示上天然静默，因此哪些干预依赖它们可被精确已知。在 GPT-2 small 上进行 72 种配置、100,800 次干预。评估三类方法：(i) 干预位点的最近邻与局部 PCA 距离（既有做法）；(ii) 提出的 Hidden-Pathway Contribution (HPC)——一种无标签测试，将下游单元钳制到具有相同输出的自然运行区间，测量决策消失程度；(iii) 优化干预（如 DAS）及带流形内惩罚的下游方法。

Result: (i) 最近邻与局部 PCA 距离低于随机水平（AUROC 0.35-0.47）；(ii) HPC 在通路表现为单元级越界活动时 AUROC≥0.99，但当所有单元仍在自然范围内时失效（被识别为开放问题）；(iii) DAS 在性别任务上四个家族中三个有 90-95% 的成功经由植入通路，流形内惩罚可将其降至 5% 以下，代价是成功率下降 6-11 个百分点；未修改的 GPT-2 中成功干预几乎不依赖单元级越界。

Conclusion: 既有分布距离指标无法识别通路主导的干预；HPC 可在通路表现为单元级越界时可靠标记，但存在"全单元在自然范围内"的开放难题。优化干预会主动搜寻隐藏通路，说明机制解释中的因果主张可能被离分布效应误导，需警惕并可通过流形内惩罚等方式缓解（以成功率为代价）。

Abstract: Causal interventions such as activation patching and distributed alignment search (DAS) are the main tool for making mechanistic claims about neural networks. Recent work showed that these interventions routinely push representations off the model's natural distribution, and that such divergence is sometimes harmless and sometimes pernicious: it can recruit pathways the model never uses on natural inputs, so that an intervention produces the expected answer through the wrong mechanism. No method currently tells the two cases apart. We make this question testable by planting hidden pathways inside pretrained language models; the pathways are silent on every benchmark prompt by construction, so which interventions depend on them is known exactly. Across 72 configurations and 100,800 interventions on GPT-2 small, we find three things. (i) Nearest-neighbour and local-PCA distances at the intervention site, as used in prior work, score below chance (AUROC 0.35-0.47) at picking out interventions that give the right answer through a planted pathway. (ii) Hidden-Pathway Contribution (HPC), a label-free test that clamps downstream units to the regime of natural runs with the same output and measures how much of the decision disappears, flags pathway-dominated interventions with AUROC >= 0.99 when the pathway shows up as unit-level out-of-regime activity, but fails when every unit stays within its natural range, which we identify as the open problem. (iii) Optimised interventions actively seek hidden pathways: on a gender task, DAS routes 90-95% of its successes through planted pathways for three of four families, and a downstream on-manifold penalty cuts this share to under 5% at a cost of 6-11 points of success rate. In unmodified GPT-2, successful interventions show almost no unit-level out-of-regime reliance.

</details>


### [135] [Trust the Critic More](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39247)
*Kaiyue Wen,Luke Bailey,Arvind Mahankali,Tengyu Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AC2通过动作分块将信用分配给轨迹片段，用学习到的critic评估，无需将每条轨迹rollout到终点，在更少解码FLOPs下超越GRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准LLM RL算法将长rollout的每个token赋予相同的优势值，信用分配粗糙；actor-critic虽可提供更细粒度信用分配，但学习到的critic被认为不够准确，现有工作即便有critic也只用作baseline，导致每条轨迹都必须rollout到终点。

Method: 提出Actor-Critic with Action Chunking（AC2），将信用分配给动作分块（前缀的短延续），由critic对分块末端状态打分，使策略无需观察终点奖励即可更新。三个可靠性设计：1) local readiness，仅当critic在某问题上足够准确时才用基于critic的更新；2) 提供先前成功rollout的参考解给critic；3) 以10k token的动作分块而非单token分配信用。

Result: 在FineProofs-RL上训练Qwen3-4B并在IMO-ProofBench评估，AC2以2.5倍更少的解码FLOPs超过GRPO峰值验证分18.5%。增益来自：训练步数减少25%，且每步生成更少token。

Conclusion: 概念上证明可以去除将每条轨迹rollout到终点的需要，为LLM RL算法开辟了此前未探索的设计空间。

Abstract: Standard language model RL algorithms credit every token of a long rollout with the same advantage determined by the terminal reward. Actor-critic methods can provide finer-grained credit assignment, but learned critics are generally considered too inaccurate to trust when training LLMs with RL. In recent works, even when a critic is present, it is used only for baseline estimation, so every trajectory must be rolled out to its terminal reward. We introduce Actor-Critic with Action Chunking (AC2) that removes the need to roll every trajectory to completion. AC2 instead assigns credit to action chunks: short continuations of prefixes of past trajectories. A learned critic scores the state reached at the end of each action chunk, allowing the policy to update without observing a terminal reward. We make critic-based credit assignment reliable through three design choices. First, we introduce local readiness which uses critic-based updates on a problem only when the critic is sufficiently accurate on that particular problem. Second, when available, we provide the critic with a reference solution from a previous successful rollout. Third, we assign credit over action chunks of 10k tokens rather than individual tokens, giving the critic a more meaningful portion of the trajectory to evaluate. We train Qwen3-4B on FineProofs-RL using AC2 and evaluate on IMO-ProofBench. AC2 exceeds GRPO's peak validation score of 18.5% using 2.5x fewer decoding FLOPs. This gain comes from two sources, (1) AC2 requires 25% fewer training steps to reach this score, and (2) each step generates fewer tokens because the policy does not need to continue every trajectory to completion. Conceptually, we demonstrate that we can remove the need to roll out every trajectory to completion, opening up a large previously unexplored design space for LLM RL algorithms.

</details>


### [136] [Jacobian Rank Collapse in Decision-Focused Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39261)
*Aojie Yuan,Haiyue Zhang,Zijian Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过预测器雅可比矩阵刻画决策聚焦学习（DFL）中参数更新方向的受限性，并实证检验几何结构是否转化为决策质量收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DFL通过下游目标训练预测器，但不同的损失函数未必提供独立的参数更新方向，因此需要理解这种更新方向的受限本质及其对实际决策质量的影响。

Method: 利用稀疏索引追踪，从预测器雅可比矩阵区分优化器读取的协方差项与学习可用的参数方向；采用秩一雅可比、条件谱界、批子空间刻画及反例分析；并进行多组实验（38个单参数权益配置、385参数条件预测器、验证调优的最短路与背包问题、可比神经网络对照等）。

Result: 38个单参数配置中DFL相对MSE增益低于1.8%；385参数条件预测器呈逐点秩一；SPO+在最短路上平均遗憾降低11.6%、背包降低10.6%，但八次比较中仅背包通过校正检验；容量对比在新数据集、不同批序与训练预算下持续存在；坐标缩放改变谱有效秩并影响SGD，补偿缩放可恢复原轨迹；匹配神经网络对照未发现总体DFL优势。

Conclusion: 研究区分了局部秩限制、依赖坐标的优化与预测精度；预测器几何有助于解释可用的学习方向，而留出集上的决策质量仍是衡量实际收益的最终标准。

Abstract: Decision-focused learning (DFL) trains predictors through downstream objectives, but a different loss need not provide an independent parameter-update direction. We characterize this restriction through the predictor Jacobian, using sparse index tracking to distinguish the covariance entries read by the optimizer from the parameter directions available to learning. Rank-one Jacobians make nonzero per-example gradients collinear; a conditional spectral bound describes near-collinearity. A batch-subspace characterization and counterexamples show why these local statements imply neither common minimizers nor collinear batch updates.
  Experiments examine when geometry translates into decision quality. Across 38 one-parameter equity configurations, DFL gains over MSE remain below 1.8%; a 385-parameter conditional predictor also has pointwise rank one. In validation-tuned shortest-path and knapsack experiments, full-capacity SPO+ reduces mean regret by 11.6% and 10.6%, respectively; only knapsack survives correction across eight comparisons. The capacity contrast persists on fresh datasets across batch orders and training budgets. Holding expressivity fixed, invertible coordinate scaling lowers spectral effective rank and ordinary SGD gains; compensating for the scaling restores the original trajectories. Financial forward-target controls separate forecast accuracy from decision quality; a matched neural comparison finds no aggregate DFL advantage in the tested architecture. These findings distinguish local rank restrictions, coordinate-dependent optimization and predictive accuracy. Predictor geometry helps explain available learning directions, while held-out decision quality remains the test of practical benefit.

</details>


### [137] [A Time-Aware Bag-of-Receptive-Fields for Interpretable Irregular Time Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39268)
*Francesco Spinnato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将 BORF 变换扩展到不规则时间序列，通过时间加权归一化使模式提取对真实时间分布敏感，并保持线性时间复杂度，在分类性能上具有竞争力且可解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不规则时间序列在医疗、移动性和环境监测中普遍存在，但现有分类方法要么依赖插补而掩盖时间结构，要么使用复杂且难以解释的神经网络架构，缺乏有效且可解释的分类器。

Method: 扩展 Bag-Of-Receptive-Fields (BORF) 到不规则时间序列设置；提出时间加权归一化方案，使每个观测按其时间间隔比例加权，从而让模式提取对样本真实时间分布敏感；推导时间加权标准差的高效滑动窗口递推公式，以保持 BORF 的线性时间复杂度。

Result: 在 PYRREGULAR 仓库数据集上与当前最先进的不规则时间序列分类器进行基准测试，取得了有竞争力的分类性能，并额外提供人类可解释的解释。

Conclusion: 该方法为不规则时间序列提供了快速、确定性、可解释且性能有竞争力的分类方案，避免了插补带来的时间结构损失以及复杂神经架构的不透明性。

Abstract: Irregular time series, characterized by non-uniform sampling intervals, missing observations, and variable lengths, are ubiquitous in healthcare, mobility, and environmental monitoring, yet effective and interpretable classifiers for this setting are limited. Existing approaches often rely on imputation, which can obscure the temporal structure of the data, or require complex neural architectures that are opaque and difficult to explain. In this work, we extend the Bag-Of-Receptive-Fields (BORF), a fast, deterministic, and interpretable transform for time series, to the irregular setting. Our key contribution is a time-weighted normalization scheme in which each observation is weighted proportionally to its associated time delta, making pattern extraction sensitive to the actual temporal distribution of samples rather than only their index position. This requires deriving an efficient sliding-window recurrence for the time-weighted standard deviation, preserving the linear time complexity of BORF. We benchmark the resulting method against state-of-the-art irregular time series classifiers on datasets from the PYRREGULAR repository, demonstrating competitive classification performance with the added benefit of human-interpretable explanations.

</details>


### [138] [RW-Flow: One-Step Generation on Compact Manifolds via Wasserstein Gradient Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39271)
*Ualibyek Nurgulan,Seungwoo Yoo,Prin Phunyaphibarn,Minhyuk Sung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RW-Flow提出一个基于Wasserstein梯度流的理论框架，用于在紧致流形上学习单步生成模型，并给出可辨识性的充要条件，在多个流形数据基准上优于现有单步方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流形值数据（如地震等地理空间事件、编码三维结构的生物分子扭转角）广泛存在，但其诱发分布难以建模。扩散与流模型虽已扩展到紧致流形，采样却通常需要数十到数百次串行网络评估，效率低，因此需要单步生成方法。

Method: 提出RW-Flow，通过Wasserstein梯度流在紧致流形上学习单步生成模型。核心挑战是可辨识性：需保证速度场趋零时模型分布与目标分布一致。作者在紧致连通黎曼流形上建立可辨识性的充要条件，并证明：对于对称、Lipschitz连续的成本函数，由Sinkhorn散度诱导的速度场可辨识，当且仅当相关Gibbs核非退化。

Result: 该刻画为在紧致流形上设计可辨识成本提供了通用原则，并揭示平方测地距离（平方欧氏距离的自然流形类比）并不总能保证可辨识性。在地理空间事件、蛋白质侧链扭转角、RNA骨架扭转角以及离散为三角网格的一般流形等基准上，RW-Flow在公平比较条件下几乎在所有设置中优于现有单步方法。

Conclusion: RW-Flow为紧致流形上的单步生成建模提供了有理论支撑的框架，明确了可辨识性条件并给出成本设计原则，实证上在多种流形数据任务中超越现有单步方法。

Abstract: Manifold-valued data, and consequently the distributions they induce, are prevalent across many domains, ranging from the locations of geospatial events, such as earthquakes, to biomolecular torsion angles that encode information about three-dimensional structure. While diffusion and flow-based generative models have been successfully extended to compact manifolds, sampling typically requires tens or hundreds of sequential network evaluations. We introduce RW-Flow, a theoretically grounded framework for learning one-step generative models on compact manifolds via Wasserstein gradient flows. The main challenge is identifiability: driving the velocity field to zero should guarantee that the model distribution matches the target distribution. We establish a necessary and sufficient condition for identifiability on compact, connected Riemannian manifolds. We specifically show that, for a symmetric, Lipschitz-continuous cost function, the velocity field induced by the Sinkhorn divergence is identifiable if and only if the associated Gibbs kernel is nondegenerate. This characterization provides a general principle for designing identifiable costs on compact manifolds. It also reveals that the squared geodesic distance, the natural manifold analogue of the squared Euclidean distance, does not always guarantee identifiability. Across benchmarks involving geospatial events, protein side chain torsion angles, RNA backbone torsion angles, and general manifolds discretized as triangular meshes, RW-Flow outperforms existing one-step methods in nearly all settings under fair comparison conditions.

</details>


### [139] [ReTaCo: Residual-Target Control for On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39275)
*Zixiang Ni,Zhuo Hu,Renjie Cao,Weijie Ren,Binqin Shi,Weijia Zhang,Shuheng Cao,Zhicheng Shi,Zhenhao Zhang,Haomin Wen,Zhiyuan Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReTaCo 通过保留 top-k 词元并将剩余词表归并为残差符号，修正了 EOPD 因重归一化导致的教师非平稳点问题，在数学与代码基准上多数优于 EOPD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: on-policy 蒸馏需传输教师全词表分布，成本高昂；EOPD 仅用 top-k 概率并以正向前向监督配合反向 KL，但重归一化使被省略词元质量为零，导致学生 top-k 质量被持续推向 1，教师本身不构成平稳点。

Method: 提出 ReTaCo（Residual-Target Control）：逐个保留 top-k 词元，将其余词元合并为一个残差符号；配合单样本估计器，其期望等于全词表反向 KL。残差目标为 (1-β)(1-m)，β=0 保留教师质量，增大 β 在不改变相对概率的前提下把更多质量移到 top-k。

Result: 证明给定前缀下总体目标存在唯一最优，其 top-k 质量介于 m 与 m+β(1-m) 之间并随 β 单调递增；β=0 时被低估的 top-k 词元仍获得非消失的恢复梯度。数值优化验证了理论预测，在三组教师-学生对上于多数数学与代码基准优于 EOPD。

Conclusion: ReTaCo 以残差符号保留被省略词表的目标质量，避免 top-k 质量被推向 1，同时维持全词表反向 KL 的无偏估计，是更稳健的熵感知 on-policy 蒸馏目标。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student on its own generated prefixes with token-level teacher feedback, but transmitting or storing the teacher's full-vocabulary distribution at every token is costly. Entropy-aware OPD (EOPD) adds forward supervision to reverse KL to help the student recover plausible tokens it underestimates, using only the teacher's top-$k$ probabilities to limit cost. Because EOPD renormalizes these probabilities, its target assigns no mass to the omitted vocabulary. We prove that the resulting loss keeps pushing the student's top-$k$ mass toward one even after the student matches the teacher's relative probabilities within the top-$k$ set, so the teacher itself is not a stationary point whenever the omitted tokens have positive teacher probability. We propose ReTaCo (Residual-Target Control), which keeps the top-$k$ tokens individually and groups the remaining tokens into one residual symbol, and pairs this forward target with a single-sample estimator whose expectation equals the full-vocabulary reverse KL.
