We analyze PMD coupled with a persistent critic advanced by one temporal-difference (TD) update. For finite discounted MDPs, we establish global linear convergence in value for exact coordinate-wise Bellman updates, with any positive constant actor stepsize and arbitrary finite critic initialization. The proof combines a resolvent-based auxiliary distribution with a decaying Bellman-violation correction and a potential weighted by inverse coordinate weights. We then study stochastic TD-PMD with general strongly convex mirror maps under a single off-policy Markov trajectory. With suitably chosen constant stepsizes and a finite-batch TD update, the method achieves an expected value gap of $ε$ after $\widetilde{O}(1/((1-γ)^5 \widetildeσ_b ε))$ transitions. The stochastic analysis relies on the trajectory-wise Lipschitz continuity of the regularizer, derived from uniform bounds on vertex Bregman divergences, together with a visitation-weighted resolvent estimate for signed critic-error propagation that yields an inverse-linear dependence on behavior coverage $\widetildeσ_b$. In contrast to many prior guarantees for regularized policy optimization, our sample-complexity guarantee holds without trajectory resets, generative-model access, or nested policy-evaluation loops. Numerical results are consistent with the theoretical convergence analysis.

</details>


### [211] [Dynamic LoRA-Experts and Prototype-Ensemble Matching for Class-Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39839)
*Hongwei Zhao,Rui Liu,Yansong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出两阶段免回放框架DLEPEM，为每个增量任务分配专属LoRA-Expert以减少跨任务干扰，并融合预训练模型原型与任务自适应LoRA-Expert原型进行任务级判别，在标准与少样本类增量学习基准上表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类增量学习需持续学习新类且不遗忘旧知识。基于预训练模型的高效参数微调虽降低参数开销，但存在累积干扰、推理样本与专用模块对齐不佳的问题。

Method: 两阶段免回放框架DLEPEM：第一阶段为每个增量任务分配任务特定LoRA-Expert以降低跨任务干扰；第二阶段结合冻结预训练模型原型与任务自适应LoRA-Expert原型，实现可靠的任务级判别。

Result: 在标准类增量学习与少样本类增量学习基准上，在所评估协议下取得较强性能。

Conclusion: DLEPEM通过任务专属LoRA-Expert与原型集成匹配，有效缓解干扰与对齐问题，为免回放类增量学习提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Class-Incremental Learning (CIL) aims to continuously learn new classes without forgetting previously acquired knowledge. Parameter-efficient fine-tuning with pre-trained models reduces parameter overhead but can suffer from cumulative interference and suboptimal alignment between inference samples and specialized modules. We propose Dynamic LoRA-Experts and Prototype-Ensemble Matching (DLEPEM), a two-stage rehearsal-free framework. DLEPEM allocates a task-specific LoRA-Expert for each incremental task to reduce cross-task interference, then combines frozen pre-trained-model prototypes with task-adaptive LoRA-Expert prototypes for reliable task-level discrimination. Experiments on standard CIL and Few-Shot CIL benchmarks demonstrate strong performance under the evaluated protocols.

</details>


### [212] [Predicting Multi-View Rashomon Representation: Can We Learn Where Models Disagree?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39848)
*Mingyue Ma,Zongbo Han,Changqing Zhang,Guangyu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"Rashomon表示"概念，即不同基础模型对同一输入可能编码出不一致的表示；作者证明这种表示分歧是输入相关、可预测且可泛化的，并提出只需单模型表示即可预测分歧，用于高效估计模型可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型被广泛用作AI系统的核心组件，但不同模型对同一输入可能编码出多种不同视角的表示，导致显著的表示分歧（Rashomon Representation）。这种分歧往往指示某模型对该输入的编码与其他模型不一致，是估计输入可靠性的有价值信号。然而已有工作多聚焦于在"表示集合"上度量多模型间分歧，未探索如何从单一表示预测分歧。

Method: 作者假设分歧并非随机，而是遵循一致且依赖输入的模式。方法上：1）通过比较同一样本在不同模型表示空间中的最近邻来量化分歧；2）训练一个轻量级预测器，仅从单一模型的表示估计该分歧。推理时，给定新输入，预测器利用其表示判断该输入与其他模型表示一致还是分歧。

Result: 在多种基础模型和数据集上的大量实验表明，表示分歧确实是输入相关的、可预测的、可泛化的；所提方法能高效实现对基础模型的可靠性估计。

Conclusion: 表示分歧具有稳定可学的输入依赖规律，可由单模型表示预测，从而无需访问其他模型即可高效进行可靠性与输入一致性估计，为利用模型间分歧信号提供了新途径。

Abstract: Foundation models are increasingly adopted across a wide range of applications, often serving as core blocks within AI systems. Yet different foundation models may encode the same input from multiple different views, leading to substantial representation disagreement, which we term Rashomon Representation. Such disagreement often signals inputs that a given model encodes in a way inconsistent with other models, offering a valuable yet underexplored signal for input reliability estimation. While prior work has largely focused on measuring disagreement across multiple models with a representation set, we instead focus on predicting disagreement from a single representation. We hypothesize that this disagreement follows some consistent, input-dependent patterns rather than occurring at random. To test this, we quantify disagreement by comparing each sample's nearest neighbors across different models' representation spaces, then train a lightweight predictor that estimates disagreement from a single model's representation. At inference time, given a new input, the predictor uses that input's representation to tell whether it aligns with or diverges from those of other models. Extensive experiments across diverse foundation models and datasets show that representational disagreement is indeed input-dependent, predictable, and generalizable, enabling efficient reliability estimation of foundation models.

</details>


### [213] [Dimension-Free Rank Lifting from Random Hyperplane Arrangements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39855)
*Luca Becchetti,Matteo Russo,Ruben Skorupinski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究随机隐藏层实现秩提升所需宽度：对正齐次非多项式激活，证明 n≳(1/θ)max{m,log(1/δ)} 即可以至少 1−δ 概率使随机特征矩阵满行秩，并给出稳定秩提升的宽度界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机特征矩阵 σ(XR) 的秩提升是理解神经网络隐层表示能力的关键问题。已有针对 sign 特征的一般维度保证较松，作者希望给出维度无关且本质紧的宽度下界条件，并统一扩展到更广的激活函数与稳定秩提升。

Method: 针对正齐次非多项式激活（sign、Heaviside、ReLU、ReLU 幂等），使用邻近高斯方向耦合与诱导超平面排列的局部穿越，证明单个随机特征列以 Ω(θ) 概率逃逸 R^m 的任意真子空间。稳定秩部分结合总体核的对角占优 Taylor 尾部、截断与矩阵集中分析。

Result: 精确秩提升：n≳(1/θ)max{m,log(1/δ)} 个神经元足以使 σ(XR) 满行秩 m，概率≥1−δ，指数级改进此前 sign 特征的一般维度保证且本质紧。稳定秩提升：n≳C^q (m/θ^{2q+1}) log^{2q+1/2}(m/θ) log(m/δ)，其中 q 为激活次数，C 为通用常数。

Conclusion: 该分析统一并推广了 q-齐次非多项式激活的稳定秩保证，涵盖并延伸 Panigrahi et al. (2020) 与 Song (2026) 的工作，给出维度无关、本质紧的宽度刻画。

Abstract: We study the width required for a randomly initialized hidden layer of a neural network to achieve rank lifting. Namely, given a dataset $X \in \mathbb{R}^{m \times d}$ of $m$, $d$-dimensional input vectors separated by an angle of at least $θ$, we consider the random feature matrix $σ(XR)$, where $R$ is standard Gaussian. For positively homogeneous nonpolynomial activations, which include sign, Heaviside, ReLU, and ReLU powers among others, we prove that $$n \gtrsim \frac{1}θ\max\left\{m,\log\left(\frac{1}δ\right)\right\}$$ neurons suffice for $σ(XR)$ to have full row rank $m$ with probability at least $1-δ$. This dimension-free bound exponentially improves the previous general-dimensional guarantee for sign features (Drago et al., 2026) and is essentially tight. The proof shows that one random feature column escapes every proper subspace of $\mathbb{R}^m$ with probability $Ω(θ)$, using a coupling of nearby Gaussian directions and a local crossing of the induced hyperplane arrangement. We also study stable rank lifting, where the goal is to establish a quantitative analogue of exact rank lifting, i.e., a lower bound on the smallest eigenvalue of the empirical feature Gram matrix in high-probability.
  Our analysis unifies and generalizes stable rank guarantees for all $q$-homogeneous non-polynomial activations following prior work in Panigrahi et al. (2020) and Song (2026). In particular, we combine a diagonally dominant Taylor tail of the population kernel with truncation and matrix concentration, to show that for positively homogeneous nonpolynomial activations, stable rank lifting is achieved at width $$n \gtrsim C^q \frac{m}{θ^{2q+1}} \log^{2q+\frac{1}{2}}\left(\frac{m}θ\right) \log\left(\frac{m}δ\right),$$ where $q$ is the degree of the activation and $C > 0$ is some universal constant.

</details>


### [214] [Fork-dLLM: Avoiding the Flexibility Trap in Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39859)
*Stipe Frković,Metod Jazbec,Christian A. Naesseth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Fork-dLLM 与 ForkGRPO,仅在不确定的“回退步”使用自回归式排序、其余步骤保留并行生成,从而在保持 AR 采样 pass@k 扩展性的同时获得 2-3 倍效率提升,并以更低训练成本实现不逊于 AR-based GRPO 的后训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码扩散语言模型(dLLM)结合置信度采样器可通过并行生成加速推理,但其易将高熵分叉位置(存在多种合理续写处)推迟解掩码,导致生成多样性下降(表现为 pass@k 扩展变差),并限制 RL 后训练收益("灵活性陷阱")。此前工作主张改用自回归采样,但论文认为这既无必要也浪费推理成本。

Method: 1) Fork-dLLM:一种简单混合采样器,仅在不确定的回退步采用 AR 式排序,其余仍保持并行生成;2) ForkGRPO:将同一原则扩展到后训练,使用 Fork-dLLM 的 rollout,并仅在回退步应用 GRPO 目标,从而保持精确的策略似然比,同时显著降低 rollout 与优化成本。

Result: Fork-dLLM 达到了 AR 采样强劲的 pass@k 扩展性,同时效率提升 2-3 倍;ForkGRPO 以显著更低的训练成本,取得与 AR-based GRPO 基线相当或更好的下游性能。

Conclusion: 无需抛弃基于置信度的采样;只需在少数不确定步引入 AR 式排序,即可兼顾并行生成的效率与 AR 的多样性和 RL 收益,实现高效且高质量的 dLLM 推理与后训练。

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models (dLLMs) have shown strong potential for faster inference through parallel token generation when combined with confidence-based samplers. However, recent work has shown that such methods can defer unmasking high-entropy fork positions at which multiple plausible continuations exist. This results in reduced generation diversity, as shown by worse pass@k scaling, and limits gains obtainable from RL post-training. To avoid this flexibility trap, prior work advocated for autoregressive (AR) sampling. Here, we show that discarding confidence-based sampling is unnecessary and, once inference cost is taken into account, wasteful. We first propose Fork-dLLM, a simple hybrid sampler that uses AR-style ordering only at uncertain fallback steps while retaining parallel generation otherwise. We then extend the same principle to post-training with ForkGRPO, which uses Fork-dLLM rollouts and applies the GRPO objective only at fallback steps, preserving exact policy-likelihood ratios while substantially reducing rollout and optimization cost. In our experiments, Fork-dLLM matches the strong pass@k scaling of AR sampling while being 2-3x more efficient, and ForkGRPO achieves downstream performance comparable to or better than AR-based GRPO baselines at a substantially lower training cost.

</details>


### [215] [Preemptive LLM Unlearning against Forbidden Capability Acquisition via Gradient Sealing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39866)
*Kemou Li,Qizhou Wang,Yue Wang,Fengpeng Li,Zhuan Shi,Negar Rostamzadeh,Golnoosh Farnadi,Masashi Sugiyama,Jiantao Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"先发遗忘"（preemptive unlearning）问题，即模型发布前需防御用户通过下游微调注入非法能力，并提出"梯度封堵"（gradient-sealing）原则来阻断此类能力的获取路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放权重模型不仅是固定产品，还是下游微调的基座，这带来了法律与伦理风险：用户可能通过微调注入非法知识或实现有害操作。因此模型提供方需要一种发布前的防御手段。现有的事后遗忘（retrospective unlearning）针对固定模型中已存在的能力进行移除，无法应对未见攻击数据和未来微调流程下的能力获取，该场景尚未被充分探索且挑战独特。

Method: 作者首先验证现有事后遗忘方法提供的发布前保护不足：即使当前输出被抑制，禁区数据仍可通过内部路径产生梯度，从而在后续被习得。基于此发现，提出梯度封堵原则，通过将相关预激活推向负区间，使 ReLU 族激活的导数接近或等于零，从而阻断这些梯度路径。

Result: 在多个 LLM 家族上的实验表明，相比事后遗忘基线方法，所提方法对下游能力获取具有更强的抵抗性。

Conclusion: 梯度封堵被验证为发布前保护的有效机制，为先发遗忘这一新问题提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Open-weight LLMs are released not only as fixed products but also as substrates for downstream fine-tuning. This openness, however, creates legal and ethical risks because users may misuse fine-tuning to instill illicit knowledge or enable hostile operations. Model providers therefore need apre-release defense against such acquisition, motivating the problem of preemptive unlearning. Unlike retrospective unlearning, which removes capabilities already present in a fixed model, preemptive unlearning seeks to prevent their acquisition under unseen attack data and future fine-tuning procedures. Despite its practical importance, this setting remains largely unexplored, presents distinct challenges, and is therefore the central focus of our work. We first verify that existing retrospective methods provide insufficient pre-release protection. Even when forbidden capabilities are suppressed in current outputs, forbidden-domain data can still induce gradients through internal pathways, enabling later acquisition. Motivated by this finding, we propose a gradient-sealing principle that blocks these pathways by pushing relevant pre-activations into the negative region, where ReLU-family activations exhibit zero or near-zero derivatives. Experiments across multiple LLM families demonstrate our stronger resistance to downstream acquisition than retrospective baselines, validating gradient sealing as an effective mechanism for pre-release protection.

</details>


### [216] [Algorithmic Recourse Under Competition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39877)
*Shahin Jabbari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"竞争下的追索"(recourse under competition)框架，研究在有限资源竞争场景下，广泛实施追索会改变录取阈值从而使原有追索建议失效的问题，并给出基于隐函数定理的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统追索计算假设决策规则在追索实施阶段保持不变，但在个人竞争有限资源的场景中，大量人实施追索会抬高录取阈值，即使评分模型不变，也会导致原追索建议失效。

Method: 提出recourse under competition框架，联合优化推荐对象集合与所需达到的分数目标，从而在初始被拒个体间平衡追索成本与偏移后有效性；并开发了基于隐函数定理(Implicit Function Theorem)的算法，在合成与真实数据集上进行实证分析。

Result: 实验表明，个性化分数目标可获得更高的有效性，但成本也更高；而通用分数目标在低到中等有效性区间通常能提供更优的成本-有效性权衡。

Conclusion: 在竞争性资源分配下，追索建议需考虑阈值漂移问题；个性化与通用分数目标各有取舍，实践中应依据目标有效性水平选择合适的策略。

Abstract: Algorithmic recourse provides individuals who have received undesirable outcomes from machine learning models with suggestions for minimum-cost improvements to achieve the desired outcome. A central assumption when computing recourse is that the decision rule remains fixed throughout the recourse implementation phase. We challenge this assumption in settings where individuals compete for limited resources. In such settings, widespread recourse implementation can change the acceptance threshold even when the scoring model that is used to evaluate individuals remains the same. This change in acceptance threshold can, in turn, invalidate the original recourse recommendations (i.e., following the recourse may not lead to the desired outcome). To address this problem, we introduce a framework called recourse under competition that jointly optimizes for recommendation recipients and the recommended score target they need to satisfy to balance the recourse cost and post-shift validity among initially rejected individuals. We develop an algorithm based on the Implicit Function Theorem and empirically analyze its performance. Experiments on synthetic and real datasets show that personalized score targets can achieve higher validity, albeit at a higher cost. In contrast, common score targets generally offer favorable cost-validity trade-offs for lower to medium validity values.

</details>


### [217] [Markovian Dynamics Enforcer: Feasibility Preserving Correction on Learned Dynamics Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39888)
*Kevin Yu,Tao Guo,Constantinos Antoniou,Panagiotis Angeloudis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MaDE后处理算子，将神经轨迹预测的状态转移映射到可行动力学流形上，提升动力学一致性但增大位移误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经轨迹预测器虽误差低，却常违反动力学、执行器限制或状态约束，尤其在控制未观测、动力学仅部分已知时。

Method: MaDE为时间不变的事后算子，基于可行状态训练、无需真实控制：对每个状态转移推断控制量，经已知物理加学习残差的补全模型重算状态，再用基于梯度的不等式削减修正控制，每次修正迭代均重新进入补全模型，使返回状态相对该模型与给定前态锚点动力学一致。

Result: 在完全给定的仿真系统上动力学残差近乎为零；在欠定系统上真实动力学残差小于基线。在实车轨迹上，相对自行车模型的一步残差MaDE为0.0071-0.0072，原始预测器为0.1703-0.1714；平均位移误差增大1.57-1.83倍。

Conclusion: MaDE可即插即用于循环、结构化状态空间与Transformer预测器，显著提升动力学一致性，但在迭代预算下不等式满足仅为尽力而为，并以增大位移误差为代价。

Abstract: Neural trajectory predictors can reach low prediction error while violating dynamics, actuator limits, or state constraints, especially when controls are unobserved and dynamics are partially specified. We introduce the Markovian Dynamics Enforcer (MaDE), a time-invariant post-hoc operator mapping state-transition proposals onto a learned feasible dynamics manifold, trained on feasible states without ground-truth controls. For each transition it infers a control and recomputes the state through a completion model of known physics plus a learned residual. It then corrects that control by gradient-based inequality reduction, so inequality satisfaction is best-effort within an iteration budget. Since every correction iterate re-enters the completion model, the returned state is dynamically consistent by construction relative to that model and the supplied previous-state anchor. MaDE drives dynamics residuals to essentially zero on fully specified simulated systems, and on an underspecified system leaves a smaller true-dynamics residual than the baselines. Designed to attach to arbitrary predictors, the frozen operator is evaluated downstream of recurrent, structured state-space, and transformer predictors. On recorded vehicle trajectories the one-step residual against a kinematic bicycle model is 0.0071 to 0.0072 for MaDE and 0.1703 to 0.1714 for raw predictors. MaDE raises average displacement error by a factor of 1.57 to 1.83.

</details>


### [218] [Shared Weights, Selected Computations: How Looped Transformers Route What Each Loop Does](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39892)
*Jiaju Wu,Yi Hu,Muhan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 循环Transformer虽共享权重，但每个循环并非执行相同计算；隐状态通过线性层J选择由主干学得的计算，注意力路由是因果路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环Transformer在迭代推理和长度泛化上表现优异，常被解释为循环提供了复用同一算法的归纳偏置。但权重共享并不意味着每次循环都执行相同操作，因此需要探究：每个循环是否真的重复同一计算？若不是，又是什么将共享参数引导至不同操作？

Method: 以图游走为测试任务。先观察模型原生轨迹中解码预测的推进步数；再通过修改进入隐状态并引入学习到的线性层J来引导冻结循环产生不同转移；随后用激活修补检验注意力模式能否恢复并切换所选转移；最后对比五对匹配图模型中不同中间监督对J可诱导转移的影响。

Result: 模型原生轨迹的预测可推进多个图步或停留在已到达目标，说明循环进展并非固定的一循环一步。冻结循环可通过修改进入隐状态被引导至不同转移，线性层J能在不改变共享Transformer层的情况下选择目标转移。激活修补显示注意力模式可恢复该效果并切换所选转移。在五对匹配图模型中，改变主干训练时的中间监督会改变J可诱导的转移。

Conclusion: 循环Transformer的每个循环并不执行相同计算；隐状态可以控制共享计算，而J是在选择主干已学得的计算而非创造新算法。注意力路由是这一控制过程的因果路径。

Abstract: Looped Transformers repeatedly apply the same set of Transformer layers, giving them a recurrent architecture for latent computation. Their strong performance on iterative reasoning and length-generalization tasks suggests an appealing explanation: recurrence may provide an inductive bias that lets the model reuse a learned algorithm across loops. However, weight sharing alone does not imply that every loop performs the same operation. This raises a basic question: is each loop actually repeating the same computation, and if not, what routes the shared parameters to different operations?
  We study this question using graph walks as a test case. In the model's native trajectories, decoded predictions can advance by different numbers of graph steps or remain at a reached target, showing that recurrent progress need not follow a fixed one-loop-one-step pattern. We then show that a frozen loop can be steered toward different transitions by modifying its entering hidden state: a learned linear layer $J$ selects the desired transition without changing the shared Transformer layers.
  To test how this steering works, we use activation patching and find that attention patterns can recover its effects and switch the selected transition. Across five matched pairs of graph models, changing intermediate supervision during backbone training changes which transitions $J$ can induce. This suggests that $J$ selects computations learned by the backbone rather than creating new algorithms. Together, these results show that the hidden state can control shared computation, with attention routing as a causal pathway.

</details>


### [219] [Do Better Goal Representations Improve Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39901)
*Syed Nazmus Sakib,Abdul Monaf Chowdhury,Nafiul Haque,Shifat E Arman,Md Mehedi Hasan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在离线GCRL导航任务中，目标表示质量的巨大变化几乎不影响性能，而改善智能体当前状态的表示才是性能瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GCRL高度依赖目标如何被表示给策略，但学界尚不清楚下游性能在多大程度上真正取决于表示质量，因此需要系统探究目标表示质量与性能的因果关系。

Method: 在确定性迷宫环境中构造精确的时间距离目标表示，并在固定下游学习器的前提下系统性地破坏其几何质量；在OGBench导航任务和两种算法上进行对照实验，随后将相同干预施加于智能体当前状态表示。

Result: 目标表示质量的大幅变化几乎不引起性能变化；而将相同干预施加于当前状态表示可使成功率提升超过一倍，揭示状态通路才是真正瓶颈。基于此，简单的随机傅里叶位置编码在无地图信息、无目标修改的情况下显著提升最难导航任务的性能。

Conclusion: 在基于状态的离线导航中，改进智能体当前状态的表示远比优化目标表示更为重要。

Abstract: Goal-conditioned reinforcement learning (GCRL) relies heavily on how target goals are represented to the policy. While recent methods encode goals via temporal distance, occupancy, or controllability, it remains unclear how much downstream performance actually depends on representation quality. We study this in offline GCRL by constructing an exact temporal-distance goal representation in deterministic mazes. We then systematically corrupt its geometric quality while keeping the downstream learner fixed. Across OGBench navigation tasks and two algorithms, large changes in goal-representation quality produce almost no change in performance. However, applying the same interventions to the agent's current state more than doubles success, revealing the state pathway as the true bottleneck. Building on this insight, we show that simple random Fourier positional encodings substantially improve performance on the hardest navigation tasks without map information or objective modifications. Overall, our findings suggest that in state-based offline navigation, improving how the agent's current state is represented matters far more than refining the goal representation. Code will be released soon.

</details>


### [220] [Patient-Centered Treatment Planning for Chronic Multimorbidity: A Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning Framework for Preference Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39911)
*Nafiseh Payani,Soham Das,G. Anthony Wilson,Anahita Khojandi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出以患者为中心的分层强化学习框架FAHOC，将患者偏好显式约束纳入治疗规划，在共病管理决策中实现偏好一致且临床安全的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 患者偏好是疗效的主要决定因素，但在现有计算治疗规划模型中结构性缺失，需要一种能联合学习治疗策略并强制执行患者偏好的框架。

Method: 提出patient-centered factored-action hierarchical option-critic（FAHOC），联合学习对应治疗策略的高层option与分解为疾病和干预子成分的option内策略，引入合作感知动作掩码机制，实现结构化探索、跨层级信用分配和可解释决策路径，并给出合作患者更优健康结局及分解Q函数近似误差有界的理论保证。

Result: 基于美国东南部五家医院约5万名高血压合并2型糖尿病患者的纵向数据评估，FAHOC质量调整生命年期望提升0.669（临床实践为-0.133），合作患者识别准确率95.9%，在留出测试中从未违反患者偏好。

Conclusion: 带显式偏好约束的分层强化学习可支持共病管理中偏好一致、临床安全的决策制定。

Abstract: Patient preference, defined as a patient's demonstrated willingness and capacity to adhere to clinical recommendations, is a primary determinant of therapeutic effect yet remains structurally absent from existing computational treatment planning models. We address this gap by presenting patient-centered factored-action hierarchical option-critic (FAHOC), a hierarchical reinforcement learning (HRL) framework that jointly learns high-level options corresponding to therapeutic strategies and factored intra-option policies that decompose the joint action space into disease- and intervention-specific subcomponents, while imposing a cooperation-aware action masking mechanism. This enables structured exploration, improved credit assignment across hierarchy levels, and more interpretable decision pathways, while enforcing patients' preferences. Formal guarantees establish that cooperative patients achieve higher optimal expected health outcomes than non-cooperative patients, and that the factored Q-function approximation error is provably bounded. The framework is evaluated using longitudinal data collected from approximately 50,000 comorbid hypertension and type 2 diabetes mellitus patients from five hospitals in the Southeast U.S. FAHOC achieves a quality-adjusted life year expectancy equivalent improvement of 0.669 (vs -0.133 observed clinician practice), correctly identifies cooperative patients in 95.9% of cases and never violates a patient's preference in held-out test, demonstrating that HRL with explicit preference constraints can support preference-consistent, clinically safe decision-making in multimorbidity management.

</details>


### [221] [TRACE: Trajectory Selection for Parallel Scaling of Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39912)
*Qisheng Zhou,Zhen Xiong,Qiaoyu Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TRACE 是一种轻量级学习式轨迹选择器，利用跨 rollout 的搜索证据对并行搜索结果进行聚合，在多个 WebQA 与长程任务上优于投票和生成式聚合方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并行搜索可能已生成正确答案，但最终答案投票无法将其选出。作者将这一聚合阶段重新定义为轨迹选择问题，动机是避免依赖重量级生成式聚合，转而复用跨 rollout 的搜索证据。

Method: 提出 TRACE（Trajectory Ranking with Aggregated Cross-Rollout Evidence）：保留每条查询与证据的出现记录，通过共享内容或文档身份连接各 rollout，并在这些关系上传播信息；每个候选答案读取其自身轨迹更新后的状态，兼顾检索来源与相关 rollout 的证据。基于冻结文本嵌入、以答案级监督训练，直接返回已有答案，无需额外搜索或自回归聚合；每个搜索设置一个选择器，可跨策略与骨干迁移。

Result: 在六个 WebQA 策略与六个长程数据集-骨干组合上（K=16）均优于投票。Qwen2.5-14B Base/SFT WebQA 池上达 45.2/49.2% EM，超过最强 Qwen3-32B 生成式聚合器的 43.9/48.0%。FRAMES、GAIA、BrowseComp 上平均准确率 78.6%，高出多数投票 3.1 个百分点。Base WebQA 池上仅用 8 次 rollout 即接近 64 次投票（差 0.4 点）。七个 WebQA 基准上吞吐量至少比 SolAgg、SummAgg、AggAgent 高 10 倍。

Conclusion: 复用跨 rollout 搜索证据是替代重量级生成式聚合的有效且高效方案，可在无需代理特定微调的情况下提升并行搜索的最终答案选择效果与效率。

Abstract: Parallel search may generate a correct answer that final-answer voting fails to select. We formulate this consolidation stage as trajectory selection and introduce TRACE (Trajectory Ranking with Aggregated Cross-Rollout Evidence), a lightweight learned selector that ranks completed trajectories using the search evidence behind their answers. TRACE preserves individual query and evidence occurrences, connects rollouts through shared content or document identity, and propagates information across these relations. Each candidate answer then reads the updated states of its own trajectory, preserving retrieval provenance while incorporating evidence from related rollouts. Trained with answer-level supervision over frozen text embeddings, TRACE returns an existing answer without additional search or autoregressive aggregation. One selector per search setting transfers across rollout policies and agent backbones without agent-specific fine-tuning, improving over voting across six WebQA policies and six long-horizon dataset-backbone combinations at $K=16$. On Qwen2.5-14B Base/SFT WebQA pools, TRACE achieves 45.2/49.2% EM, compared with 43.9/48.0% for the strongest Qwen3-32B generative aggregators. On long-horizon FRAMES, GAIA, and BrowseComp, it reaches 78.6% average accuracy, exceeding majority voting by 3.1 percentage points. On Base WebQA pools, TRACE with only 8 rollouts comes within 0.4 points of majority voting over 64. TRACE also achieves at least $10\times$ higher processing throughput than SolAgg, SummAgg, and AggAgent across all seven WebQA benchmarks. These results show that reusing cross-rollout search evidence provides an effective and efficient alternative to heavyweight generative aggregation for parallel search. Code is available at https://github.com/Jaasssoooonnnnn/TRACE.

</details>


### [222] [RoPE at the End of Its Rope? Theory, Diagnosis, and Mitigation of Long-Context Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39929)
*Yuyang Wu,Yufeng Du,Hao Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过允许RoPE各频率上query-key尺度不等，提出更精确的理论刻画，量化语义反转与位置不敏感两大弱点，并基于此设计轻量诊断工具RoPE Profiler，揭示推理任务主要受语义反转影响、检索任务主要受位置不敏感影响，并通过高频重缩放无需训练即提升准确率最高达20-25个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于RoPE的语言模型在长上下文场景下存在失效问题，根源在于RoPE在维持稳定token偏好与区分邻近位置之间的内在权衡。要确定应解决哪种弱点以及如何解决，需要更精确地刻画训练后模型在不同上下文长度下RoPE的行为。此前理论存在局限，未能很好对齐实际经验观察。

Method: 作者放宽了以往理论假设，允许RoPE不同频率上query-key尺度不相等，从而更贴合实际观察。该理论使两类脆弱性（语义反转和位置不敏感）对单个注意力头和输入均可度量，并量化高频分量如何支持位置敏感性却可能破坏语义稳定性。作者还推导出理论上的上下文长度边界，超过该边界后，在特定条件下固定的注意力分数比较无法同时避免语义反转和位置不敏感。基于此，作者提出RoPE Profiler，一种轻量、即插即用的诊断工具，通过复用缓存的query和key激活，在不增加额外前向传播的情况下补充两个诊断分数。

Result: 在49个长上下文任务设置上的评估揭示出明显模式：推理任务主要受语义反转困扰，而检索任务主要受位置不敏感影响。依据理论与诊断画像，有针对性的高频重缩放无需额外训练即可立即带来收益，在Qwen3-8B上任务准确率提升最高达20个百分点，在Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct上提升最高达25个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过更精确的RoPE理论刻画与轻量诊断工具RoPE Profiler，本文能够为单个头和输入度量语义与位置弱点，帮助用户优先确定需解决的问题；理论与诊断指导的高频重缩放方法无需训练即可显著提升长上下文任务表现。

Abstract: Long-context failures of RoPE-based language models can arise from RoPE's intrinsic tradeoff between maintaining stable token preferences and distinguishing nearby positions. Determining which weakness to address, and how, requires a more precise characterization of RoPE's behavior in trained models across context lengths. We address a key limitation of prior theory by allowing unequal query-key scales across RoPE frequencies, which aligns well with practical empirical observations. Our theory makes both vulnerabilities measurable for individual heads and inputs, and quantifies how high-frequency components support positional sensitivity while potentially disrupting semantic stability. We also derive a theoretical context-length bound beyond which, under specified conditions, a fixed attention-score comparison cannot jointly avoid semantic reversal and positional insensitivity. Guided by our fresh theoretical insights, we introduce RoPE Profiler, a lightweight, plug-and-play diagnostic toolkit that augments existing evaluations with zero additional forward passes by reusing cached query and key activations. Reusing activations collected during evaluation, the toolkit incurs little overhead. It supplements standard benchmark scores with two diagnostic scores that reveal semantic and positional weaknesses and help users prioritize which aspect to address. Crucially, our evaluations across 49 long-context task settings reveal a distinct pattern where reasoning tasks predominantly suffer from semantic reversal, whereas retrieval tasks are primarily vulnerable to positional insensitivity. Guided by our theory and diagnostic profiles, targeted high-frequency rescaling achieves immediate gains without additional training, improving task accuracy by up to 20 percentage points on Qwen3-8B and 25 percentage points on Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct.

</details>


### [223] [Reliability-Aware Checkpoint Selection for Domain Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39934)
*Jinshi Liu,Jiahao Li,Pan Liu,Yanfeng Li,Rui Qian,Zhao Tong,Yue Sun,Tao Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出精度约束的可靠性选择（AC）：在固定训练轨迹内，保留源验证精度接近最优的检查点，并按源域可靠性（NLL与CwECE经D∞聚合）排序，从而在不使用目标域数据的情况下改善目标域概率质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 域泛化中的检查点选择通常依赖源验证精度，但在未见目标域上该检查点未必提供可靠概率。源-目标分布偏移会改变精度排序，且精度本身无法衡量预测概率质量，因此需要一种同时顾及精度与可靠性的选择方式。

Method: 提出精度约束的可靠性选择（AC）：在固定训练轨迹中，保留源验证精度在最优值容差范围内的检查点，再以源域可靠性排序。参考规则将集合内归一化的负对数似然（NLL）与类级校准误差（CwECE）用D∞聚合。该方法不使用目标域数据，也无需额外训练或权重平均。在PACS上开发目标并采用0.5个百分点容差，在三个基准上评估五种域泛化训练算法。

Result: 在360次OfficeHome与TerraIncognita运行的探索性聚合比较中，相对Source-Acc，参考规则使目标域soft-bin平方间隔ECE与CwECE分别降低0.240%和0.182%，NLL降低0.030，平均目标域精度变化为+0.213个百分点。联合排序相对单目标排序的额外收益仍未确定。

Conclusion: 结果表明在固定训练轨迹内进行可靠性感知的检查点重选具有潜力，可在目标域精度基本不变的情况下改善概率质量；但联合多目标排序是否优于单目标排序仍有待进一步研究。

Abstract: Checkpoint selection in domain generalization often relies on source-validation accuracy, yet the selected checkpoint need not provide reliable probabilities on unseen target domains. Source-target distribution shifts can alter accuracy rankings, while accuracy alone does not measure predictive probability quality. We identify an empirical selection opportunity within fixed training trajectories: reselecting among checkpoints with near-optimal source accuracy can improve mean target probability quality with small observed changes in mean target accuracy. We study accuracy-constrained reliability selection (AC), which retains checkpoints within a tolerance of the best source-validation accuracy and ranks them by source reliability. Our reference rule aggregates within-set normalized negative log-likelihood (NLL) and class-wise calibration error (CwECE) using $D_\infty$. AC uses no target data and requires neither additional training nor weight averaging. We evaluate five domain generalization training algorithms on three benchmarks, using PACS to develop the objectives and a 0.5-percentage-point tolerance. In exploratory aggregation comparisons on 360 OfficeHome and TerraIncognita runs, the reference rule reduces mean target soft-bin squared-gap ECE and CwECE by 0.240% and 0.182%, respectively, and NLL by 0.030 relative to Source-Acc. Mean target accuracy changes by +0.213 percentage points. These results identify opportunities for reliability-aware reselection, while the additional benefit of joint over single-objective ranking remains unresolved.

</details>


### [224] [What Limits Recursive Reasoning Models: Optimization, Architecture and Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39967)
*Yuliana Shakhvalieva,Dmitrii Kharchev,Viacheslav Bezrukov,Inessa Fedorova,Dmitry Bocharov,Ivan Oseledets,Valerii Ternovskii*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 递归推理模型用少量共享参数反复精炼隐状态，获得大有效深度。本文在统一流程下研究其优化机制，发现一个简单配方（中间梯度视野、大物理批、受控的循环状态更新）即可实现稳定且泛化的递归推理，且无需显式层级架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HRM、TRM、URM 等递归推理模型在架构、梯度传播和训练流程上同时存在差异，难以判断性能来源，且优化不稳定、理解不足；同时这类紧凑求解器有潜力作为 LLM 调用窄算法子问题的工具。

Method: 在覆盖六个算法领域的统一实验流程下，于代表性领域做单个可控消融，再将所得配方在全套任务上评估；据此训练一个 13.6M 参数的稳定模型。

Result: 在评估的递归基线中取得最强整体性能，OOD 泛化提升尤为显著：算术 OOD 准确率从最强基线的 36.2% 提升到 71.2%，Sudoku 达 98.41%，ARC-AGI-1 pass@2 达 59.5%。

Conclusion: 在所研究的递归架构范围内，性能强烈取决于循环如何被优化与稳定，而非显式层级结构；更广泛地说，逐个优化组件可改进 AI 系统。

Abstract: Recursive reasoning models apply a small shared Transformer block many times to refine a latent state. This gives them large effective depth with few parameters and makes them strong on algorithmic tasks. Such compact solvers are natural candidates for tools that an LLM can call on narrow algorithmic subproblems. However, existing models such as HRM, TRM and URM differ in architecture, gradient propagation and training procedure simultaneously. This makes it hard to tell what drives their performance, and their optimization is still poorly understood and often unstable. In this work we address both of these gaps. First, we study these questions under a unified experimental pipeline spanning six algorithmic domains. Individual controlled ablations are performed on representative domains, while the resulting recipe is evaluated across the full suite. The study reveals a surprisingly simple recipe for stable and generalizable recursive reasoning: an intermediate gradient horizon, large physical batches and controlled updates of the recurrent state. An explicit hierarchical architecture is not needed. Second, we combine these findings into a stable 13.6M-parameter model that achieves the strongest overall performance among the evaluated recursive baselines, with particularly large gains on out-of-distribution generalization. It raises Arithmetic OOD accuracy to 71.2%, from 36.2% for the strongest baseline, while reaching 98.41% on Sudoku and 59.5% pass@2 on ARC-AGI-1. Our results show that, within the recursive architectures studied here, performance depends strongly on how recurrence is optimized and stabilized. More broadly, it shows how AI systems can be improved by optimizing their components one at a time.

</details>


### [225] [Learning to Explain While Planning: Rule-Aligned Diffusion Planning for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39995)
*Jiaxi Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出规则对齐扩散规划器（RADP），将可微驾驶规则融入扩散目标，并设计规则压力归因（RPA）实现规则级可解释性，在nuPlan上提升安全关键场景表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散规划器主要依赖专家示范拟合轨迹分布，仅学习场景、行为与轨迹间的统计相关性，未显式建模驾驶规则。在长尾场景中专家数据稀缺，缺乏可模仿行为易导致违反安全或合规要求的轨迹；同时生成过程缺乏规则级解释，难以判断哪些规则驱动轨迹调整、何时生效、作用多强，限制了失败诊断、安全验证与针对性改进。

Method: 1）提出RADP，在训练时把可微驾驶规则纳入扩散目标，将规则知识转化为超越有限示范的内在行为准则；2）提出规则压力归因（RPA），利用规则损失对预测轨迹的梯度构造监督信号，并通过轻量级归因头在线估计各规则施加的优化压力；3）提出时间风险对齐协议，评估当前规则压力是否反映后续闭环执行中的对应风险。

Result: 在nuPlan上实验表明，RADP在具有挑战性的安全关键场景中改善了闭环规划性能；RPA与后续规则特定风险表现出一致的时间对齐性。

Conclusion: 结果验证了内在规则学习与规则级可解释性的有效性，表明将可微规则融入扩散目标、并对规则压力进行归因，可同时提升闭环安全表现与解释能力。

Abstract: Diffusion planners exhibit strong capabilities in generating multimodal trajectories. However, existing methods primarily rely on expert demonstrations to fit trajectory distributions, learning statistical correlations among scenes, behaviors, and trajectories without explicitly modeling driving rules. In long-tail scenarios where expert data are scarce, the lack of behaviors to imitate may lead to trajectories that violate safety or compliance requirements. Moreover, their generation process lacks rule-level explanations, making it difficult to determine which rules drive trajectory adjustments, when they take effect, and how strongly they act, thereby limiting failure diagnosis, safety validation, and targeted improvement. To address these limitations, we propose the Rule-Aligned Diffusion Planner (RADP), which incorporates differentiable driving rules into the diffusion objective during training, turning rule knowledge into intrinsic behavioral principles beyond finite demonstrations. We further introduce Rule-Pressure Attribution (RPA), which constructs supervision signals from gradients of rule losses with respect to predicted trajectories and employs a lightweight attribution head to estimate the optimization pressure exerted by each rule online. To assess the closed-loop behavioral relevance of these attributions, we propose a temporal risk-alignment protocol that evaluates whether current rule pressures reflect corresponding risks during subsequent closed-loop execution. Experiments on nuPlan show that RADP improves closed-loop planning in challenging safety-critical scenarios, while RPA exhibits consistent temporal alignment with subsequent rule-specific risks, validating both intrinsic rule learning and rule-level interpretability.

</details>


### [226] [DashVMC: Real-Time Discrete World Model Control in Geometry Dash](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40003)
*Florent Tariolle,Florian Yger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DashVMC：从约两小时《几何冲刺》录像中学习紧凑的动作条件世界模型；控制器经行为克隆初始化后，完全在冻结模型的模拟推演中用PPO微调，无需再与真实游戏交互。三个种子下的微调策略在三个官方关卡和一个留出社区布局上存活时间均超过BC初始策略；部署时跳过视觉生成，在消费级GPU上维持60Hz的采集-动作循环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型智能体多在可为策略等待的模拟器中评估，而实时游戏约束相反：必须在下一帧到来前完成采集、预测与动作。本文旨在检验所学动力学在真实时间约束下是否具备可操作性。

Method: 从约两小时《几何冲刺》录制数据学习紧凑的、以动作为条件的世界模型；控制器先用行为克隆初始化，再完全在冻结模型的rollout中用PPO精调，不与实时游戏进一步交互；部署时省略视觉生成以降低延迟。

Result: 在三个控制器随机种子下，精调策略在三个官方关卡及一个留出社区关卡上的存活时长均优于BC初始化；消费级GPU上实现60Hz采集到动作闭环；动作条件延续与rollout诊断显示尽管长期保真度不完美，模型对控制仍有用。

Conclusion: 学习到的动作条件世界模型足以支撑实时约束下的策略改进与部署，即使长时程预测保真度有限；为实时游戏中的世界模型控制提供了一条不依赖在线交互的可行路线。

Abstract: World-model agents are usually evaluated in simulators that can wait for the policy; live games impose the opposite constraint, requiring capture, prediction, and action before the next frame. We present DashVMC, which learns a compact, action-conditioned world model from approximately two hours of recorded Geometry Dash gameplay. To test whether the learned dynamics are actionable, a controller is initialized by behavioural cloning (BC) and refined with Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) entirely in frozen-model rollouts, without further interaction with the live game. Across three controller seeds, the refined policies survive longer than their BC initializations on all three official levels and a held-out community layout. At deployment, the baseline skips visual generation and sustains a 60-Hz capture-to-action loop on a consumer GPU. Action-conditioned continuations and rollout diagnostics show that the model remains useful for control despite imperfect long-horizon fidelity.

</details>


### [227] [Fenchel Tilting: Weighted Correction for Efficient Finetuning of Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40030)
*Maksim Bobrin,Maksim Zhdanov,Dmitry Dylov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Fenchel Tilt Flow Control (FTFC)，将效用优化与生成模型拟合解耦，可高效适配任意效用函数偏好，兼具通用性与效率，效率最高提升 20 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有微调方法在通用性与计算成本之间存在权衡：要么限制支持的偏好类别以保证优化简单，要么保持通用性但牺牲效率。需要一种既能支持一般效用函数、又能高效适配预训练生成模型的方法。

Method: FTFC 先通过在预训练样本上联合拟合有效奖励与密度比权重来优化目标分布，将效用的变分结构与 Fenchel 对偶结合，支持一般 f-散度惩罚以决定奖励如何转换为分布修正权重。随后冻结这些权重，在单阶段重要性加权去噪或流匹配中修改扩散/流模型，无需对采样轨迹求导。

Result: 在合适条件下为凹效用建立了精确对偶性，并证明加权拟合能复现给定效用的最优目标分布；在图像与分子生成基准上，FTFC 在多种偏好函数上优于基线，且效率最高提升 20 倍。

Conclusion: FTFC 无需复杂优化即可实现超越期望奖励最大化的适配，同时在更一般的效用函数类别上保持稳健性，兼顾通用性与效率。

Abstract: Adapting a pretrained generative model to an arbitrary preference expressed as a utility function underlies reward alignment, guided design, and constraint satisfaction, enabling diverse applications. Existing fine-tuning methods trade off generality against computational cost: they either restrict the family class of supported preferences to keep optimization simple or preserve generality at the expense of efficiency. We introduce Fenchel Tilt Flow Control (FTFC), which decouples utility optimization from generative-model fitting. FTFC first optimizes for a target distribution by jointly fitting an effective reward and density-ratio weights on pretrained samples. Method combines the utility's variational structure with Fenchel duality, supporting general $f$-divergence penalties that determine how rewards are transformed into an distribution-correction weights. These weights are then frozen and used to modify a diffusion or flow model in a single stage of importance-weighted denoising or flow matching, without differentiating through sampling trajectories. We establish exact duality for concave utilities under suitable conditions and show that weighted fitting reproduces the optimal target distribution for a given utility. Across image and molecule generation benchmarks, FTFC improves over baselines on diverse preference functions, while also being up to $20\times$ more efficient. roposed method enables adaptation beyond expected-reward maximization without complex optimization, while preserving robustness for more general class of the utility functions compared to baselines.

</details>


### [228] [Efficient Active Auditing of Multi-Group Fairness with Bias Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40034)
*Ayoub Ajarra,Debabrota Basu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"bias probe"框架与主动审计器ALeBi，通过针对性、自适应查询揭示黑盒模型的多群体公平性偏差结构，在保护模型机密性的同时实现可靠的公平性审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去十年机器学习在最小化预测误差（ERM）的同时需控制公平性偏差，但公平性训练相对于标准ERM改进有限，因此事后审计至关重要。现有黑盒审计方法要么依赖模型重构（易受提取攻击、泄露模型机密），要么直接估计公平性指标但无法揭示数据分布中驱动偏差的区域；更根本的是，仅提取目标公平性信息而不重构模型的"属性特定审计"尚缺乏理论理解。

Method: 提出bias probe框架，支持针对性、自适应的查询以揭示偏差结构并保持模型机密性；基于该框架提出主动审计器ALeBi，学习此类探针以高效估计多群体公平性指标。建立了由属性特定复杂度度量控制的样本复杂度保证，并将分析扩展到模型所有者可能策略性隐藏偏差的对抗场景。

Result: 建立了新的样本复杂度保证，解决了此前提出的开放问题；揭示了模型机密性与可靠审计之间的基本权衡；表明属性特定探测既能实现准确估计，又能可解释地识别高偏差与低偏差区域。大量实验支持了理论发现并验证了方法的实际有效性。

Conclusion: 属性特定探测（property-specific probing）可在不重构模型的前提下实现准确且可解释的公平性审计，模型机密性与审计可靠性之间存在根本性权衡，ALeBi有效兼顾二者。

Abstract: Over the past decade, Machine Learning (ML) has been trained under dual objectives: minimizing prediction error via Empirical Risk Minimization (ERM) while controlling unfairness bias. In practice, however, fairness-aware training often yields limited improvements over standard ERM, making reliable post hoc auditing essential. Existing auditing approaches for black-box models either rely on model reconstruction --exposing systems to extraction attacks-- or directly estimate fairness metrics, offering limited insight into which regions of the data distribution drive bias. More fundamentally, property-specific auditing --aimed at extracting only targeted fairness information without reconstructing the model-- remains poorly understood. In this work, we introduce the bias probe framework, which enables targeted and adaptive querying to reveal bias structure while preserving model confidentiality. Building on this framework, we propose ALeBi, an active auditor that learns such probes to efficiently estimate multi-group fairness metrics. We establish novel sample complexity guarantees governed by a property-specific complexity measure, resolving a previously posed open question, and extend our analysis to adversarial settings where the model owner may strategically obscure bias. Our results uncover a fundamental trade-off between model confidentiality and reliable auditing, and show that property-specific probing enables both accurate estimation and interpretable identification of high and low-bias regions. Extensive experiments support our theoretical findings and demonstrate the practical effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


### [229] [Gromov-Wasserstein Distillation for Inductive Multi-View Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40047)
*Rafael Pereira Eufrazio,Eduardo Fernandes Montesuma,Charles Casimiro Cavalcante*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出基于重心蒸馏的归纳式框架，将传导式Gromov-Wasserstein多维标度（GW-MDS）转化为可对未见样本直接映射的神经模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GW-MDS能从关系数据学习低维表示，但本质是传导式的，对未见样本缺乏显式映射；直接神经GW训练在样本索引关系保持上表现不佳。

Method: 以GW-MDS为教师，学习潜在支撑集与最优传输方案，通过重心投影将耦合转化为样本对齐目标；再用神经学生网络学习显式样本外映射。方法从单视图扩展到Mean-GWMDS与Multi-GWMDS（基于共识与选择投影目标，多视图学生配合视图专属编码器），并研究仅用GW目标的直接神经基线。

Result: 在合成与真实数据上，使用欧氏、测地线与余弦关系进行实验，蒸馏模型能在未见样本上保持教师几何，且在样本索引关系保持上持续优于直接神经GW训练。

Conclusion: 重心投影是连接传导式GW嵌入与归纳式神经映射的有效桥梁。

Abstract: Gromov-Wasserstein multidimensional scaling (GW-MDS) learns low-dimensional representations from relational data but remains transductive, providing no explicit mapping for unseen samples. We introduce an inductive framework based on barycentric distillation. A GW-MDS teacher learns a latent support and an optimal transport plan from the training data, and barycentric projection converts the resulting coupling into sample-aligned targets. A neural student then learns an explicit out-of-sample mapping, avoiding additional relational-matrix construction and GW optimization at inference. We formulate the approach for single-view data and extend it to Mean-GWMDS and Multi-GWMDS teachers through consensus and selected-projection targets learned by a multi-view student with view-specific encoders. We also investigate a direct neural baseline trained solely with a GW objective. Experiments on synthetic and real-world data using Euclidean, geodesic, and cosine relations show that the distilled models preserve the teacher geometry on unseen samples and consistently outperform direct neural GW training in sample-indexed relational preservation. These results establish barycentric projection as an effective bridge between transductive GW embeddings and inductive neural mappings.

</details>


### [230] [LARC: Low-Rank Adaptive Residual Connections for Learning in Frozen Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40063)
*Junyi Zou,Avrova Donz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究LARC低秩自适应残差连接在冻结模型上的数值策略载体实现，验证因子空间动态与学习寿命。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为冻结模型赋予可从反馈中学习的紧凑数值状态，区分残差容量、相对起点的适应性与后续决策的有用性。

Method: 在冻结的MiniCPM5-1B-SFT上使用rank-4输入残差（12,288可训练参数）；慢状态ρ学习任务起始因子，私有快状态Φ随反馈变化并重置到训练初始化；在四候选程序选择任务与公共CI作业回放中测试。

Result: 两次反馈梯度步使期望查询执行误差相对重置到静态与后适应初始化分别降低24.65与36.65个百分点；直接支撑损失选择规则误差0.78125%；在仓库平衡的CI回放中，保留在线更新使half-Brier损失从0.1274升至0.1808。

Conclusion: 代数分析与测量共同区分了残差容量、相对起点的适应性与后续决策中的有用性。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptive Residual Connections (LARC) give a frozen model a compact numerical state that can learn from feedback. The map $h+BAh$ adds a low-rank correction to a hidden representation. A slow state $ρ$ learns starting factors across tasks; a private fast state $Φ$ copies them, changes with feedback, and resets to the trained initialization. This report specifies an input-side realization of the numerical policy carrier in Memory-Mediated Learning Architecture and examines its factor-space dynamics and learning lifetime. We study a rank-4 input residual with 12,288 trainable parameters on a frozen MiniCPM5-1B-SFT substrate. In a four-candidate program-selection task, two feedback-gradient steps reduce expected query execution error by 24.65 and 36.65 percentage points relative to resetting to the respective trained static and post-adaptation initializations. These development results cover 16 parameter groups and three paired training seeds. A direct support-loss selection rule is much more accurate, reaching 0.78125% error. In a repository-balanced chronological replay of public continuous-integration jobs, retaining online updates raises half-Brier loss from 0.1274 to 0.1808. A fixed follow-up intervention records same-batch non-descent and inconsistent future benefit from shrinking updates. Together, the algebra and measurements distinguish residual capacity, adaptation relative to a starting point, and usefulness on later decisions.

</details>


### [231] [Inference Auctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40070)
*Keegan Harris,Siddharth Prasad,Asher Trockman,Nika Haghtalab,Michael I. Jordan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文设计了一种推理拍卖机制，让用户通过竞价获得更快的LLM服务，实现经济高效的优先级分配，并开发了快速定价算法和自动竞价代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当推理需求超过可用算力时，模型提供商需要决定优先服务哪些请求。用户对LLM API延迟的容忍度各不相同，但现有的优先级定价方案将这些差异压缩为粗糙的固定价格服务层级，无法高效反映用户真实需求。

Method: 设计推理拍卖机制，以经济高效的方式分配优先级且不牺牲延迟；开发快速算法实现激励真实出价的定价；设计自动竞价代理，用户指定推理预算，代理在预算约束下动态调整出价以最大化用户效用。

Result: 实验验证了拍卖的实用性：在保持SGLang（先进推理服务框架）的缓存利用率和延迟优势的同时，提升了系统福利。

Conclusion: 该推理拍卖机制可在不牺牲延迟性能的前提下提升系统福利，兼具经济效率与工程可行性。

Abstract: When inference demand exceeds available compute capacity, model providers must decide which requests should be served first. Users have different tolerances for delay from an LLM API, but current priority pricing schemes compress these differences into coarse fixed-price service tiers. We design an inference auction that allows users to bid for faster service. Our auction allocates priority in an economically efficient way without sacrificing latency, and we develop fast algorithms for implementing prices that incentivize truthful bidding. We also design an autobidding agent for our inference auction, where users specify an inference budget and the autobidder dynamically adjusts its bids over time to maximize user utility subject to the budget constraint. Experiments validate the practicality of our auction: it increases system welfare while maintaining the cache utilization and latency advantages of SGLang, a state-of-the-art inference serving framework.

</details>


### [232] [Accelerated Algorithm for Sparse Regularized Partial Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40075)
*Khoa Nguyen,Dung T. Nguyen,Thong Huynh,Hoang-Hiep Nguyen-Mau,Anh Nguyen,Minh Ngoc Dinh,Juho Kannala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于惩罚重构的优化框架，利用光滑强凸正则化（二次、弹性网等）求解部分最优传输，设计加速一阶算法，实现稀疏传输方案，在多项任务中优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分最优传输（POT）放宽了严格质量守恒约束，应用广泛，且许多场景偏好稀疏传输方案以提升可解释性与计算效率。光滑强凸正则化（如二次、弹性网）虽能诱导稀疏并加速计算，但在计算POT中相比熵正则方法受到的算法关注较少。

Method: 通过惩罚式重构利用光滑强凸正则化，保持原问题结构并支持高效梯度更新；方法适用于一大类促进结构化、稀疏传输方案的正则化。基于该形式，设计在光滑更新与简单投影步骤间交替的加速一阶算法。

Result: 在颜色迁移、域适应、点云配准等基准上，该方法持续优于现有基线，实现更低的传输成本、更高的稀疏性和更快的收敛速度。

Conclusion: 该框架为现代传输问题提供了实用且可扩展的解决方案，兼顾稀疏性与计算效率。

Abstract: Partial Optimal Transport (POT) extends the classical optimal transport problem by relaxing the strict mass conservation constraint, enabling its use in a wide range of real-world applications. In many of these settings, sparse transport plans are preferred for their interpretability and computational benefits. While smooth and strongly convex regularizers - such as quadratic or elastic net - have been vastly used in various machine learning applications to induce sparsity and accelerate computation, they have received less algorithmic attention compared to entropic approaches for computational POT. In this paper, we propose a new optimization framework that leverages these regularizers through a penalty-based reformulation, enabling efficient gradient-based updates while preserving the structure of the original problem. Our method accommodates a broad class of regularizers that promote structured and sparse transport plans. Building on this formulation, we design an accelerated first-order algorithm that alternates between smooth updates and simple projection steps. Through empirical benchmarks on color transfer, domain adaptation, and point cloud registration, our approach consistently outperforms established baselines - achieving lower transport cost, higher sparsity, and faster convergence - making it a practical and scalable solution for modern transport problems.

</details>


### [233] [Replay on Demand: An Emergent Curriculum for Balancing Adaptation and Forgetting in Continued Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40089)
*Lukas Thede,Shengzhuang Chen,Stefan Winzeck,Matthias Bethge,Zeynep Akata,Jonathan Richard Schwarz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Replay on Demand (RoD)，一种根据模型学习动态在线分配回放样本的方法，在持续预训练中同时兼顾新领域适应与旧能力保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续预训练使语言模型适应新领域与知识，但常以遗忘已有能力为代价。回放可缓解此权衡，但固定回放配比无法根据模型实际的保持需求分配训练资源。

Method: RoD 从模型的学习动态中推导回放分配：联合地对适应样本按其剩余学习潜力、对回放样本按其已观测到的遗忘程度进行优先级排序。两类样本竞争同一训练预算，形成一个在线课程，决定每一步训练什么。该方法无需预先指定回放配比。

Result: 在不同模型、规模与适应领域上，RoD 达到或超过了调优后的固定回放基线与模型融合的适应-遗忘前沿，且无需预先设定回放分配。回放集中于更易遗忘的来源，并随训练中遗忘的出现而动态增加和重新分配。

Conclusion: 回放可以基于模型不断演变的状态在线分配，做到在需要时针对需要的内容进行训练。

Abstract: Continued pretraining enables language models to adapt to new domains and knowledge, but often at the cost of forgetting previously acquired capabilities. Replay can mitigate this trade-off, but fixed replay mixtures allocate training independently of the model's actual retention needs. We introduce Replay on Demand (RoD), which instead derives the replay allocation from the model's learning dynamics. RoD jointly prioritizes adaptation samples by their remaining learning potential and replay samples by their observed forgetting. Their competition for a shared training budget yields an online curriculum that determines what to train on at each step. Across models, scales, and adaptation domains, RoD reaches or improves upon the adaptation-forgetting frontier of tuned fixed-replay baselines and model merging without prescribing a replay allocation in advance. Replay concentrates on sources that are more vulnerable to forgetting and dynamically increases and redistributes as forgetting emerges during training. Together, our results show that replay can be allocated online from the model's evolving state, targeting what is needed, when it is needed.

</details>


### [234] [Beyond Model Ranking: Regime Diagnosis for Distributional-Statistical Misspecification in Industrial Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40117)
*Pengyu Nie,Chenglang Xu,Yaoshi Chen,Chaogan Ren,Wei Hu,Chao Yang,Jiangong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了时间序列预测中"训练—部署"差距的一个被忽视来源：标准损失函数内嵌的固定统计先验在工业需求数据的病理态（零膨胀、偏态、高波动）下被系统性违背，从而产生持久偏差；作者据此提出以"分制度相对偏差向量(RBV)"为核心的评估工具包，并通过大规模实验证明该偏差源于损失而非模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序预测模型在基准测试上表现优异，但在工业部署中却出现严重的系统性偏差。传统解释将其归因于时序结构误差或分布漂移，但这些解释忽略了一个互补来源：标准损失函数中嵌入的固定统计先验，在面对工业需求中良性态与病理态（零膨胀、偏态、高波动）混合时被系统性地违背。这种偏差即使在完美时序建模下依然存在，且无法被归一化消除，还会造成被聚合指标掩盖的权衡问题。

Method: 提出以分制度相对偏差向量(RBV)为核心的评估工具包，这是一种与指标无关、按制度分解的诊断工具，用于审计池化训练如何将系统性失配分配到各病理子群体。采用受控归因分析，将RBV分解为模型无关的内在下限（由每个损失的估计目标决定）和可归因于训练的超额分量。开展大规模研究：13种损失目标、3个随机种子、60000+条序列，覆盖RetailShiftBench与M5数据集，并设置随机划分对照组。

Result: 分制度诊断能够区分"优化型"失败与"偏差型"失败；对于均值型损失，分制度训练可解决容量扩展无法消除的池化诱导偏差。给出形式化结构观察：评估分布污染下的风险是病理混合权重的仿射函数，为上述发现提供了理论依据。

Conclusion: 本研究通过机制驱动、面向制度的评估，补充了单纯的模型排序方法，将偏差溯源至损失函数而非模型本身，为弥合工业部署中的训练—部署差距提供了新的评估视角与实践工具。

Abstract: Time-series forecasting models achieve strong benchmark performance but exhibit severe systematic bias in industrial deployments. This train--deploy gap is conventionally attributed to temporal-structural errors or distribution shifts. We characterize a complementary source that these explanations overlook: canonical losses embed fixed statistical priors, while industrial demand mixes benign and pathological regimes---zero-inflation, skewness, high variability---in which these priors are systematically violated. The induced bias persists even under perfect temporal modeling, remains in a distributional-shape component that normalization cannot remove, and creates an aggregation trade-off invisible to aggregate metrics. We turn these observations into an evaluation toolkit centered on the Regime-wise Relative Bias Vector (RBV): a metric-agnostic, regime-decomposed diagnostic that audits how pooled training allocates systematic mismatch across pathological subpopulations. A controlled attribution analysis decomposes RBV into a model-independent intrinsic floor, set by each loss's estimand, and an excess component attributable to training, tracing observed bias to the loss rather than the model. A large-scale study---13 loss objectives, 3 seeds, 60,000+ series spanning RetailShiftBench and M5, with random-split controls---shows that regime-aware diagnosis separates optimization-type from bias-type failure, and that regime-aware training resolves the pooling-induced bias that capacity scaling cannot, for mean-type losses. A formal structural observation, that risk under evaluation-distribution contamination is affine in the pathology mixture weight, grounds these findings. Our work complements model ranking with mechanism-grounded, regime-oriented evaluation.

</details>


### [235] [Scalable Cox Regression via Grouped Risk Sets and Sharper LogSumExp Rates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40120)
*Elizaveta Iashchinskaia,Egor Gladin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出软plus代理与风险集分组压缩策略，为大规模Cox回归提供无偏单样本梯度的随机优化方法，获得更优收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模Cox回归计算代价高，其LogSumExp目标中的归一化项用小批量估计会产生有偏梯度，亟需无偏且高效的随机优化方案。

Method: 用softplus代理引入每个归一化项的辅助标量，实现无偏单样本梯度；对Cox回归利用嵌套风险集结构，将相邻失效事件分组并每组共享一个辅助变量，得到压缩目标并建立一致的得分与曲率界。

Result: 光滑凸LogSumExp目标下取得O(T^{-1/2})平均目标界（优于此前T^{-1/4}）；加强凸正则时末次迭代平方误差率达Õ(T^{-1})；Cox压缩估计相对全量解的均方率达T^{-4/5}（对数因子内），且渐近分布与全量估计一致；合成与真实生存数据实验表现优于随机基线。

Conclusion: 软plus代理与分组压缩结合可同时保证无偏梯度、理论收敛保证与渐近等价性，在风险集缓慢递减的生存数据上有效。

Abstract: Motivated by the computational challenges of large-scale Cox regression, we study stochastic minimization of LogSumExp objectives over large sets. Mini-batch normalizer estimates generally yield biased gradients. We instead use a softplus surrogate that introduces one auxiliary scalar per normalizer and admits unbiased single-sample gradients. For smooth convex LogSumExp objectives, we prove an $O(T^{-1/2})$ averaged objective bound, improving the previous $T^{-1/4}$ analysis. With a strongly convex regularizer on the original variable, we also obtain a last-iterate squared-error rate of $\widetilde{O}(T^{-1})$ without strong convexity in the auxiliary variables. For Cox regression, the normalizers are defined over nested risk sets. We exploit this structure by grouping neighboring failures and sharing one auxiliary variable per group. The resulting compressed objective admits uniform score and curvature bounds that control the errors from grouping and softplus approximation. Together with the general optimization result, these bounds give a mean-square rate of $T^{-4/5}$, up to logarithmic factors, relative to the full Cox solution. The compressed estimator also matches the full estimator's asymptotic distribution. Experiments on synthetic and real survival datasets with slowly decreasing risk sets show a favorable performance relative to stochastic baselines.

</details>


### [236] [Learning Functional Subspaces for Neural Network Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40127)
*Massimo Bini,Anders Christensen,Stephan Alaniz,Judah Goldfeder,Ole Winther,Yann LeCun,Ravid Shwartz-Ziv,Zeynep Akata*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出可学习子空间投影（LSP）方法，端到端学习低秩权重分解中应丢弃的子空间，在LLM和ViT上以更高压缩率取得更优性能，并带来推理加速和内存节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代Transformer虽能力强，但内存与计算需求大。低秩权重分解能同时降低两者且保持矩阵稠密、适配标准硬件。然而现有方法用局部闭式准则（激活能量、逐层重构误差、损失的二次近似）选择每个权重矩阵要移除的子空间，忽略了误差在网络中的传播，导致高压缩时误差随深度累积、性能崩溃。因此需要一个端到端学习子空间的方法。

Method: LSP为每个线性层（或读取相同激活的绑定层组）分配一个正交投影器，所有权重保持冻结，所有投影器针对全局目标联合优化：与稠密模型输出分布的KL散度或模型原始的损练损失。投影器从白化SVD截断初始化，秩按每个投影器每节省参数所诱导的输出KL来分配。训练后投影器合并为标准低秩因子，每个绑定组共享一个因子；在注意力中还可缓存一个窄隐向量替代完整K和V。

Result: 在LLM（OPT-125M/1.3B、Qwen3-4B、Llama-2-7B）和ViT-B/16上，LSP优于基线，且压缩率越高优势越明显。在-70%压缩下，LSP将Llama-2-7B的WikiText-2困惑度降到10.9、零样本平均准确率到42.2%，而最强基线为13.3和36.0%。在小编解码时比稠密模型快1.6倍；缓存共享隐向量后，在128k上下文下权重与KV缓存的总内存减少13.5倍，而未绑定基线分解最多为6.5倍。

Conclusion: LSP通过端到端学习丢弃子空间，克服了局部准则忽略误差传播导致的性能崩溃问题，在高压缩率下显著优于现有方法，并同时带来解码加速和大上下文下的内存节省。

Abstract: Modern transformers pair impressive capabilities with substantial memory and compute demands. Low-rank weight factorization reduces both while keeping the matrices dense, and thus efficient on standard hardware. Existing methods, however, choose the subspace to remove from each weight matrix with local closed-form criteria: activation energy, layer-wise reconstruction error, or a quadratic approximation of the loss. These criteria ignore how errors propagate through the network, so at high compression the errors compound with depth and performance collapses. We introduce Learnable Subspace Projections (LSP), which instead learns the subspaces to discard end-to-end. Each linear layer, or tied group of layers that read the same activations, is assigned an orthogonal projector. All projectors are optimized jointly against a global objective--the KL divergence to the dense model's output distribution or the model's original training loss--while the pretrained weights remain frozen. Projectors are initialized from a whitened SVD truncation, and ranks are allocated by the output KL each projector induces per parameter saved. After training, the projectors merge into standard low-rank factors, with each tied group sharing one factor. In attention, this also lets the model cache one narrow latent in place of full keys and values. Across LLMs (OPT-125M/1.3B, Qwen3-4B, Llama-2-7B) and ViT-B/16, LSP outperforms baselines, and its advantage widens as compression increases. At -70% compression, LSP brings Llama-2-7B to 10.9 WikiText-2 perplexity and 42.2% mean zero-shot accuracy, versus 13.3 and 36.0% for the strongest baseline. The factorized model decodes up to 1.6x faster than the dense model at small batch sizes, and aching the shared latent shrinks the combined memory of weights and KV cache by 13.5x at a 128k-token context, versus at most 6.5x for untied baseline factorizations.

</details>


### [237] [Prototype-Rule Neurosymbolic Regularization for Rank-Constrained Tensor Neural Networks under Label Scarcity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40131)
*Eftychios Protopapadakis,Konstantinos Makantasis,Konstantinos M. Giannoutakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究在Rank-R张量神经网络中引入可微原型规则的归纳偏置，用于高光谱分类，并进行多项基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rank-R张量网络虽能降低高阶输入参数化，但未显式约束所学表示的类别几何结构；作者想探究可微原型规则能否在有限监督下提供互补归纳偏置。

Method: 在Rank-R目标中加入基于原型的正则化，并可选地在推理时将原型证据与神经logits融合；在四个高光谱基准上以四种Rank-R配置评估，采用七折分层与空间分离折以减少泄漏，另做类别支持预算从2到20样本的空间研究。

Result: 空间评估下，完整神经符号推理使Macro-F1变化：Botswana +8.82个百分点、Indian Pines +5.49、Pavia University +1.59、Salinas -0.62；收益主要来自训练时正则化，推理融合效果小且依赖数据集。

Conclusion: 原型规则可作为Rank-R张量学习的互补归纳偏置，尤其通过训练时正则化提升有限监督下的类别几何约束效果。

Abstract: Rank-constrained tensor neural networks reduce the parameterization of high-order inputs, but they do not explicitly constrain class geometry in the learned representation. This study investigates whether a differentiable prototype-rule can provide a complementary inductive bias for Rank-R tensor learning under limited supervision. The proposed framework augments the Rank-R objective with prototype-based regularization and optionally fuses prototype evidence with neural logits at inference. Four hyperspectral benchmarks are evaluated with four Rank-R configurations under both seven-fold stratification and spatially separated folds that mitigate leakage; a separate spatial study varies the class support budget from 2 to 20 samples. Under spatial evaluation, full neurosymbolic inference changes Macro-F1 score by +8.82 percentage points on Botswana, +5.49 on Indian Pines, +1.59 on Pavia University, and -0.62 on Salinas. Most of the benefit arises from training-time regularization, whereas inference fusion is small and dataset dependent.

</details>


### [238] [Game-Guided Skill Discovery through Self-Play for Playable Agent Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40137)
*Seungeun Rho,Jeonghwan Kim,Xue Bin Peng,Sehoon Ha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 GGSD 框架，通过游戏自博弈发现人类可直接操控的可玩性运动技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督技能发现方法难以同时满足技能的语义区分度、可解释性与表达力，且技能难以被人类直接使用。

Method: 将技能发现建立在竞争性博弈上：分层智能体与自身历史版本对抗，高层策略从少量离散技能中选择，低层技能条件策略学习对应行为；训练后人类可替换高层策略直接操控。

Result: 在 Ant、Franka-arm 和 Unitree G1 环境中，生成的技能可被人类组合解决未见任务（如 Maze、CubePush），无需额外训练。

Conclusion: GGSD 通过博弈驱动的分层技能发现，得到少量、语义清晰的技能，且技能转移产生涌现连招行为，扩展了整体表达力。

Abstract: We present Game-Guided Skill Discovery (GGSD), a framework that uses self-play in games to discover motor skills that are directly playable by humans. Playable skills provide a compact abstraction for controlling embodied agents through a small set of learned behaviors rather than low-level actions. To be effective, these skills should be semantically distinct, interpretable, and expressive; properties that existing unsupervised skill-discovery methods often fail to achieve simultaneously. GGSD achieves these desiderata by grounding skill discovery in competitive gameplay. A hierarchical agent competes against its past selves, with a high-level policy selecting from a small discrete skill set and a skill-conditioned low-level policy learning the corresponding behaviors. After training, a human can replace the high-level policy and directly control the agent through the same discrete skills. Despite the small number of high-level actions, skill transitions give rise to emergent combo behaviors, expanding expressivity beyond individual primitives. Across Ant, Franka-arm, and Unitree G1 environments, we show that GGSD produces human-playable skills that humans can compose to solve unseen tasks, such as Maze and CubePush, without additional training. An interactive demo is available at https://ggsd-demo.github.io.

</details>


### [239] [From DNA Design to DNA Slimming: Auditable Agentic Discovery of a Deletion-Only Designer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40143)
*Joel Shor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出"序列精简"（sequence slimming）这一删除型任务并建立首个专用基准，用编码智能体ERA将替换型设计器GrAdaBeam改造为GRADASLIM，在五个转录因子结合靶点上多数设定优于随机与贪心方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧凑的调控DNA可节省载体载荷空间、降低合成与检测成本，并揭示驱动预测活性的序列特征。然而现有基于模型的核酸设计器多通过替换优化固定长度序列，未探讨从已有功能元件中删除哪些碱基仍能保留活性。

Method: 将序列精简定义为在保持活性的前提下选取等长、保序的子序列；仿照NucleoBench设计量化评测，兼顾序列缩减与功能保持；要求精简器同时返回子序列及其源索引以便核验。以编码智能体ERA搜索可执行设计程序，从替换型设计器GrAdaBeam出发改造出GRADASLIM。

Result: 在五个转录因子结合靶点的留出评估中，比较随机、贪心与ERA引导的精简（400 bp和100 bp）：ERA在9/10个设定中均值最高；ERA减贪心的配对bootstrap区间在全部五个400 bp设定中高于零，在一个100 bp设定中低于零，其余四个与零重叠。

Conclusion: 该工作提出并首个系统评测了删除型序列精简任务，表明编码智能体可自动改造设计程序以在缩减序列的同时较好保留预测活性，但在较短长度设定上优势尚不稳定。

Abstract: Compact regulatory DNA can free up space in vector payloads, reduce synthesis and assay burden, and expose which sequence features drive predicted activity. Yet most model-based nucleic-acid designers optimize fixed-length sequences through substitutions; they do not ask which bases of an existing functional element can be removed while retaining predicted activity. We define the task of sequence slimming as selecting an exact-length, order-preserving subsequence while retaining activity. Modeled on the design benchmark NucleoBench, we propose a quantitative evaluation for slimming that balances sequence reduction with maintaining function. Each slimmer must return both the subsequence and its source indices, which can be used to verify that the slimmer obeyed task requirements. To our knowledge, this is the first dedicated benchmark of this deletion-only problem. The coding agent Empirical Research Assistant (ERA) then searched over executable designer programs. ERA received the task prompt and a successful substitution-only designer GrAdaBeam as a starting program, and it modified the designer to produce GRADASLIM. We report held-out evaluations for five transcription-factor binding targets, comparing random, greedy, and ERA-guided slimming at 400 and 100 bp. ERA has the highest mean in 9/10 settings. Paired bootstrap intervals for ERA minus greedy are above zero in all five 400-bp settings, below zero in one 100-bp setting, and overlap zero in the remaining four.

</details>


### [240] [Policy Iteration Is Not Strongly Polynomial for Deterministic Markov Decision Processes: The Price of Algorithmic Anarchy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40147)
*Han Zhong,Yinyu Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在每状态至多两个动作的确定性折扣MDP上，Howard策略迭代存在关于状态数的指数级迭代下界，从而否定其强多项式性，并与单纯形法（Dantzig规则）形成指数分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Howard策略迭代在最简单的确定性折扣MDP上是否具有强多项式复杂度，并厘清其与单纯形法（Dantzig枢轴规则）在复杂度上的本质差异。

Method: 构造性下界分析：在折扣因子作为输入的确定性折扣MDP（每状态最多两个动作）上，建立迭代次数的指数下界；并进一步在奖励仅取对数位长时给出拉伸指数下界；通过对比Howard的分散/同时自私改进与Dantzig跨状态协调选择最大增益动作，揭示两者的算法差异。

Result: 得到状态数指数级的迭代下界，排除Howard策略迭代的强多项式性；与在同类问题上被证明为强多项式的Dantzig规则单纯形法形成指数分离；即使奖励为对数位长，仍有拉伸指数下界。

Conclusion: Howard策略迭代在确定性折扣MDP上本质脆弱，其分散式改进与Dantzig的全局协调单动作选择之间存在显著“算法无政府状态的价格”，说明协调式枢轴规则可带来实质性的复杂度优势。

Abstract: We establish an exponential iteration lower bound in the number of states for Howard's policy iteration on deterministic discounted Markov decision processes, with at most two actions per state. This rules out strong polynomiality of Howard's policy iteration when the discount factor is part of the input and yields an exponential separation from the simplex method with Dantzig's pivoting rule, which is proved to be strongly polynomial on this class. Even when each reward is restricted to logarithmic bit length, we obtain a stretched-exponential iteration lower bound. The gap between Howard's decentralized and simultaneous selfish improvements and Dantzig's coordinated selection of a single action with the largest gain across all states reveals a ``price'' of algorithmic anarchy.

</details>


### [241] [From Spectra to Joint Schedules in LLM Pre-training: 3+3(+2) Scaling-Law Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40148)
*Yichen Wang,Fanghui Liu,Yudong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究学习率与批量大小调度如何影响幂律学习曲线，用带线性随机特征的噪声在线SGD建立精确Volterra方程，揭示幂律保持/改变/破坏的条件，并在nanoGPT上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 幂律学习曲线通常被视为模型与数据的固定属性，但学习率和批量大小调度会改变观测到的损失，因此需要理解这种依赖机制。

Method: 在线性随机特征的噪声在线SGD中，条件于表示，用精确Volterra方程将响应分解为推动未解析目标误差的forcing项和传播随机误差注入的memory核；引入内在时间T_t=Ση_s与噪声注入比r_t=B_t/η_t分析联合调度。

Result: 证明分量服从幂律当且仅当其累积加权谱质量具有相应低谱标度；给出调度保持、改变或破坏干净幂律的尖锐条件及噪声削减的记忆上限；幂律随机特征模型呈3+3(+2)传播机制；nanoGPT实验显示匹配B/η路径的学习率与批量调度在内在时间上近乎等价，forcing-memory代理能准确预测损失，拟合指数可识别LLM所处机制。

Conclusion: 幂律学习曲线并非固定属性，而由内在时间与噪声注入比的联合调度决定；该框架统一解释了调度对损失的影响并提供了可识别LLM传播机制的代理模型。

Abstract: Power-law learning curves are often treated as fixed properties of a model and its data, although learning-rate and batch-size schedules can change the observed loss. We study this dependence in noisy online SGD with linear random features. Conditional on the representation, an exact Volterra equation separates two response components: a forcing term that propagates unresolved target error and a memory kernel that propagates stochastic-error injections. We prove that either component follows a power law if and only if its cumulative weighted spectral mass has the corresponding low-spectrum scaling; individual eigenvalues and target coefficients need not obey coordinatewise power laws. Under a joint schedule, intrinsic time $T_t=\sum_{s<t}η_s$ controls optimization progress, while $r_t=B_t/η_t$ controls noise injection. Their interaction yields sharp conditions under which a schedule preserves, changes, or destroys the clean power law, together with a memory ceiling on noise reduction. The power-law random-feature model realizes this mechanism in $3+3(+2)$ propagation regimes with phase-dependent compute rates. Controlled nanoGPT experiments show that (1) learning-rate and batch-size schedules with matched $B/η$ paths are nearly equivalent in intrinsic time, (2) a forcing-memory surrogate accurately predicts loss across schedules, and (3) its fitted exponents across real-world datasets identify the regime of LLMs in $3+3(+2)$ map.

</details>


### [242] [Role-Adaptive Policy Optimization for Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40149)
*Seonvin Cho,Soohyun Choi,Songnam Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RAPO通过为策略的不同角色（执行与引导）自适应调整更新系数，提升了离线强化学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习中，策略正则在策略改进与依赖不确定价值估计之间权衡，而这一权衡在执行动作选择与供给critic引导时可能不同。但如TD3+BC等方法通过共享策略耦合了这两种角色，缺乏灵活区分。

Method: 提出Role-Adaptive Policy Optimization (RAPO)，根据不同角色自适应策略更新系数，通过对候选策略更新微分来学习系数。对TD3+BC，分离bootstrap与execution actor并独立调整系数；对IQL，保留原值更新并仅调整优势加权策略提取中的逆温度。

Result: 在D4RL locomotion与AntMaze任务上，RAPO优于两个基算法，对TD3+BC提升更大，其RAPO实例平均超越基线。

Conclusion: RAPO能有效解耦策略角色并自适应优化，显著提升离线强化学习性能，尤其适用于角色耦合的算法如TD3+BC。

Abstract: Policy regularization in offline reinforcement learning balances policy improvement against reliance on uncertain value estimates. This balance can differ between selecting actions for execution and supplying actions for critic bootstrapping, yet methods such as TD3+BC couple these roles through a shared policy. We propose Role-Adaptive Policy Optimization (RAPO), which adapts policy-update coefficients according to their roles in value learning and execution. RAPO learns these coefficients by differentiating through candidate policy updates formed using the base algorithm's actor objective. For TD3+BC, RAPO separates bootstrap and execution actors and adapts their coefficients independently: the bootstrap objective penalizes policy-induced changes in target values, while the execution objective evaluates a local policy-improvement surrogate. For IQL, whose value learning is already independent of the execution actor, RAPO preserves the original value updates and adapts only the inverse temperature in advantage-weighted policy extraction. Experiments on D4RL locomotion and AntMaze tasks show improvements over both base algorithms, with larger gains for TD3+BC, whose RAPO instantiation outperforms baselines on average.

</details>


### [243] [MANET-GNN: Learned Decentralized Optimization of Power Allocation in Multi-Channel MANETs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40170)
*Tomer Alter,Nir Shlezinger,Michael Segal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MANET-GNN，一个基于消息传递图神经网络的分布式学习优化框架，用于动态多跳多信道MANET中的去中心化发射功率分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代MANET利用多频信道并支持异构业务模式，使得去中心化发射功率分配日益困难，需要统一且可扩展的解决方案。

Method: 将覆盖单播、组播、多商品、汇聚及多对多通信的受限端到端吞吐量最大化问题（集中式、非凸）作为无监督训练目标；MANET-GNN仅使用局部（可能含噪）CSI和规定数量的邻居消息交换进行分布式推理。

Result: 数值结果表明，MANET-GNN在各通信框架下达到接近集中式方案的性能，对信道不确定性具有鲁棒性，并能有效扩展至不同MANET配置。

Conclusion: MANET-GNN可作为低时延、可泛化的去中心化学习优化器，适用于动态多跳多信道MANET的功率分配。

Abstract: MANETs enable flexible infrastructure-less wireless connectivity in dynamic and resource-constrained environments. As modern MANETs exploit multiple frequency channels and support heterogeneous traffic patterns, decentralized transmit-power allocation becomes increasingly challenging. We develop a unified learned optimization framework for decentralized power allocation in dynamic multi-hop, multi-channel MANETs. We formulate a constrained end-to-end throughput maximization problem covering unicast, multicast, multicommodity, convergecast, and many-to-many communication. Although centralized and non-convex, this problem serves as an unsupervised training objective for MANET-GNN, a message-passing GNN that operates as a distributed learned optimizer. MANET-GNN uses only local, possibly noisy, CSI and a prescribed number of neighbor message exchanges, enabling low-latency decentralized inference while generalizing across topologies and network sizes. Numerical results show that MANET-GNN achieves centralized-competitive performance across communication frameworks, remains robust to channel uncertainty, and scales effectively across MANET configurations.

</details>


### [244] [Cheap to Draw, Expensive to Trust: Certifying Test-Time Scaling Curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40190)
*Sohail,Sarkar,Shakuntala Baichoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究"采样k个答案并由验证器选最优"这一测试时缩放曲线的统计认证成本问题，推导出认证整条曲线的最小最大（minimax）代价（至对数因子），并据此提出一种无需先导实验的配对审计方法，显著降低所需生成答案数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 采样-验证（sampling-and-verification）是提升测试时精度的简单手段，其效果以"精度对采样数k的缩放曲线"呈现。问题在于：从该曲线上读取某个预算需要在观测完所有点之后再选，因此只有能同时覆盖所有预算的置信带才能保护这一选择；而固定精确二项设计在这种认证上代价极高（如100题基准上认证64个预算需192,000个答案）。作者指出其中大部分成本花在了"错误的不确定性"上——基准是固定的题目列表，预算64时约四分之三的方差来自题目间差异，逐题重新审阅的审计不必为其付费。因此动机是降低认证整条缩放曲线的统计成本。

Method: 作者推导认证整条曲线的最小最大代价（至对数因子），将其分解为三部分：（1）校准分数分布尾部；（2）区分不同题目；（3）随曲线累加的同题内噪声。在单一基准上，第三部分收敛为"一次答案影响量在最优答案-题目分配下"的方差——这是任何有效审计都要付、而学习到该分配的审计（至对数）能够达到的下界，随精度提高成立。据此提出一种基于"同题两次独立抽样"指数不等式的配对审计（paired audit），无需先导实验。方法还通过同一条路径认证pass@k与多数投票，并可将置信带扩展到题目总体及依赖先前答案的情形。

Result: 在185个留出（held-out）分数池上，该方法在64个预算下所用答案数为最便宜竞争性认证审计的0.74倍，在1,024个预算下为0.53倍。在一项新生成的MMLU-Pro研究中，它以79,133个答案认证了曲线，与事先根据该研究同题内方差拟合的成本律预测值相差在0.6%以内。

Conclusion: 通过在固定题目基准上正确刻画不确定性来源（区分题目间方差与同题内噪声）并采用配对审计，可大幅降低认证整条测试时缩放曲线的样本成本，且理论下界与实际表现吻合；该框架可推广至pass@k、多数投票及更一般的题目总体和序列依赖答案场景。

Abstract: Sampling several answers and keeping the one a verifier scores highest is one of the simplest ways to buy accuracy at test time. Its effect is reported as a scaling curve: accuracy against the number $k$ of sampled answers. The curve is cheap to draw and expensive to trust. A budget read off it is chosen after looking at every point, so only a band that covers all budgets at once protects the choice, and on a 100-question benchmark a fixed exact-binomial design needs 192,000 generated answers to certify 64 budgets to within $\pm1/32$ at 95%. Most of that cost pays for the wrong uncertainty. A benchmark is a fixed list of questions; at budget 64, about three quarters of the variance of a selected answer's correctness lies between questions, and an audit that revisits every question need not pay for it. We derive the minimax cost of certifying the whole curve, up to logarithmic factors. It has three parts: calibrating the tail of the score distribution, telling the questions apart, and within-question noise summed along the curve. At a single benchmark the last part sharpens to the variance of one answer's influence under the best allocation of answers to questions, which every valid audit pays and an audit that learns the allocation attains, up to a logarithm, as the precision grows. A paired audit built on an exponential inequality for two independent draws at the same question needs no pilot. On 185 held-out score pools it uses 0.74 times the answers of the cheapest competing certified audit at 64 budgets and 0.53 times at 1,024, and on a newly generated MMLU-Pro study it certified the curve with 79,133 answers, within 0.6% of what a cost law fitted beforehand predicted from the study's within-question variance. The same paths certify pass@$k$ and majority voting, and the bands extend to populations of questions and to answers that depend on earlier ones.

</details>


### [245] [Near-Linear Accuracy Bounds for Moreau--Yosida Unadjusted Langevin Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40193)
*Yuchen Xin,Zhihua Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 为经典MYULA算法建立了近线性精度界，在显式步长条件下将不变测度偏差控制为$\widetilde O(h)$，并给出$\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-1})$的迭代复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标分布$ \pi\propto e^{-f-g}$中$g$仅为凸且全局Lipschitz，缺乏光滑性，导致经典Langevin分析难以直接应用，需要为Moreau-Yosida松弛的MYULA提供更紧的精度保证。

Method: 在显式依赖参数的步长条件下分析MYULA；通过Poisson型估计将二阶平稳残差转化为Wasserstein界；再将不变测度偏差估计与Moreau近似偏差、Wasserstein收缩性结合。

Result: 不变测度相对Moreau平滑目标的偏差为$\widetilde O(h)$，误差系数对逆平滑参数仅对数依赖；第$N$步分布满足$\sqrt m\,W_2(\mu_N,\pi)\le\varepsilon$需$\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-1})$次迭代；无需假设三阶导数或Lipschitz Hessian；每步仅一次梯度与一次精确近端计算。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了MYULA的近线性精度界，突破了以往对更高阶光滑性的依赖，为处理非光滑复合势提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: We establish near-linear accuracy bounds for the classical Moreau--Yosida unadjusted Langevin algorithm (MYULA). The target is $π\propto e^{-f-g}$, where $f\in C^2(\mathbb{R}^d)$ is $m$-strongly convex with Lipschitz gradient and $g$ is convex and globally Lipschitz. Under an explicit parameter-dependent step-size condition, we bound the invariant-measure bias relative to the Moreau-smoothed target by $\widetilde O(h)$, with only logarithmic dependence on the inverse smoothing parameter in the error coefficient. Combining this estimate with the Moreau approximation bias and Wasserstein contraction gives $\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-1})$ iterations to make the $N$th-iterate law $μ_N$ satisfy $\sqrt m\,W_2(μ_N,π)\le\varepsilon$, for fixed model parameters and initialization. We bound the stationary error directly, without assuming third derivatives or a Lipschitz Hessian. Each iteration uses one gradient evaluation and one exact proximal evaluation. The key idea in our analysis is to convert a second-order stationary residual into a Wasserstein bound using a Poisson-based estimate.

</details>
