### [246] [PhantomEnvironments: Training LLM Agents in Fictional Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40221)
*Anmol Kabra,Swathi Saravana Selvam,Albert Gong,Chao Wan,Christian Belardi,Dongyoung Go,Katie Z. Luo,Kilian Q. Weinberger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出用纯规则生成的虚构世界环境（PhantomEnvironments）来训练LLM搜索智能体，无需LLM参与生成、零边际成本，训练出的智能体能迁移到真实世界多跳搜索基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用强化学习训练LLM智能体受限于环境：需提供可验证奖励、支持长程交互且能低成本扩展。现有方法要么依赖昂贵的人工标注数据，要么依赖LLM生成的环境，后者存在幻觉与基准污染风险。

Method: 构建PhantomEnvironments：基于规则生成的多轮RL环境，由虚构世界构成；智能体需在模板化文章语料中检索以回答多跳问题。环境生成完全不使用LLM，仅靠规则，边际成本为零。

Result: 这些与真实世界无任何事实关联的极简环境训练出的智能体可迁移到真实多跳搜索基准，在新基准上常优于用真实数据训练的模型；智能体能泛化到未见过的虚构宇宙；Qwen模型的搜索预算随问题难度近似线性增长，显示搜索规模化的涌现；消融实验表明跳数（hop count）比约束或比较更能驱动迁移。

Conclusion: 即使最简单的规则生成环境也是训练可泛化LLM智能体的高效且免费的资源，为RL环境瓶颈提供了一种廉价可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Training LLM agents with reinforcement learning (RL) is bottlenecked by environments, which must provide verifiable rewards, support long-horizon interaction, and scale cheaply. Existing approaches rely on costly human-curated data or on LLM-generated environments that risk hallucinations and benchmark contamination. We show that LLMs can instead be trained into capable search agents using synthetic environments generated entirely by rules, whose generation requires no LLM and has zero marginal cost. We build PhantomEnvironments, multi-turn RL environments from fictional worlds, where agents must search a corpus of templated articles to answer multi-hop questions. Despite sharing no facts with the real world, these strikingly simple environments yield agents that transfer to real-world multi-hop search benchmarks, often outperforming real-world training data on newer benchmarks. Trained agents generalize to unseen fictional universes, and Qwen models learn to scale their search budget roughly linearly with question difficulty, suggesting emergent search scaling from environment interaction alone. Ablating environment complexity reveals that hop count drives transfer more than constraints or comparisons: even the simplest rule-generated environments are a surprisingly effective, free resource for training generalizable LLM agents.

</details>


### [247] [Distribution Matching Distillation for Continuous Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40235)
*Paul Le Van Kiem,Dario Shariatian,Umut Simsekli,Alain Durmus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究通过分布蒸馏来降低连续扩散语言模型的网络评估次数（NFEs），提出统一框架并衍生出 Simplex-DMD 和 Reinforce-DMD 两种方法，在 OpenWebText 上显著降低生成困惑度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续扩散语言模型虽然并行生成所有 token，但高质量生成仍需数百次网络评估，计算成本高。作者希望利用学生模型的概率化 token 输出，通过分布蒸馏降低这一成本。

Method: 提出统一公式，将学生输出参数化方式与梯度估计器联系起来，得到两种共享学生架构和反向 KL 匹配目标的方法：Simplex-DMD 使用连续 token 松弛和路径梯度；Reinforce-DMD 使用类别采样和学习密度比的 REINFORCE。两者均针对多步生成开发，并研究各自的训练与采样选择。

Result: 在 OpenWebText 上，1024 token 序列，Simplex-DMD 仅用 4 次 NFE 达到生成困惑度 45.6（unigram 熵 5.44 nats），在匹配熵和采样预算下相比最强扩散基线降低 49%。Reinforce-DMD 在更大预算下改善前沿，256 NFE 时达到困惑度 14.9（熵 5.00 nats），相同比较协议下降低 20%。

Conclusion: 分布蒸馏可有效减少连续扩散语言模型的网络评估次数，Simplex-DMD 适合极低 NFE 场景，Reinforce-DMD 适合较大预算场景，两者共同推进了生成质量与效率的前沿。

Abstract: Continuous diffusion language models generate all tokens in parallel, yet high-quality generation can still require hundreds of network evaluations (NFEs). We study how distributional distillation can reduce this cost by exploiting the student's probabilistic token outputs. Our unified formulation connects the student's output parameterization to the resulting gradient estimators and yields two methods with the same student architecture and reverse-KL matching objective: Simplex-DMD uses continuous token relaxations and pathwise gradients, while Reinforce-DMD uses categorical sampling and REINFORCE with a learned density ratio. We develop both methods for multi-step generation and investigate the training and sampling choices associated with each parameterization. On OpenWebText, for sequences of 1,024 tokens, Simplex-DMD achieves a generative perplexity of 45.6 at a unigram entropy of 5.44 nats in just 4 NFEs, a 49% reduction relative to the strongest evaluated diffusion baseline at matched entropy and sampling budget. Reinforce-DMD improves the frontier at larger budgets, reaching a generative perplexity of 14.9 at an entropy of 5.00 nats with 256 NFEs, a 20% reduction under the same comparison protocol.

</details>


### [248] [OpenTSLM TeeMoE: A Unified Time-Series Language Model for Forecasting, Contextual Prediction, and Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40265)
*Tony Chen,Timo Stoffregen,Maxwell Xu,Thomas Kaar,Martin Maritsch,Geremia Pompei,Nicolas Zumarraga,Robert Jakob,Paul Schmiedmayer,Patrick Langer,Juncheng Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出OpenTSLM TeeMoE，一种通用时间序列语言模型，通过LoRA专家混合架构统一时序预测、上下文条件预测与语言驱动时序推理三类能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时序基础模型能力割裂：数值模型擅长预测，语言模型擅长上下文理解与分析，如何统一异构能力且不牺牲各自性能是核心挑战。

Method: 在共享主干上独立训练三个低秩（LoRA）专家：预测聚合、原生预测、时序分析；再由学习的LoRA专家混合控制器针对每个请求对冻结的参数更新加权。

Result: 在时序预测、上下文条件预测和语言时序推理等常用基准上表现优异，在GIFT-Eval按MASE均值排名、Context is Key按RCRPS、TimeSeriesExam按准确率均进入前三。

Conclusion: 所提模型可作为通用时序语言模型，无需牺牲单项能力即可统一多种异构时序能力，验证了LoRA专家混合统一路线的有效性。

Abstract: Real-world time-series applications increasingly require models that can handle time series forecasting, context-conditioned prediction, and language-based temporal reasoning. Yet current time-series foundation models remain fragmented across these capabilities: numerical specialists often provide the strongest forecasts, while language-based models offer broader contextual understanding and analysis. A central challenge is to unify these heterogeneous capabilities without reducing their individual performance. We introduce OpenTSLM TeeMoE, a generalist time-series language model that can forecast directly from observed time series, reason over textual context and temporal patterns, and synthesize and refine predictions from external numerical forecasting specialists. We independently train three low-rank experts for forecast aggregation, native forecasting, and temporal analysis over a shared backbone. A learned LoRA mixture-of-experts controller then weights their frozen parameter updates for each request. Our proposed model achieves strong performance on widely used benchmarks for time series forecasting, context-conditioned prediction, and language-based temporal reasoning, ranking among the top three on GIFT-Eval by mean MASE rank, Context is Key by RCRPS, and TimeSeriesExam by accuracy.

</details>


### [249] [cua-speedrun: Standardized Benchmarking of the Speed of Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40284)
*Pranjal Aggarwal,Lawrence Keunho Jang,Sean Welleck,Daniel Fried,Ruslan Salakhutdinov,Jing Yu Koh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 cua-speedrun，一个标准化基础设施与任务集，用于评估计算机使用代理（CUA）的速度与效率，并揭示推理强度、代理框架和环境延迟对性能、速度与成本的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CUA 虽在诸多基准上超越人类，但其速度与成本仍是广泛部署的关键障碍。当前 CUA 基准因复杂的基础设施、机器与容器配置差异而面临可复现性危机，难以可靠评估执行速度。

Method: cua-speedrun 采用统一的虚拟机设置与执行流水线，并设计通用代理接口，使单一代理实现可跨不同基准无缝运行。在四个 CUA 基准上，评估推理强度、代理框架与环境延迟对性能、速度和成本的影响，并探索缩减评估任务集而不损失统计功效的方法。

Result: 没有单一模型系列在三者（性能、速度、成本）上均最优；开源权重模型均未达到前沿水平；反直觉地，对某些模型提高推理强度反而加快任务完成，而更快环境输入输出可能拖慢整体任务完成时间；可有效缩减多数基准的评估任务集而不降低统计功效。

Conclusion: cua-speedrun 为快速、高效 CUA 的研发提供结构化进展路径，有望解锁新的实际应用场景，全部代码、基础设施与分析已公开。

Abstract: Computer use agents (CUAs), which use graphical user interfaces (GUIs) to complete tasks on a computer, have recently surpassed human performance on many standard benchmarks, including difficult long-horizon tasks. Their capabilities are undoubtedly impressive, however, a key barrier to the widespread adoption and deployment of CUAs remains their speed and cost. Progress towards faster yet capable CUAs requires reliable evaluation of their speed, but many CUA benchmarks currently face a reproducibility crisis. Benchmarks are based on complex infrastructure with varying machine and container configurations that confound the evaluation of the execution speed of CUAs. Towards addressing this gap, we propose cua-speedrun, which introduces standardized infrastructure and task sets, with a focus on evaluating the speed and efficiency of CUAs. cua-speedrun uses a uniform virtual machine setup and execution pipeline, along with a common agent interface that enables single-agent implementations to operate seamlessly across different benchmarks. Across four different CUA benchmarks, we evaluate how reasoning effort, agent harnesses, and environment latency affect performance, speed, and cost. We find no single model family is optimal for all three; none of the open-weight models are on the frontier, and also, unintuitively, for some models increasing the reasoning effort can speed up task completion, while faster environment input-output can slow down overall task completion time. We also demonstrate that we can effectively reduce the evaluation task set of most CUA benchmarks without degrading overall statistical power, allowing for more efficient benchmarking and comparison. We believe cua-speedrun will enable structured progress towards fast, efficient CUAs, unlocking new real-world use cases and applications. All code, infrastructure, and analysis are available at https://cuaspeedrun.com.

</details>


### [250] [PMosFM: Preconditioned Manifold Matching for One-Step Physics-Constrained Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40287)
*Zhangyong Liang,Haibin Ling*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PMosFM：一种预条件流形单步流匹配框架，用于一步物理约束生成，降低训练与采样成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理约束生成模型需满足目标分布与物理约束，但现有方法通过迭代修正增加采样成本，或通过残差优化与轨迹展开增加训练成本。

Method: 将约束编码进流形解码器，在内蕴坐标中学习传输，无需独立残差损失或终端残差展开；引入几何预条件器利用解码器诱导度量重缩放坐标，正则化协方差变换近似白化插值态输入；采用有限区间目标耦合速度监督与物理空间解码端点一致性。

Result: 证明精确参数化可消除残差诱导的高斯-牛顿曲率；几何与协方差效应在局部条件界中可分离；物理流映射误差可界定端点分布误差。消融实验检验条件化，实验评估优化器更新耗时与内存；推理仅需一次神经传输评估加物理解码，训练与采样时间低于多步基线且物理与分布保真度相当。

Conclusion: PMosFM通过预条件流形匹配实现一步物理约束生成，在保持保真度的同时显著降低训练和采样成本，代码与数据将公开。

Abstract: Physics-constrained generative models aim to generate physical fields that match a target distribution and satisfy prescribed constraints. However, enforcing these constraints often increases sampling costs through iterative corrections or training costs through residual optimization and trajectory unrolling. To address this issue, we introduce \textbf{P}reconditioned \textbf{M}anifold \textbf{o}ne-\textbf{s}tep \textbf{F}low \textbf{M}atching (\textbf{PMosFM}), a preconditioned manifold matching framework for one-step physics-constrained generation. By encoding constraints in a manifold decoder, PMosFM learns transport in intrinsic coordinates without separate residual losses or terminal residual unrolling. A geometric preconditioner rescales coordinates using the decoder-induced metric, while a regularized covariance transform approximately whitens the interpolation-state inputs. A finite-interval objective couples velocity supervision with consistency between decoded endpoints in physical space. We show that exact parameterization removes residual-induced Gauss--Newton curvature, that geometric and covariance effects separate in a local conditioning bound, and that physical flow-map error bounds endpoint distributional error. Controlled ablations examine conditioning, and experiments evaluate optimizer-update time and memory footprint. At inference, PMosFM uses one neural transport evaluation followed by physical decoding. Experiments across benchmarks show lower training and sampling time than the multi-step baselines at comparable physical and distributional fidelity. Code and datasets will be released publicly.

</details>


### [251] [Disentangling Computation in Multi-Task Neural Networks with the Green's Operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40292)
*James Hazelden*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出用有限时域格林算子（Green's operator）刻画训练好的循环网络中扰动的全局路由，从而在任务间与时间间两个维度揭示计算的组织与复用结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往对训练后循环网络计算组织的分析多聚焦于神经活动的几何结构、动力学基元（dynamical motifs）或局部扰动增长，缺少一个能够整体刻画"扰动如何沿轨迹被路由"的全局视角。作者希望用一个统一的、全局的一阶扰动响应工具来理解计算如何在任务与时间上被组织与复用。

Method: 研究网络的一阶扰动响应，定义有限时域格林算子：它把轨迹上每个源点处的扰动映射到其下游的状态空间响应，从而直接表示扰动路由。对该算子做简单归约，得到同一计算的任务间视图与时间间视图；采用无矩阵（matrix-free）乘积，无需构造完整算子即可获得这些视图。将其应用于一个灵活的多任务循环网络。

Result: 在多任务循环网络中，任务维归约揭示了已知计算基元的结构化复用；时间维归约揭示了因果通路以及这些通路在训练过程中的涌现方式。

Conclusion: 格林算子提供了一种用于映射所学动力学计算之组织的全局响应几何，说明全局一阶扰动响应可作为分析循环网络计算组织与复用的通用工具。

Abstract: How is computation organized and reused across tasks and time in a trained recurrent network? Most analyses emphasize the geometry of neural activity, dynamical motifs, or local perturbation growth. We instead study the network's global first-order perturbation response. The finite-horizon Green's operator maps perturbations at each source along a trajectory to their downstream state-space responses and therefore directly represents perturbation routing. Simple reductions of this operator provide task-to-task and time-to-time views of the same computation, while matrix-free products make these views accessible without constructing the full operator. In a flexible multitask recurrent network, task reductions reveal structured reuse of known computational motifs, while temporal reductions reveal causal pathways and how they emerge during training. Our main point is simple: the Green's operator provides a global response geometry for mapping the organization of learned dynamical computation.

</details>


### [252] [Compression Footprints as Security Signals for Model-Poisoning Defense in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40312)
*Sachi Shome,William Eiers*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将联邦学习中的有损压缩从"误差源"重新定位为"安全信号"，提出压缩足迹概念，并据此设计了服务器端鲁棒聚合方法CRAFT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有损压缩在联邦学习中普遍被视为误差来源，而传统投毒防御主要依赖检查更新几何结构。作者希望转而利用压缩器对输入依赖的失真与载荷行为，作为区分诚实更新与恶意更新的新信号。

Method: 提出压缩足迹（compression footprint）概念，即由有损压缩器诱导的重构、方向、稀疏度与载荷统计构成的低维集合；刻画了压缩足迹可区分诚实与恶意更新的充分条件；并实现服务器端鲁棒聚合方法CRAFT（Compression-guided Robust Aggregation via Footprint Trust），仅使用服务器可验证的足迹，无需客户端元数据与恶意客户端数量先验，且不增加通信开销。

Result: 误差有界有损压缩器足迹的分离能力强于Top-K足迹；在IID客户端数据、36%恶意参与率下，针对6种标准模型投毒攻击、3个数据集、6种鲁棒聚合基线进行评测，CRAFT在18个设置中有7个取得最佳精度，其余设置与最优结果差距在1.7个百分点以内。

Conclusion: 有损压缩既可充当通信机制，也可作为联邦学习鲁棒聚合的安全信号。

Abstract: Lossy compression is widely used in Federated Learning (FL) but is generally treated as an error source, while conventional poisoning defenses inspect update geometry. In this work, we instead treat the compressor's response as a security signal: the input-dependent distortion and payload behavior induced by lossy compression can expose differences between honest and attack-generated updates. We introduce the concept of a \emph{compression footprint}: the low-dimensional collection of reconstruction, directional, sparsity, and payload statistics induced by a lossy compressor. We characterize sufficient conditions under which compression footprints separate honest and malicious updates, and operationalize our findings in the CRAFT (\emph{Compression-guided Robust Aggregation via Footprint Trust}) server-side robust aggregation method. Crucially, under a strict honest-majority assumption, CRAFT uses server-verifiable footprints, requires no client-side metadata nor knowledge of the number of malicious clients, and adds no communication beyond the compressed FL pipeline. Moreover, while CRAFT assumes a strict honest majority, it does not require the number of malicious clients to be known in advance. We observe that error-bounded lossy compressor (EBLC) footprints provide stronger separation than Top-K footprints and that footprint trust suppresses malicious influence. We evaluate CRAFT under IID client data with 36\% malicious participation across six standard model-poisoning attacks, three datasets, and six robust aggregation baselines, finding that CRAFT consistently achieves the best accuracy in 7 out of 18 settings and within 1.7 percentage points of the best in the others. Our results show that lossy compression can serve as both a communication mechanism and a security signal for robust aggregation in FL.

</details>


### [253] [Scaling Laws for Looped Mixture of Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40316)
*Yanbei Chen,Anirudh Goyal,Raghuraman Krishnamoorthi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出首个联合建模循环（recurrence）与稀疏（MoE）的缩放定律 Loop Scaling Laws，能更准确预测循环模型损失，并指导在算力与内存约束下设计循环 MoE 模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环结构与 MoE 稀疏性是两条互补的高效扩展路径：循环在固定参数下增加计算深度，MoE 在固定激活算力下扩展总容量。但现有缩放定律仅孤立建模其一，缺乏统一框架。

Method: 提出 Loop Scaling Laws，联合建模循环、稀疏性、模型规模与数据量；核心是一个有界的、以稀疏性为条件的循环映射，刻画循环带来的有效参数增益以及稀疏性如何提升该增益。

Result: 定律对循环模型留出损失的预测比现有方案更准确，并可将标准稠密与 MoE 缩放定律作为特例复原。下游评估显示：稀疏带来约 3 倍激活参数效率，循环在推理上带来约 2 倍总参数效率，联合扩展进一步提升性能前沿；在万亿 token 规模下，匹配训练算力的循环 MoE 在推理基准上可媲美约 2 倍大的非循环 MoE，并支持通过循环实现测试时扩展。

Conclusion: 循环与稀疏具有互补收益，联合缩放定律为循环 MoE 的设计提供原理性基础，且在超大训练规模下依然有效。

Abstract: Looped transformers and Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) offer complementary routes to efficient scaling: recurrence increases computational depth at fixed parameters, while MoE sparsity expands total capacity at fixed active compute. Yet existing scaling laws model recurrence or sparsity in isolation. In this work, we introduce Loop Scaling Laws, the first scaling law to jointly model recurrence and sparsity alongside model size and data. At its core is a bounded, sparsity-conditional recurrence mapping that characterizes the effective-parameter gain from looping and how sparsity raises this gain. The laws predict the held-out loss of looped models more accurately than prior alternatives, and recover the standard dense and MoE scaling laws as special cases. Beyond prediction, the fitted laws provide a principled foundation for designing looped MoE models under compute and memory constraints. Downstream evaluations further demonstrate the complementary benefits of the two axes: sparsity delivers ~3x active-parameter efficiency, recurrence yields ~2x total-parameter efficiency on reasoning, and joint scaling further advances the performance frontier. As a practical extension, we show these gains hold at trillion-token scale: at matched training compute, a looped MoE with law-derived recurrence matches a ~2x larger non-looped MoE on the reasoning benchmarks, while enabling test-time scaling through recurrence.

</details>


### [254] [Is Weight Tying Still Beneficial for Decoder-Only LLMs in Private Settings Under DP-SGD?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40335)
*Razan El Mais,Ali Chehab,Ibrahim Issa,Razane Tajeddine*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在DP-SGD下训练解码器LLM时，解绑输入输出嵌入（untied embeddings）比权重绑定（weight tying）更优：准确率提升最多4.74个百分点，并因兼容ghost clipping使内存占用降低超60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 权重绑定是解码器LLM常用的参数高效设计，但其在差分隐私训练中的作用尚未被充分研究，作者希望填补这一空白。

Method: 以GPT2和DistilGPT2为代表架构，在DP-SGD下对比权重绑定与解绑嵌入的表现，并分析两者与ghost clipping的兼容性。

Result: 解绑嵌入在SST-2、QNLI、QQP上准确率最高提升4.74个百分点；解绑后可启用内存高效的ghost clipping，而权重绑定因共享参数交互使ghost norm计算复杂化、削弱其计算优势，解绑模型内存占用降低超60%。

Conclusion: 解绑嵌入为差分隐私训练解码器LLM提供了更有效、可扩展的设计，并提示应重新审视隐私保护场景下的标准LLM架构选择。

Abstract: Differentially Private Stochastic Gradient Descent (DP-SGD) is a leading approach for privacy-preserving fine-tuning of large language models (LLMs). Many decoder-only LLMs employ weight tying between input and output embeddings, a design choice originally introduced for parameter efficiency and improved language modeling performance in the non-private setting. However, the impact of weight tying under differentially private training remains largely unexplored. In this work, we investigate the role of weight tying in the DP setting using GPT2 and DistilGPT2 as representative decoder-only architectures. Interestingly, we find that untied embeddings consistently outperform weight-tied models under DP-SGD, achieving gains of up to 4.74% points in accuracy on SST-2, QNLI, and QQP. Beyond improved utility, untying embeddings enables the use of memory-efficient ghost clipping for DP-SGD. By contrast, weight tying introduces shared-parameter interactions that complicate standard ghost norm computation and largely negate its computational advantages. As a result, untied models achieve over 60% lower memory usage while preserving the benefits of ghost clipping. Our results indicate that untied embeddings provide a more effective and scalable design for differentially private training of decoder-only LLMs and highlight the need to revisit standard LLM architectural choices in the privacy-preserving setting.

</details>


### [255] [Removing Timing Shortcuts Improves Non-Invasive Brain-to-Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40359)
*Dulhan Jayalath,Oiwi Parker Jones*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 脑到文本解码的主要性能提升可被无脑数据的"捷径"复现；通过独立处理每个窗口消除该捷径后，简单方法SimpleB2T将词错误率降至36.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年非侵入式脑记录解码单词取得显著进展，但作者怀疑这些提升可能并非源于真正学习脑活动信息，而是方法本身存在可利用的捷径，需要验证并纠正。

Method: 在d'Ascoli等(2025)的工作中，脑活动时间序列以每个单词为起点切分为固定长度窗口并部分重叠，隐含词间间隔（即词时长）。作者先验证在无脑信息的合成信号上能否复现高性能；再将句子中所有窗口联合编码改为每个窗口独立处理，以阻断网络利用时长捷径。

Result: 该方法在无脑信息的合成信号上达到22.0%平衡准确率，与真实脑记录的22.3%相当，证实捷径存在。独立处理后，聚合同一单词的多次神经响应与使用预训练LLM作为语言先验两种策略效果显著提升。在感知语音基准上，SimpleB2T在每词五次观测下词错误率达36.6%。

Conclusion: 研究暴露了脑到文本解码中一个重要的捷径（窗口重叠隐含词时长信息），移除该捷径后催生了一个简单且更有效的解码策略，为脑解码评估的严谨性提供了警示。

Abstract: We find that major reported improvements in decoding words from non-invasive brain recordings are largely reproducible without any brain data. In the influential work of d'Ascoli et al. (2025), time series of brain activity from subjects perceiving continuous speech are segmented into fixed-length windows starting at each word. A neural network then generates predictions for all of the words in a sentence together. Neighbouring windows partially overlap, implicitly revealing the interval between words. Since these intervals indicate the duration of the words spoken, and different words tend to have different durations - for example, "the" is much shorter than "supercalifragilisticexpialidocious" - the neural network can improve its predictions of words without relying on the underlying brain activity. Consistent with this, the method reaches 22.0% balanced accuracy on synthetic signals containing no brain information, compared with 22.3% on real brain recordings. To prevent the network from learning this shortcut, we make a single, simple change. Instead of jointly encoding all windows in a sentence, we process each independently. As a result, the neural network achieves better performance by learning underlying word-specific information from brain recordings. This makes two existing strategies become much more effective than before. Both aggregating predictions from distinct neural responses to the same word and using a pretrained LLM as a linguistic prior now substantially improve results. On our perceived speech benchmark, this simple recipe (SimpleB2T) achieves a word error rate of 36.6% with five observations per word, approaching past invasive speech decoding performance, albeit under different conditions. The results in this work expose an important shortcut in brain-to-text decoding and show that removing it leads to a simple and considerably more effective strategy.

</details>


### [256] [Semifactual Credit-Augmented Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40360)
*Junshu Pan,Zhizhang Fu,Shulin Huang,Yiran Ding,Zifan Cheng,Wenqi Shao,Qiaosheng Zhang,Yue Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究RLVR中LLM对任务无关提示特征的敏感性，提出SCAPO方法，将半事实稳定性纳入token级信用分配，在多个数学基准上超越GRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR虽提升了LLM推理能力，但其预测对任务无关的提示特征仍然敏感。作者发现GRPO将同一结果优势分配给所有响应token，可能强化虚假依赖关系。因此需要更细粒度的信用分配方法来抑制这种由无关提示特征引起的不稳定。

Method: 作者通过"半事实提示干预"（保留问题与答案）分析token级敏感性，发现抑制高漂移token候选可提升准确率。据此提出SCAPO：一种因果启发的GRPO变体，测量固定响应在半事实干预下的token概率漂移，用归一化稳定性分数在训练早期降低不稳定token的优势，同时不对稳定性本身给予额外奖励。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B-Base和Qwen3-1.7B-Base上，SCAPO相较GRPO在AIME 2024-2026上的准确率分别提升5.63和4.17个百分点。在两种模型规模下，SCAPO在多数数学基准及所有分布外基准上均取得最优结果。

Conclusion: 半事实稳定性可作为有效的训练信号，通过RLVR中更细粒度的信用分配来提升推理能力与泛化性能。代码已开源。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has improved the reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs), yet their predictions remain sensitive to task-irrelevant prompt features. We investigate this sensitivity through semifactual prompt interventions that preserve the underlying problem and its answer. Our analysis reveals substantial variation in token-level sensitivity and shows that suppressing high-drift token candidates during decoding improves reasoning accuracy without updating model weights. These findings highlight a limitation of Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), which assigns the same outcome-derived advantage to every response token and may reinforce potential spurious dependence alongside useful reasoning. Motivated by this observation, we introduce Semifactual Credit-Augmented Policy Optimization (SCAPO), a causally inspired variant of GRPO that incorporates semifactual stability into token-level credit assignment. SCAPO measures token probability drift for fixed responses under semifactual interventions and uses normalized stability scores to reduce advantages for relatively unstable tokens during early training, while granting no additional credit for stability alone. On Qwen3-4B-Base and Qwen3-1.7B-Base, SCAPO improves AIME 2024-2026 accuracy over GRPO by 5.63 and 4.17 percentage points, respectively. At both model scales, SCAPO achieves the best results on most evaluated mathematics benchmarks and all evaluated out-of-distribution benchmarks among the compared methods. These results suggest that semifactual stability provides an effective training signal for improving reasoning and generalization through finer-grained credit assignment in RLVR. The code is available at https://github.com/DtYXs/SCAPO.

</details>


### [257] [Ranking-Aware Prompt Optimization for Multimodal Clinical Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40361)
*Tian Xia,Minghao Liu,Yiqing Liang,Laixi Shi,Jiayun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Ranking-PE，将反射式提示演化从“正确率”目标换成“成对排序”目标，使列均值等于 AUROC，从而在类不平衡的临床数据上优化阈值无关的排序指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型在临床诊断中的应用日益广泛，但其适配流程仍以正确率为目标。临床数据严重类别不平衡，恒预测多数类即可获得 90% 以上正确率却毫无临床价值。因此需要以 AUROC 这类与类别分布无关的排序指标来评估与优化。

Method: 本文聚焦提示优化，指出 GEPA 等反射方法使用“实例×候选提示”的正确率矩阵，列均值为正确率并用于选择提示。Ranking-PE 把每一行正确率替换为（正例,负例）实例对上的成对排序行，当候选提示给正例打分高于配对负例时记为 1，此时列均值按 Wilcoxon-Mann-Whitney 恒等式等于经验 AUROC。该替换同时作用于提示演化搜索读取的三个层面：决定 Pareto 支配的分数矩阵、给反射 LM 的逐样本反馈、以及最终候选选择，且不增加模型调用、不使用替代损失。

Result: 在 MIMIC 的三个疾病任务上，基于正确率的提示演化会损害排序性能，Ranking-PE 则逆转这一现象：在微调后的 Qwen3-VL-8B 上比正确率方案高 +5.8 AUROC 个百分点，在 MedGemma-4B 上高 +16.2 个百分点。消融实验逐一检验各设计组件，并表明医学级视觉骨干（通过视觉编码器微调 SFT 或医学预训练）是提示搜索无法替代的前提条件。

Conclusion: 该工作把反射式提示演化从纯文本数据扩展到多模态临床决策，用成对排序替换正确率目标，在不增加模型调用的前提下显著提升类不平衡场景下的 AUROC，并强调医学视觉骨干不可被提示搜索替代。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are rapidly advancing clinical diagnosis, yet their adaptation pipelines remain anchored to accuracy-based objectives. Clinical data are heavily class-imbalanced: a constant-majority predictor can score above 90% accuracy while being clinically useless. We therefore evaluate and optimize for AUROC, a threshold-free score that ranks positives above negatives and is invariant to class balance. We focus on prompt optimization in MLLMs. Reflective methods such as GEPA use a binary scores matrix with one row per evaluation instance and one column per candidate prompt; cells record per-instance correctness, so the column average is accuracy and drives candidate selection. We introduce pair-level Pareto prompt evolution (Ranking-PE), which replaces each correctness row with a pairwise-ordering row over (positive, negative) instance pairs: the cell is 1 if the candidate scores the positive higher than the paired negative. The column average then equals empirical AUROC (by the Wilcoxon-Mann-Whitney identity). We apply this swap at all three layers the prompt evolution search reads from - the scores matrix that decides Pareto dominance, the per-example feedback to the reflection LM, and final candidate selection - at no extra model calls and with no surrogate loss. Across three diseases on MIMIC, accuracy-based prompt evolution can degrade ranking; Ranking-PE reverses this, beating the accuracy-based recipe by +5.8 AUROC pp on fine-tuned Qwen3-VL-8B and +16.2 pp on MedGemma-4B. Ablations examine each design component and show that a medical-grade visual backbone - via vision-encoder-tuned SFT or medical pretraining - is a prerequisite that prompt search cannot replace - our recipe extends reflective prompt evolution from text-only data to multimodal clinical decision-making.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [258] [When Correct Memory Goes Wrong: Fuzzing Persistent Memory Use in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38275)
*Yuqiao Meng,Luoxi Tang,Yingxue Zhang,Yuchen Yang,Zhaohan Xi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该论文将LLM智能体的"记忆误用"失败形式化为模糊测试问题,提出U-Fuzz方法,从记忆检查点出发变异查询或记忆状态,能在多种记忆系统与仅可见输出的设定下发现更多已确认的记忆误用失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续记忆让LLM智能体在长交互中携带信息,但即使记忆内容正确,当查询变化或记忆状态演化时,记忆仍可能被错误使用。现有工作多关注记忆内容错误或基于固定测试用例评估,难以系统性地发现"记忆使用"层面的失败,因此需要一个能主动、系统地挖掘此类失败的方法。

Method: 作者将该问题形式化为模糊测试(fuzzing)问题,把记忆误用失败分为查询相关失败与记忆状态失败两类,并开发了U-Fuzz：以记忆检查点为测试种子,在显式变异义务下变异查询或记忆状态,对每个变异体进行验证,利用观察到的记忆行为引导迭代测试,同时将失败标签排除在搜索过程之外。

Result: 在多个记忆系统上,与多种模糊测试基线对比,U-Fuzz均能一致地发现更多已确认的记忆误用失败;在仅能通过API访问LLM、记忆检索被隐藏的纯输出设定下,该方法同样有效。

Conclusion: U-Fuzz的搜索在不同记忆架构下均保持有效性,甚至在只能观察最终响应的条件下也能发现记忆误用失败,表明将记忆误用视为模糊测试问题是一种可行且通用的系统性检测思路。

Abstract: Persistent memory helps LLM agents carry information across long interactions, but correct memory can still be used incorrectly when queries change or memory states evolve. Existing work mainly studies memory content errors or evaluates fixed test cases, leaving memory-use failures hard to discover systematically. We formulate this issue as a fuzzing problem and categorize such failures into query-related and memory-state failures. We then develop U-Fuzz, which starts from memory checkpoints as test seeds, mutates queries or memory states under explicit mutation obligations, validates each mutant, and uses observed memory behavior to guide iterative testing while keeping failure labels outside the search. We evaluate U-Fuzz across several memory systems against diverse fuzzing baselines, and further test an output-only setting with API-based LLMs where memory retrieval is hidden. Across these settings, U-Fuzz consistently uncovers more confirmed memory-use failures, showing that its search remains effective across different memory architectures and even when only final responses are observable.

</details>


### [259] [Cascadia: A Control-Plane-Free Alternative to Hyperconverged AI Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38697)
*Matias Parij,Pawan Paudel,Tate Berenbaum,Muthaiah Venkatachalam*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Cascadia 是一个面向大规模语言模型推理的分布式服务系统，可在由商用 Intel AI PC 组成的集群上统一利用 CPU、集成 GPU 与 NPU 资源，实现无专用路由控制平面的去中心化推理服务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在商用 Intel AI PC 集群上部署大语言模型推理服务时，传统方案依赖专用路由控制平面与集中式调度，部署复杂、硬件要求高且扩展性受限。作者希望构建一种去中心化、资源异构统一调度、同时支持多种服务模式并提供溯源审计能力的推理系统，以降低部署门槛并提升吞吐。

Method: 系统每个节点内置入口、调度与执行模块，无需专用路由控制平面；节点通过 CA 签发的 ed25519 准入证书加入 libp2p QUIC 网络，广播已签署的能力信息、通过直连对等流交换负载并路由 OpenAI 兼容请求。运维方证书颁发机构在推理路径之外负责准入与集群管理。三种服务模式共用同一接口：单节点整模型执行、负载均衡副本、以及基于配套论文编译与推测解码机制的流水线分片链。可选 KV-cache 迁移在路由切换后复用兼容对话前缀，未命中时冷重算。签名响应凭证与哈希链日志支持溯源审计。

Result: 三节点 Phi-3.5-mini NPU 测试床在十个并发请求下取得单节点配置 3.10 倍响应吞吐；另一四节点部署取得直接单节点服务的 4.06 倍吞吐。通过配对延迟观测、运行时测量与内部功能检查刻画了所测配置；并基于厂商文档，从部署占用、硬件需求、调度、扩展、授权与信任等维度，将 Cascadia 与 IBM、Nutanix、VMware、HPE 平台进行了对比。论文仓库提供基准脚本、整理后的测量数据以及声明到证据的映射。

Conclusion: Cascadia 证明可在商用 Intel AI PC 集群上以去中心化方式高效服务大语言模型，通过多节点流水线与副本调度显著提升吞吐，同时保留溯源审计能力，并在部署与信任等维度上相对主流商用平台具有竞争力。

Abstract: We present Cascadia, a system for serving large language models on fleets of commodity Intel AIPCs using their CPU, integrated-GPU, and NPU resources. Every node embeds ingress, scheduling, and execution; inference requests require no dedicated routing control plane. Nodes join a libp2p QUIC mesh using CA-issued ed25519 admission certificates, gossip signed capabilities, exchange live load over direct peer streams, and route OpenAI-compatible requests to eligible peers. An operator-run certificate authority handles admission and fleet management outside the inference path. Three serving modes share one interface: whole-model execution on one node, load-balanced replicas, and pipeline-sharded chains using the compilation and speculative decoding mechanism of our companion paper. Optional KV-cache mobility reuses compatible conversation prefixes after a routing move, with cold recomputation on a miss. Signed response receipts and hash-chained logs support provenance and audit. A three-node Phi-3.5-mini NPU testbed delivered 3.10x the response throughput of its one-node configuration under ten concurrent requests; a separate four-node deployment recorded 4.06x the throughput of direct single-node serving. Paired latency observations, runtime measurements, and internal functional checks characterize the tested configurations. We compare Cascadia with IBM, Nutanix, VMware, and HPE platforms on deployment footprint, hardware requirements, scheduling, scaling, licensing, and trust, using vendor documentation. The paper repository provides benchmark scripts, curated measurements, and a claim-to-evidence map.

</details>


### [260] [Preserving Provenance in Shared KV Caches for LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38706)
*Wei Song,Yuxin Cao,Xi Zheng,Leo Zhang,Xiao Cheng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 论文首次系统研究LLM服务栈中本地前缀缓存与共享KV缓存之间的"来源盲复用"组合缺口，提出KV来源契约并实现跨引擎防护方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产级LLM服务栈中，推理引擎的本地前缀缓存按适配器、权重配置和共享域区分请求，但共享KV缓存层可能仅按token内容和粗粒度模型元数据建键。这一边界抹除了来源信息，导致相同token在不兼容的计算或共享上下文中发生碰撞，形成安全隐患。作者将此称为"来源盲复用"的组合缺口，并指出此前缺乏系统性研究。

Method: 对三个vLLM连接器进行源码审计以确认结构性缺失；在vLLM和两个SGLang版本、7个家族12个模型（0.5B-32B）、超160种配置上进行运行时实验。形式上提出KV来源契约：对声明的维度注册表，共享键必须在计算和共享来源上单射，且身份跨worker稳定。设计规范描述符将每请求和每worker来源绑定进查找与存储键，并设计差分检查器检测改变KV状态却不改变键的维度。在vLLM和SGLang 0.5.20的三条缓存路径上实现。

Result: 跨适配器碰撞使准确率从0.94降至0.64；不兼容KV表示使推理准确率降为零；盐值缺失导致93%的提示可通过时序识别。来源绑定消除了不安全复用，同时保留合法共享。命中路径延迟变化在0.34ms以内且低于运行间波动；保留量随来源多样性增长。

Conclusion: 本地与共享KV缓存间的来源盲复用是真实且广泛存在的安全缺口，跨引擎、跨模型、跨配置均可复现。通过KV来源契约与规范描述符绑定，任何具有稳定身份的维度都可在无需连接器特定键逻辑的情况下被添加，从而实现安全且高效的复用。

Abstract: Production LLM serving stacks combine an inference engine's local prefix cache with a shared KV-cache tier for fleet-wide reuse. The local cache distinguishes requests by adapter, weight configuration and sharing domain, but the shared tier may key entries only by token content and coarse model metadata. This boundary erases provenance and lets identical tokens under incompatible computational or sharing contexts collide. We call this composition gap provenance-blind reuse and present its first systematic study. A source audit of three vLLM connectors confirms the structural omission, while runtime experiments reproduce it across vLLM and two SGLang releases, 12 models from 7 families (0.5 B-32 B), and over 160 configurations. Cross-adapter collisions reduce accuracy from 0.94 to 0.64, incompatible KV representations reduce reasoning accuracy to zero, and salt omission enables 93% prompt identification from timing. We formalize the missing guarantee as the KV provenance contract: for a declared dimension registry, shared keys must be injective over computational and sharing provenance, with identities stable across workers. Any dimension with a stable identity can therefore be added without connector-specific key logic. A canonical descriptor binds per-request and per-worker provenance into lookup and store keys, while a differential checker detects dimensions that change KV state without changing the key. Implemented in vLLM and SGLang 0.5.20 across three cache paths, provenance binding eliminates unsafe reuse while preserving legitimate sharing. Hit-path latency changes remain within 0.34 ms and below run-to-run variation; retention grows with provenance diversity.

</details>


### [261] [Towards Efficient HPC Systems for Agents: Challenges and Opportunities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38723)
*Yunjia Zheng,Bintang Dwi Marthen,Zachary Pan,Minghao Li,Raminder Singh,Manasvita Joshi,Minlan Yu,Juncheng Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 编码智能体已成为HPC系统的真实用户，但其行为模式与人类差异巨大，现有HPC抽象与策略亟需面向智能体重构，论文主张将智能体视为一等公民进行协同设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HPC抽象、接口与策略是为人类驱动的工作流设计的，而编码智能体已大规模进入HPC系统。测量显示智能体用户仅占观测人群的19.5%，却贡献了55.8%的作业提交、29.1%的CPU核心时和42.7%的GPU时。智能体并非"更快的人类"，其高频命令、细粒度循环和开放式试错行为给调度器控制面、元数据密集型I/O、知识复用以及提示注入与策略执行等带来压力，禁止或当作普通用户都不可持续，因此需要重新思考HPC与智能体的关系。

Method: 通过大规模测量刻画编码智能体在HPC系统中的占比与行为特征，并据此分析其对现有抽象与策略造成的压力面。在此基础上提出"协同设计"思路：将智能体视为设施感知的一等租户，并梳理在计算、存储、智能体记忆与安全四个方向上的挑战与机遇。

Result: 测量结果表明智能体虽仅占用户总数19.5%，却产生55.8%的作业提交、29.1%的CPU核心时和42.7%的GPU时；其命令速率是人类20.8倍，采用细粒度探索—修改—执行循环，并以开放目标驱动持续试错，跨昼夜与周末运行，暴露出控制面压力、元数据密集型I/O、重复发现已有知识以及新的提示注入与策略执行面等问题。

Conclusion: 既不应禁止智能体，也不应将其当作普通用户；HPC设施应与智能体协同设计，把智能体作为一等主体和设施感知租户，围绕计算、存储、智能体记忆与安全展开系统性重构。

Abstract: Coding agents have become real users of high-performance computing (HPC) systems, yet today's HPC abstractions, interfaces, and policies remain designed for human-driven workflows. In our measurement, users running coding agents are only 19.5% of the observed population, but account for 55.8% of job submissions, 29.1% of CPU core-hours, and 42.7% of GPU-hours. Agents are not simply faster humans. They issue commands at 20.8x the human rate, decompose work into fine-grained explore-modify-execute loops, and pursue open-ended goals through trial-and-error campaigns that continue through nights and weekends. These behaviors strain abstractions built for human timescales, surfacing as control-plane pressure on the scheduler, metadata-intensive I/O on bandwidth-provisioned filesystems, repeated rediscovery of what earlier sessions already learned, and new prompt-injection and policy-enforcement surfaces. Neither banning agents nor treating them as ordinary users is sustainable. We instead argue for co-design, that facilities should treat agents as first-class principals where agents become facility-aware tenants. We outline the resulting challenges and opportunities in compute, storage, agent memory, and safety.

</details>


### [262] [Vosti: Specifying, Implementing, and Verifying Deterministic LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38981)
*Jianxing Qin,Alexander Du,Danfeng Zhang,Matthew Lentz,Danyang Zhuo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文形式化定义了确定性LLM推理（固定模型与部署配置下，相同提示与会话状态的请求在对应输出位置产生逐位一致的logits），发现vLLM和SGLang的生产模式在某些执行变化下仍产生不同logits，并提出经形式化验证的推理引擎Vosti来满足该规约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理系统在批组合、提示分块、prefill/decode执行、KV缓存复用/驱逐/重算等方面存在多种优化，这些优化不应影响系统输出。现有生产系统（如vLLM的batch-invariant模式、SGLang的deterministic模式）虽有此目标，但缺乏形式化的系统级规约，且实测在某些执行变化下仍会产生不同的logits。

Method: 作者形式化定义了确定性LLM推理规约；设计并验证了推理引擎Vosti：其内核选择独立于运行时引擎状态，并将缓存的KV值绑定到其逻辑token前缀；证明在引擎/GPU内核边界处分解——用Verus归纳证明调度与分页、前缀共享的KV缓存保持输出logits不变，用Triton分析器证明所选内核在不同批、查询长度和分页KV缓存布局下输出逐位一致。

Result: 测试发现vLLM和SGLang的生产模式在某些执行变化下会产生不同的logits。Vosti在所有测试的执行变化下均产生逐位一致的logits，在decode密集型负载上性能与vLLM的batch-invariant模式相当，并提供了更强、经形式化验证的确定性保证。

Conclusion: Vosti通过形式化验证实现了系统级的确定性LLM推理，在保持与vLLM batch-invariant模式相当性能的同时，提供了更强的逐位一致性保证。

Abstract: LLM inference systems may vary batch composition, prompt chunking, prefill/decode execution, and KV-cache reuse, eviction, or recomputation. These optimizations should not affect system outputs. Production systems, including vLLM's batch-invariant mode and SGLang's deterministic mode, target this goal but lack a formal system-level specification.
  We formalize deterministic LLM inference: under a fixed model and deployment configuration, requests with the same prompt and initial sampler state produce bitwise-identical logits at corresponding output positions across executions. Our tests find that these production modes produce different logits under some execution variations. To address this limitation, we present Vosti, an inference engine designed and verified against this specification. Vosti chooses kernels independently of runtime engine state and ties cached KV values to their logical token prefixes. Its proof decomposes at the engine/GPU kernel boundary: a Verus inductive proof establishes that scheduling and the paged, prefix-sharing KV-cache preserve output logits, while a Triton analyzer proves bitwise-equal selected kernel outputs across batches, query lengths, and paged KV-cache layouts. Vosti produces bitwise-identical logits across every tested execution variation and achieves performance comparable to vLLM's batch-invariant mode on decode-heavy workloads, while providing a stronger, formally verified determinism guarantee.

</details>


### [263] [Taming Speculative Search for Test-Time Scaling in LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39334)
*Jinwoo Jeong,Woohyung Choi,Myeongjae Jeon,Jeongseob Ahn*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: SpecScale：面向LLM推理的推测执行服务系统，通过剪枝、去重、延迟验证三项技术，同时提升吞吐并降低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时缩放通过在推理阶段增加计算来提升LLM在数学、编程等难题上的准确率，但探索推理路径的开销巨大。近期研究提出推测执行来加速路径探索，然而该方式给LLM服务系统带来两大独特挑战：(1)候选路径搜索空间爆炸；(2)对候选的频繁、细粒度验证任务。

Method: 提出SpecScale服务系统，用三项技术权衡延迟与计算开销：(1)对低质量候选路径进行早期剪枝；(2)对冗余候选路径的计算去重；(3)将细粒度验证任务延后执行。

Result: 在MATH、Olympiad等挑战性推理基准上评估，SpecScale显著优于非推测执行方法及近期推测执行方法，在保持答案质量的同时大幅提升吞吐、降低延迟。

Conclusion: 推测执行用于LLM推理服务需解决搜索空间爆炸与细粒度验证两大问题，SpecScale通过剪枝、去重与验证延迟有效缓解，实现了吞吐与延迟的兼顾。

Abstract: Test-time scaling has recently emerged as a powerful approach for improving LLM reasoning by allocating additional computation during inference, substantially enhancing accuracy on challenging tasks such as mathematics and coding. To accelerate the exploration of reasoning paths, recent studies proposed speculative execution. However, we show that supporting speculative execution poses two unique challenges for LLM serving systems: (1) an explosion in the search space of candidate paths and (2) frequent, fine-grained verification tasks for candidates.
  To address these challenges, this paper proposes SpecScale, a serving system for efficient speculative execution. We introduce three techniques to reconcile the trade-off between latency and computational overhead: (1) early pruning of low-quality candidate paths, (2) deduplicating computation across redundant candidate paths, and (3) deferring fine-grained verification tasks. We evaluate SpecScale on challenging reasoning benchmarks, including MATH and Olympiad. Our results show that SpecScale significantly outperforms both non-speculative and recent speculative approaches, delivering substantial improvements in throughput and latency while preserving answer quality.

</details>


### [264] [HAPMoE: Heterogeneity-Aware Automatic Parallelism Planning for Mixture-of-Experts Models Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39350)
*Mengyuan Fan,Peizhuang Cong,Zixiao Huang,Si Xu,Tong Qiao,Yanghao Li,Jing Yang,Tong Yang,Quanlu Zhang,Yu Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: HAPMoE是一个面向MoE训练的异构感知自动并行规划器，通过轻量级成本模型与六维并行空间搜索，生成可直接在Megatron-LM部署的并行方案，在异构集群上实现最高3.2倍端到端训练吞吐提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着模型规模扩大，分布式训练不可避免；MoE架构逐渐成为主流，且加速器硬件快速演进使集群异构性普遍存在。然而现有自动并行方法通常只针对MoE架构或异构集群之一，无法应对两者共存的场景。

Method: 构建轻量级且感知MoE的成本模型，并高效搜索六维并行空间，直接输出可在Megatron-LM上部署的并行方案；采用非均匀流水线划分与剪枝增强的动态规划算法加速搜索。

Result: 相比基线，端到端训练吞吐在异构集群上最高提升3.2倍；非均匀流水线划分额外带来最高78%的收益；剪枝增强的动态规划算法可在1分钟内完成搜索。

Conclusion: HAPMoE在MoE与异构集群共存的复杂硬件环境下兼具高效性与实用价值，能有效解决现有方法难以泛化的自动并行化难题。

Abstract: As model sizes continue to scale, distributed training has become inevitable. Automatic parallelization techniques can derive efficient training parallelism strategies at low cost while achieving superior performance. The difficulty of this problem is jointly determined by the complexity of the model and the underlying compute cluster. Meanwhile, mixture-of-experts (MoE) models are increasingly emerging as the dominant architecture and the rapid evolution of accelerator hardware has made cluster heterogeneity commonplace, posing substantial challenges to automatic parallelization. However, existing approaches typically target either MoE architectures or heterogeneous clusters, failing to generalize to scenarios where both challenges coexist. To this end, we present HAPMoE, a heterogeneity-aware automatic parallelism planner for MoE training. HAPMoE builds a lightweight MoE-aware cost model and efficiently searches a six-dimensional parallel space, producing parallel plans directly deployable on Megatron-LM. Experiments show that HAPMoE improves end-to-end training throughput by up to 3.2$\times$ over baselines across heterogeneous clusters. Its non-uniform pipeline partitioning yields an additional up to 78% gains, and its pruning-enhanced dynamic programming algorithm completes the search within 1 minute, demonstrating high efficiency and practical value in complex hardware environments.

</details>


### [265] [Communication-Efficient $(1+\varepsilon)Δ$-Edge Coloring and Lovász Local Lemma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39359)
*Yi-Jun Chang,Nima Dolatabadi,Hung Thuan Nguyen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文在双边边划分模型下研究边染色问题，同时降低颜色数与通信量，提出使用 $(1+\varepsilon)\Delta$ 种颜色、期望 $O(ne^{-\gamma\Delta}+1)$ 比特通信的公开硬币 Las Vegas 协议，并发展了通信高效的双边构造性 Lovász 局部引理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前工作给出确定性 $(2\Delta-1)$ 边染色协议，使用 $O(n)$ 比特通信，但能否高效地减少颜色数仍悬而未决。本文旨在同时减少颜色数量和通信开销。

Method: 核心是一种新的随机染色过程，使 Alice 与 Bob 能在基本相同的颜色空间中染色，仅需少量协调，从而保留大部分私有随机选择。为使该过程成功，作者构造了一个通信高效的双边构造性 Lovász 局部引理（LLL）。

Result: 对任意固定 $\varepsilon>0$ 及足够大的 $\Delta$，给出公开硬币 Las Vegas 协议，实现 $(1+\varepsilon)\Delta$ 边染色，期望使用 $O(ne^{-\gamma\Delta}+1)$ 比特、$O(\log n/\Delta+1)$ 轮，其中 $\gamma$ 仅依赖 $\varepsilon$。当 $\Delta=\omega(1)$ 时期望通信为 $o(n)$；当 $\Delta\ge C_\varepsilon\log n$ 时为 $O(1)$。仅用私有硬币会增加 $O(\log n)$ 期望比特。

Conclusion: 该协议在颜色数与通信量上均优于此前工作。所提出的双边构造性 LLL 具有独立价值，可应用于若干经典问题的标准 LLL 形式，得到通信高效的双边协议。

Abstract: We study edge coloring in the two-party edge-partition model, where Alice and Bob each know part of the edge set and must jointly produce a proper coloring with little communication. Previous work gave a deterministic $(2Δ-1)$-edge-coloring protocol using $O(n)$ bits, leaving open whether fewer colors can be obtained efficiently.
  We simultaneously reduce both the number of colors and the communication. For every fixed $\varepsilon>0$ and all sufficiently large $Δ$, we give a public-coin Las Vegas protocol that finds a proper $(1+\varepsilon)Δ$-edge coloring using $O(ne^{-γΔ} + 1)$ expected bits and $O\left(\frac{\log n}Δ+1\right)$ expected rounds, where $γ>0$ depends only on $\varepsilon$. Thus, the expected communication is $o(n)$ when $Δ=ω(1)$ and $O(1)$ when $Δ\ge C_\varepsilon\log n$, for a sufficiently large constant $C_\varepsilon$. Using only private coins adds $O(\log n)$ expected bits.
  Our key idea is a new randomized coloring procedure that allows Alice and Bob to color their edges using essentially the same color space with only a small amount of coordination, so most of their random choices remain private. To make this procedure succeed, we develop a communication-efficient constructive Lovász local lemma (LLL) for two parties.
  Our two-party constructive LLL is also of independent interest. We illustrate its broader applicability by applying it to standard LLL formulations of several other classical problems, obtaining communication-efficient two-party protocols.

</details>


### [266] [FissionReady: Joint Workload and Power Scheduling for Data Centers Powered by Small Modular Reactors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39422)
*Raghavendra Kanakagiri,Rohan Basu Roy,Yankai Jiang,Pranathi Wuppuluru,Devesh Tiwari*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出 FissionReady，一个针对小型模块化核反应堆（SMR）供电数据中心的调度器，通过跟踪各模块燃料年龄、氙状态和剩余灵活性来确定运行包络，并采用双时间尺度优化协调负荷跟随、批处理推迟和电网购电，实现零非自愿停机、零批处理截止违约，同时降低约三分之一水耗和约一半电网成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据中心日益探索以 SMR 作为无碳电源，但数据中心负荷波动及负电价要求核电站进行负荷跟随而非恒定输出。由于核物理特性，SMR 的负荷跟随尤为困难复杂，现有调度方案难以应对这一挑战。

Method: 提出 FissionReady 数据中心调度器：跟踪每个反应堆模块的燃料年龄、氙状态和剩余灵活性，据此确定各模块的运行包络；通过双时间尺度优化协调负荷跟随、批处理任务推迟以及电网购电。

Result: 与同等规模的基础配置相比，FissionReady 实现零非自愿停机与零批处理截止违约，同时将水消耗降低约三分之一，电网成本降低约一半。

Conclusion: FissionReady 表明，通过细粒度跟踪核模块状态并协同调度计算负荷与电网交互，可在保证核电站安全运行约束的前提下显著提升数据中心的经济性与可持续性。

Abstract: Data centers are increasingly exploring small modular nuclear reactors (SMRs) as a carbon-free power source, but variable datacenter demand and negative grid prices require the SMR plant to load-follow rather than run at constant output. Load following is uniquely challenging and complex for SMR plants due to underlying nuclear physics. We propose FissionReady, a datacenter scheduler that tracks each module's fuel age, xenon state, and remaining flexibility to determine per-module operating envelopes, then coordinates load-following, batch deferral, and grid purchasing through a two-timescale optimization. FissionReady achieves zero involuntary shutdowns and zero batch deadline misses while reducing water consumption by roughly one-third and grid cost by roughly half compared to a same-sized base configuration.

</details>


### [267] [About the Influence of Workflow Topology on Task Intensity Prediction through Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39481)
*Max Otto,Haci Ismail Aslan,Joel Witzke,Jonathan Bader,Odej Kao*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文通过全面基准测试，系统量化了DAG工作流拓扑结构对任务级资源强度预测的影响，发现拓扑是关键特征，图原生模型精度最高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云上大规模工作流的资源供给是性能工程难题：DAG结构下资源不足会造成瓶颈，过度供给则增加成本。准确的任务级资源强度（CPU、内存）预测至关重要，但现有方法多依赖任务级特征，往往忽略或仅假设工作流整体拓扑结构的影响，其作用程度与建模方式尚不明确。

Method: 作者构建了一个综合基准，系统量化图拓扑对任务强度预测的影响，并评估比较了一系列建模方法，包括基线模型、融入简单手工拓扑特征的模型，以及图原生（graph-native）模型，并对两类特征进行组合。

Result: 拓扑是准确预测的关键特征。引入重要拓扑信息的模型（即便是简单手工特征）显著优于基线模型；图原生模型精度最高，在CPU与内存预测上均取得较低平均绝对误差，且可与它们未学到的简单拓扑特征结合以进一步提升性能。

Conclusion: DAG拓扑结构对任务级资源强度预测具有重要影响，不应被忽略；建模时采用图原生方法并结合手工拓扑特征是当前最优策略，可为云工作流的高效资源供给提供依据。

Abstract: Efficient resource provisioning for large-scale workflows on cloud infrastructures is a critical performance engineering challenge. These workflows are often structured as directed acyclic graphs (DAGs), where under-provisioning can cause critical bottlenecks and over-provisioning leads to unnecessary costs. Accurate, task-level prediction of resource intensity (e.g., CPU load and memory usage) is essential for mitigating these issues. While task-level features are commonly used for prediction, the performance impact of the workflow's overall topological structure is often overlooked or assumed. The central question of our work is: To what extent does what part of the DAG topology influence task-level resource intensity, and what is the most effective way to model this influence?
  This paper presents a comprehensive benchmark to systematically quantify the impact of graph topology on task intensity prediction. We evaluate and compare a spectrum of modeling approaches. Our findings demonstrate that topology is a critical feature for accurate prediction. Models incorporating important topological information, even through simple handcrafted features, significantly outperform baseline models. We show that graph-native models provide the highest accuracy, achieving low mean absolute errors for both CPU and memory predictions, and can still be combined with simple topological features that they do not learn for better performance.

</details>


### [268] [Kirin: Cloud-native WebAssembly Service Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39631)
*Joshua Bauer,Sebastian Werner,Maria C. Borges*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出 Kirin，一个面向云原生 WebAssembly 服务的编排器，支持资源调度、生命周期管理、扩缩容与请求路由，并与基于容器的部署进行对比评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容器虽因编排快速而成为主流部署原语，但仍带来不可忽视的开销；WebAssembly 作为轻量级隔离技术，因兼容性限制此前主要应用于 FaaS 和边缘计算。随着 Wasm 生态成熟，需探讨其能否替代容器用于云原生工作负载。

Method: 设计并实现 Kirin——云原生 WebAssembly 服务编排器，涵盖核心编排职责：资源调度、生命周期管理、扩缩容和请求路由；针对多种服务工作负载，将 Kirin 与基于容器的部署进行对比评估。

Result: 评估结果显示 Kirin 表现良好，尤其在服务生命周期管理方面优势明显；同时验证了其在 CPU 密集型工作负载上的已知局限，但对于典型服务工作负载，Kirin 展现出有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 对于典型服务工作负载，WebAssembly 有望作为云原生部署技术被更广泛采用，是容器的可行候选替代方案。

Abstract: Modern cloud computing infrastructure relies heavily on virtualization to provide workload isolation and resource efficiency. While containers have become the dominant deployment primitive due to their fast orchestration compared to traditional virtual machines, they still introduce non-trivial overhead. WebAssembly (Wasm) has emerged as an alternative isolation technology, offering a lightweight execution model. Because of compatibility limitations, WebAssembly has mainly been applied to Function-as-a-Service (FaaS) and Edge Computing scenarios, where it has attracted growing research interest.
  However, recent advances in the WebAssembly ecosystem have significantly matured the technology, raising the question of whether it can serve as a viable replacement for containers in cloud-native workloads. In this paper, we present Kirin, an orchestrator for cloud-native WebAssembly services. It supports core orchestration responsibilities, including resource scheduling, lifecycle management, scaling, and request routing. We evaluate Kirin against container-based deployments for several service workloads and demonstrate promising results, particularly in service lifecycle management. Simultaneously, we confirm known limitations in CPU-intensive workloads. However, for typical service workloads, Kirin demonstrates competitive performance, suggesting that WebAssembly is a viable candidate for broader adoption as a cloud-native deployment technology.

</details>


### [269] [Exploring Adaptive Byzantine Quorum Systems to Improve Latency in the WAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39715)
*Linus Gnan,Rüdiger Kapitza,Christian Berger*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文通过为BFT-SMaRt扩展模块化quorum system抽象，将多种quorum构造置于同一框架并共享相同优化技术，从而在广域BFT复制中公平比较quorum设计对客户端延迟的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在广域网络中，quorum的大小与组成决定副本达成进展的速度，进而影响客户端感知延迟。虽已有threshold、weighted、grid等多种quorum构造，但文献缺乏公平对比：每种构造通常嵌入不同协议实现，quorum设计对延迟的影响难以与实现差异（尤其优化技术）分离。

Method: 作者为BFT-SMaRt扩展了一个模块化quorum-system抽象，使多种quorum构造可在同一框架内共存，并与同一组优化技术交互；随后借助网络仿真，在广域BFT复制场景中考察不同quorum system构造对客户端延迟的影响。

Result: 通过统一框架下的网络仿真，实现了对多种quorum构造在相同优化条件下的可比较评估，分离出quorum设计本身对客户端延迟的影响。

Conclusion: 模块化quorum抽象为公平比较quorum构造提供了可行途径，有助于厘清quorum设计对广域BFT复制客户端延迟的作用。

Abstract: Quorum systems enforce strict consistency in Byzantine fault-tolerant (BFT) state machine replication: Before a value is decided, a subset of replicas (called quorum) must exchange votes for the value. In wide-area networks, the size and composition of a quorum determines the speed at which replicas can make progress and thus impacts the latency perceived by clients. A variety of quorum constructions has been proposed, e.g., threshold, weighted, grid and others, but the literature offers little in the way of an apples-to-apples comparison. Each construction typically lives inside a different protocol implementation, so the influence of quorum design on client latency cannot be easily separated from other implementation effects, especially the use of optimization techniques. In this paper, we close that gap by extending BFT-SMaRt with a modular quorum-system abstraction that allows a variety of quorum constructions to live inside the same framework and interact with the same set of optimization techniques. Via our framework, we explore the impact of different quorum system constructions on client-observed latency in wide-area BFT replication using network simulation.

</details>


### [270] [Darpan: A Digital Twin Framework for the Next-Generation Computing Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39799)
*Zhiyu Wang,Rajkumar Buyya*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出数字孪生框架 Darpan，通过物理侧实时观测与数字侧可执行虚拟副本的持续同步，在真实应用当前状态下评估候选决策并验证可行性，从而降低计算连续体中的决策失效风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算连续体应用跨设备、边缘、雾和云分布工作负载，在放置、调度或恢复决策制定期间，资源可用性、网络条件和应用进度可能发生变化，导致决策在执行时已失效。现有运行时只能执行预定决策，仿真工具只能在预配置环境中比较方案，无法从运行中应用的当前状态直接探索替代结果。

Method: Darpan 支持物理与数字双重执行：物理侧运行真实应用并持续观测其运行时状态；数字侧基于物理观测维护一个持续更新、可执行的虚拟副本。Darpan 从同一初始点独立评估候选决策，并将选定的决策返回物理侧，在执行前依据最新物理状态验证其可行性。

Result: 在真实 DAG 工作负载上，Darpan 预测物理响应时间的平均绝对误差为 0.485 秒，在 40 节点时保持 93.1% 的扩展效率。在状态变化试验中，从状态捕获到拒绝失效决策平均约需 2 毫秒，可在数据传输前停止请求，避免不必要的物理执行开销。在相同物理 DAG 预算下，Darpan 生成的经验使较弱的 PPO 调度器分别超越两个最先进的 DRL 调度器 29.1% 和 20.2%。

Conclusion: Darpan 通过数字孪生实现物理与数字执行的协同，能够准确预测响应时间、快速应对状态变化、避免无效执行的物理开销，并能以较低成本生成有效经验来提升调度器性能，为计算连续体中的动态决策提供了一种可行方案。

Abstract: Computing-continuum applications distribute work across devices, edge systems, fog resources, and clouds. While a placement, scheduling, or recovery decision is being made, resource availability, network conditions, and application progress may change, so the decision can be invalid by the time it is executed. Existing runtimes enact predetermined decisions, whereas simulation tools compare alternatives in preconfigured environments; neither explores alternative outcomes directly from the current state of a running application. We propose a Digital Twin framework, called Darpan, that supports both physical and digital execution: the physical side runs real applications and continuously observes their runtime state, while the digital side maintains a continuously updated, executable virtual counterpart based on these physical observations. Darpan evaluates candidate decisions independently from the same starting point and returns the selected decision to the physical side, where its feasibility is validated against the latest physical state before actual execution. Across real Directed Acyclic Graph (DAG) workloads, Darpan predicts physical response time with a mean absolute error of 0.485 s and retains 93.1% scale-out efficiency at 40 nodes. In the state-change trials, Darpan takes about 2 ms on average from state capture to rejection of an invalidated decision, stopping the request before data transfer and avoiding unnecessary physical execution overhead. Under the same physical-DAG budget, Darpan-generated experience enables a weaker Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) scheduler to outperform two state-of-the-art Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) schedulers by 29.1% and 20.2%.

</details>


### [271] [From Pilots to Production: Lessons in Cross-Institutional Federated Training and Artificial Intelligence for Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39803)
*Olivera Kotevska,Max Carlson,Yan Gao,Francis Jeanson,Yijiang Li,William Lindskog,Mohammad Naseri,Minseok Ryu,Sahil Tyagi,Jerry Watkins,Feiyi Wang,Ravi Madduri,Kibaek Kim*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该文综合美国能源部国家实验室、产业部署与开源社区经验，提出将联邦AI从试点推向多站点可靠生产的结构化路径，围绕五域就绪框架与分层技术栈展开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多高价值科学数据集因专有、出口管制、保密或数据主权限制而无法集中，因此必须让模型移动到数据端，使联邦AI成为开放科学的核心基础设施。

Method: 整合五个并行实践的经验，涵盖领导级超算与受监管环境中的云基础设施；采用改编的五域就绪框架，并对已训练模型之下的技术栈进行分层（数据架构、隐私与安全控制、信任与验证、治理与社会技术因素、运维）。

Result: 总结了内存效率、可靠性与同步方面的系统性经验；阐明生产环境中隐私、安全与去中心化信任的要求及尚未验证之处；识别出异步联邦、泄露审计、验证标准与统一数据契约等跨领域开放问题。

Conclusion: 研究问题已从“能否训练”转向“能否安全地运维、审计与变更”；作者主张设立专门的国际开放科学工作组来推进上述开放问题。

Abstract: Many of the most valuable scientific datasets cannot be centralized: they are proprietary, export-controlled, classified, or bound by data-sovereignty restrictions. This inverts the usual paradigm: the model must move to the data, making federated artificial intelligence (AI) core infrastructure for open science. We synthesize lessons from U.S. Department of Energy national laboratories, industry deployments, and the open-source community into a structured account of moving federated AI from pilot demonstrations to dependable, multi-site production. The lessons come from five concurrent efforts spanning leadership-class supercomputers and cloud infrastructure in regulated settings. We organize them around an adapted \textit{five-domain readiness frame} and a stratified view of the stack beneath a trained model: data architecture, privacy and security controls, trust and verification, governance and socio-technical factors, and operations, the layers that decide whether a pilot becomes dependable. The question shifts from ``can we train it?'' to ``can we operate it, audit it, and change it safely?'' We report systems lessons in memory efficiency, reliability, and synchronization; set out what privacy, security, and decentralized trust require in production, and what is not yet validated there; and identify cross-cutting open problems (asynchronous federation, leakage auditing, verification standards, and harmonized data contracts) that we argue warrant a dedicated, international, open-science working group.

</details>


### [272] [EPR Count for Runtime Prediction in Distributed Quantum Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39851)
*Fatih E. Bilgen,Ozgur B. Akan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了分布式量子计算中EPR消耗量与运行时间的关系，发现二者相关但不等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EPR对消耗常被用作分布式量子计算中的通信代价目标，但最小化EPR代价未必能最小化分布式执行时间。尽管EPR数量被广泛用作运行时间的代理指标，其可靠性尚未在不同负载和通信条件下得到直接的、系统的刻画。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 系统地评估EPR代价与运行时间之间的关系，覆盖QFT、QAOA和CDKM电路在所有平衡双QPU映射下的表现。

Result: EPR代价相同的映射执行时间可能差异显著；EPR更低的映射可能更慢；最小EPR映射在运行时间上可能非最优。EPR数量的可靠性强烈依赖工作负载，并随通信运行区间变化：EPR生成延迟增大会放大被相同EPR代价掩盖的运行时间差异，而通信并发度提高会削弱EPR数量保持运行时间排序的能力。

Conclusion: EPR数量与执行时间是相关但不同的优化目标，研究识别出必须进行显式通信感知运行时间建模的区间。

Abstract: EPR-pair consumption is commonly used as a communication-cost objective in distributed quantum computing, but minimizing EPR cost does not necessarily minimize distributed execution time. Despite its widespread use, the reliability of EPR count as a runtime surrogate has received limited direct characterization across different workloads and communication conditions. This work addresses this gap by systematically evaluating the relationship between EPR cost and runtime over all balanced two-QPU mappings of QFT, QAOA, and CDKM circuits. The results show that mappings with identical EPR cost can have substantially different execution times, lower-EPR mappings can be slower, and minimum-EPR mappings can be runtime-suboptimal. The reliability of EPR count is strongly workload dependent and also changes with the communication operating regime: increasing EPR-generation latency amplifies runtime differences hidden by equal EPR cost, while increased communication concurrency can reduce the ability of EPR count to preserve runtime ordering. These results show that EPR count and execution time are related but distinct optimization objectives, and identify regimes in which explicit communication-aware runtime modeling is necessary.

</details>


### [273] [LatencyLab: A DPDK-Based P4 Pipeline Latency Measurement Framework for FPGA SmartNICs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39978)
*Pavani Kuppili,Zhaoyang Han,Yicheng Qian,Suranga Handagala,Michael Zink,Miriam Leeser,Robert Ricci*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出 LatencyLab，一个无需 PHC/PTP 或时钟同步、基于 DPDK 的 FPGA P4 流水线延迟测量框架，并在 AMD Alveo U280 上验证了其时延测量的紧致性与可复现性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可编程 FPGA SmartNIC 将报文处理直接置于线路上，但现有开放 FPGA P4 工具流未在流水线边界暴露时间戳，导致 P4 程序在目标 FPGA 上引入的延迟很少被实际测量。

Method: 利用 FPGA 两个端口共享同一网段，交换机将每个探测包组播复制到两条路径：一条经 VitisNetP4 流水线，另一条经匹配的旁路路径；内核旁路 DPDK 接收端轮询两端口并在从网卡接收环形缓冲区取出时用 CPU TSC 打时间戳，通过两份副本到达时间差隔离流水线延迟，并用空比特流校准；在 AMD Alveo U280 上对四个 VitisNetP4 程序评估，每个用 20000 包轨迹、每会话测 10 次、共 5 个独立会话。

Result: 延迟分布紧致且可复现：99% 报文落在中位数 20 ns 内，会话中位数重复性在 1–2 ns 内（250 MHz 下 FiveTuple 107/137 ns、Forward 149 ns、RemoveHeader 177 ns、Checksum 364 ns）；无内核反射器将探测对返回 ConnectX-5 网卡，其适配器时钟逐分位复现测量分布（数纳秒内）；每个被测报文比厂商最坏情况延迟界低 18–22 个时钟周期。

Conclusion: LatencyLab 提供了一种无需 PHC/PTP 支持的 FPGA P4 流水线延迟测量方案，测量结果紧致可复现，并经两组独立校验与厂商最坏情况界一致，验证了框架的有效性。

Abstract: P4-programmable FPGA SmartNICs place packet processing directly on the wire, but open FPGA P4 toolflows do not expose timestamping at the pipeline boundary, so the latency a P4 program adds on the target FPGA is rarely measured. This paper presents LatencyLab, a DPDK-based measurement framework for FPGA P4 pipeline latency that needs neither PHC/PTP support on the datapath nor clock synchronization. The FPGA's two ports share a network segment, so the switch multicasts a copy of each probe packet to both: one copy passes through the VitisNetP4 pipeline, the other through a matched bypass path. A kernel-bypass DPDK receiver busy-polls both ports and timestamps every packet with the CPU timestamp counter (TSC) as it is retrieved from the NIC's receive circular buffer. The arrival-time difference of the two copies isolates the pipeline latency after calibration against a null bitstream carrying the same traffic; transmit time cancel in the subtraction. We evaluate four VitisNetP4 programs on an AMD Alveo U280, probing each with a 20,000-packet trace measured ten times per session over five independent sessions, all TSC-timestamped and reflected for hardware timestamping. The measured latency distributions are tight and reproducible: 99% of packets fall within 20 ns of the median, session medians repeating within 1 to 2 ns (FiveTuple 107/137 ns, Forward 149 ns, RemoveHeader 177 ns, Checksum 364 ns at 250 MHz). Two independent checks agree with the framework: a kernel-free reflector returns every probe pair to a ConnectX-5 NIC whose adapter clock reproduces the measured distributions within a few nanoseconds, quantile by quantile, and every measured packet falls 18 to 22 clock cycles below the vendor's worst-case latency bound.

</details>


### [274] [Efficient Expert-Parallel Communication on PCIe-Connected Consumer GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40093)
*Jaehwan Lee,Sangmin Lee,Chaewon Kim,Junsik Shin,Jaejin Lee*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ThunderEP：面向PCIe消费级GPU系统的MoE专家并行通信方案，通过消除环形中继跳、用DMA引擎传输与降低同步开销，显著加速dispatch/combine通信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型专家并行需在每层进行大量GPU间通信，随激活专家数增加，通信占比不断上升。在PCIe消费级GPU系统上，跨GPU传输必须经CPU内存。现有EP通信库假设GPU直连，忽视消费级GPU；主流框架改用NCCL，但其经CPU中转的通信造成冗余PCIe传输，并与专家计算争抢GPU资源，难以有效重叠。

Method: 提出ThunderEP通信设计：去除传统ring算法的中继跳；通过DMA引擎搬运数据以避免计算资源争用；通过减少CPU内存中完成标志的轮询开销来降低同步延迟；并将其集成到vLLM中。

Result: 在RTX 4090与RTX 5090两套PCIe系统、三种主流MoE模型上评测：dispatch平均加速2.00倍，combine平均加速1.53倍（相对NCCL），端到端相比最先进MoE推理框架最高加速1.66倍。

Conclusion: ThunderEP针对PCIe消费级GPU系统的通信瓶颈进行优化，在不依赖GPU直连的条件下有效加速MoE专家并行推理，集成vLLM后取得显著端到端收益。

Abstract: Expert parallelism (EP) enables inference of large Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models by placing their experts across multiple GPUs, but requires substantial communication between GPUs at every MoE layer. As contemporary MoE models activate more experts per token, this communication accounts for a growing fraction of inference time. The cost becomes particularly pronounced on PCIe-based consumer GPU systems, where all inter-GPU transfers traverse CPU memory. However, existing MoE-specialized EP communication libraries assume that direct GPU-to-GPU access is available, largely overlooking consumer GPUs. Therefore, most LLM frameworks instead rely on NCCL, whose CPU-staged communication incurs redundant PCIe transfers and competes with expert computation for GPU resources, limiting their overlap. We present ThunderEP, a novel communication design for such systems that removes the relay hops of traditional ring algorithm, moves data through DMA engines to avoid compute resource contention, and minimizes synchronization latency by reducing the polling overhead of completion flags in CPU memory. We integrate the proposed design into vLLM and evaluate it on three widely used MoE models. Experiments on two PCIe systems equipped with RTX 4090 and RTX 5090 GPUs show that ThunderEP achieves average speedups of 2.00$\times$ and 1.53$\times$ over NCCL for dispatch and combine, respectively, and up to 1.66$\times$ end-to-end speedup over state-of-the-art MoE inference frameworks.

</details>


### [275] [Reinforcement Learning-Guided Graph Transformations for SpTRSV Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40159)
*Buse Yılmaz*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出强化学习引导的图变换框架用于稀疏三角求解（SpTRSV），通过将图变换建模为序贯决策问题，学习依赖矩阵的变换策略，在仅修改极少行的情况下显著降低层级数与各层代价变异系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SpTRSV是科学与工程应用中的基础核心，但稀疏三角矩阵的固有数据依赖严重限制了可用并行度，使高效负载分配变得困难。已有的图变换技术虽通过修改依赖图改善并行执行，却依赖人工设计的启发式规则，开发与适应不同优化目标都很困难。

Method: 将图变换形式化为序贯决策问题，构建强化学习引导的图变换框架，由RL智能体学习依赖具体矩阵的变换策略；并通过课程学习与微调实现策略迁移。

Result: 在真实稀疏矩阵上，层级数最多降低94%，层级代价变异系数最多降低80%，最高情况下仅修改1.50%的行。平均而言，层级数降低23%，变异系数降低29%，仅重写0.82%的矩阵行。启发式策略层级缩减更激进（31%–46%），但RL方法在变异系数上的平均降幅最大，体现其平衡多个竞争目标的能力。学习到的策略可通过课程学习与微调迁移到未见矩阵，零样本实验则揭示了跨稀疏模式泛化的局限。

Conclusion: 强化学习引导的图变换能有效平衡SpTRSV中相互竞争的多重优化目标，并以极小的矩阵修改代价获得显著收益，策略具备一定迁移能力，但跨稀疏模式的零样本泛化仍受限。

Abstract: Sparse triangular solve (SpTRSV) is a fundamental kernel in numerous scientific and engineering applications. However, the data dependencies inherent in sparse triangular matrices significantly limit the available parallelism and make efficient workload distribution challenging. Recent graph transformation techniques address these limitations by modifying the dependency graph of the input matrix to improve parallel execution. Existing graph transformation strategies, however, rely on manually designed heuristics, making their development and adaptation to different optimization objectives challenging. This work proposes a reinforcement learning-guided graph transformation framework for SpTRSV, in which graph transformation is formulated as a sequential decision-making problem and an RL agent learns matrix-dependent transformation policies. Experimental results on real-world sparse matrices demonstrate level reductions of up to 94% and reductions of up to 80% in the coefficient of variation of level costs, while modifying only 1.50% of the rows in the highest case. On average, the RL- guided graph transformation achieves a 23% reduction in the number of levels and a 29% reduction in the coefficient of variation of level costs while rewriting only 0.82% of the matrix rows. Although the heuristic strategies generally achieve more aggressive level reduction(between 31% and 46%), the RL-based approach achieves the largest average reduction in the coefficient of variation of level costs, demonstrating its ability to balance competing graph transformation objectives. The results further show that the learned policies can be transferred to previously unseen matrices through curriculum learning and fine-tuning, while zero-shot experiments provide insights into the limitations of generalizing graph transformation policies across different sparsity patterns.

</details>


### [276] [DynaFlow: Transparent and Flexible Intra-Device Parallelism via Programmable Operator Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21603)
*Yi Pan,Yile Gu,Jinbin Luo,Yibo Wu,Ziren Wang,Hongtao Zhang,Ziyi Xu,Shengkai Lin,Baris Kasikci,Stephanie Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: DynaFlow通过将逻辑模型定义与物理执行调度解耦，实现设备内并行的透明灵活集成，以最小代码改动在6个主流ML系统中提升最高1.29倍吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设备内并行可重叠使用不同资源的算子执行，以缓解ML推理与训练中的资源利用不足。但其广泛应用受限于与现有框架静态、顺序编程模型的根本冲突：集成这些策略需要侵入式、针对特定模型的代码重构，工程代价极高；且策略对执行上下文（工作负载、模型架构、硬件）高度敏感，迫使开发者实现并维护多种专门方案。

Method: DynaFlow将逻辑模型定义与物理执行调度解耦。前端提供带注解的图划分能力，以及可编程接口用于定义自定义设备内并行策略；后端异步管理复杂控制流与数据流，采用自定义内存管理消除拷贝开销，并保持与CUDA Graphs、TorchInductor等优化的兼容性。

Result: DynaFlow能将代表性并行策略集成到6个最先进的ML系统中，且代码改动极小，吞吐提升最高达1.29倍。代码已开源于GitHub。

Conclusion: 通过解耦逻辑定义与物理调度，DynaFlow以低侵入方式实现了设备内并行的透明集成，兼容现有优化，并在多种ML系统中验证了其有效性与通用性。

Abstract: Intra-device parallelism addresses resource under-utilization in ML inference and training by overlapping the execution of operators with different resource usage. However, its wide adoption is hindered by a fundamental conflict with the static, sequential programming model of existing frameworks. Integrating these strategies requires invasive, model-specific code overhauls, representing an intractable engineering cost. This is further amplified by the high sensitivity of strategies to execution contexts (e.g., workload, model architecture, hardware), forcing developers to implement and maintain multiple specialized solutions. To address this, we propose DynaFlow, a framework that enables the transparent and flexible integration of intra-device parallelism by decoupling the logical model definition from the physical execution schedule. DynaFlow introduces a flexible frontend with annotations for graph partitioning and a programmable interface for defining custom intra-device parallelism strategies. Its efficient backend manages complex control/data-flow asynchronously, uses custom memory management to eliminate copy overheads, and preserves compatibility with optimizations like CUDA Graphs and TorchInductor. We demonstrate that DynaFlow can integrate representative parallelism strategies into 6 state-of-the-art ML systems with minimal code changes, achieving up to a 1.29x throughput improvement. DynaFlow is publicly available at https://github.com/uw-syfi/DynaFlow.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [277] [Automated Prefetching for Object Spatial Programming Using Temporal Trace Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38439)
*Baichuan Li,Jayanaka Dantanarayana,Savini Kashmira,Lingjia Tang,Jason Mars*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出Temporal Trace Graph（TTG），通过在编程模型层面暴露可检查的访问路径与显式遍历续延，实现跨遍历步骤的自动预取规划，在Jac/PostgreSQL上最高取得2.99倍延迟加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应用中依赖式的数据库访问会在请求关键路径上串行化应用与数据库之间的往返（round trips）。若要跨多个遍历步骤进行规划，必须同时知道两件事：计算可能沿哪些关系前进，以及已发现的对象如何成为后续的执行上下文。现有编程模型未能显式暴露这些信息，导致难以自动规划预取。

Method: 作者识别出一种编程模型契约，通过可检查的访问路径（inspectable access paths）与显式遍历续延（explicit traversal continuation）来暴露上述信息。在Object-Spatial Programming（OSP）中演示该契约：walker在带类型对象图上遍历并执行节点类型特定代码。据此提出Temporal Trace Graph（TTG）——一种有界的、请求特定的候选对象与遍历派生转移图。walker生成时，TTG Builder基于请求起始节点，将静态提取的访问规则与续延规则在实时图上组合；异步预取器在walker执行期间加载已规划对象。系统在Jac/PostgreSQL上实现。

Result: 在覆盖线性、多跳、递归和值依赖四类遍历的四个应用上评估：TTG在三个应用中改善了端到端延迟，相对原版运行时最高达2.99倍加速。与手工分阶段预取的对比刻画了自动跨步骤规划的收益与成本。评估还显示规划开销可能超过所避免的需求延迟，且值依赖控制会引入有界的过度预取（bounded overfetch）。

Conclusion: 通过将访问路径与遍历续延显式化，编程模型可为跨步骤自动预取提供规划依据；TTG验证了该思路在多种遍历形态下的有效性，但规划开销与值依赖导致的过度预取构成其代价边界。

Abstract: Dependent database accesses can serialize application--database round trips on a request's critical path. Planning across traversal steps requires knowing both which relationships a computation may follow and how discovered objects become subsequent execution contexts. We identify a programming-model contract that exposes this information through inspectable access paths and explicit traversal continuation. We demonstrate this contract in Object-Spatial Programming (OSP), where walkers traverse a typed object graph and execute node-type-specific code. We introduce the Temporal Trace Graph (TTG), a bounded, request-specific graph of candidate objects and traversal-derived transitions. At walker spawn, the TTG Builder composes statically extracted access and continuation rules over the live graph from the request's starting node. An asynchronous prefetcher loads planned objects while the walker executes. We implement TTG in Jac/PostgreSQL and evaluate four applications covering linear, multi-hop, recursive, and value-dependent traversals. TTG improves end-to-end latency in three applications, achieving up to 2.99x speedup over the stock runtime. Comparisons with manually staged prefetching characterize the benefits and costs of automatic cross-step planning. The evaluation also shows that planning overhead can exceed avoided demand latency and that value-dependent control can introduce bounded overfetch.

</details>


### [278] [A 3GPP-Compliant Benchmark Dataset for RIS-Aided Beyond 5G Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39058)
*Pujitha Mamillapalli,Pankaj Singh Rathour,Abhinav Kumar*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个大规模、符合3GPP TR 38.901标准的RIS辅助毫米波网络数据集，包含基于全局最优暴力码本搜索得到的oracle相位配置标签，并引入CSI到CQI映射作为基准任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RIS是B5G网络中实现可编程无线环境的关键技术，但数据驱动的RIS研究受限于缺乏标准化、高保真、开源的数据集。

Method: 构建覆盖多个3GPP典型部署场景、20种受控变体（用户密度、衰落条件、阻塞情形各异）的数据集；每个样本通过全局最优暴力码本搜索获得oracle RIS相位配置，并提供完整CSI、逐链路信道分解、最优相位矩阵和CQI标签等多任务标注。

Result: 提出CSI到CQI映射的基准任务，将RIS辅助链路质量预测转化为可扩展的标量分类问题，避免直接预测相位向量的指数级输出复杂度；在分布内、分布外及真实硬件测量条件下与先进架构进行了对比评估。

Conclusion: 该数据集为RIS辅助B5G网络的数据驱动研究提供了可复现、可扩展、面向社区的基础资源。

Abstract: Reconfigurable Intelligent Surfaces (RIS) are emerging as a key technology for programmable wireless environments in the beyond the fifth generation (B5G) networks. However, data-driven RIS research remains bottleneck by the lack of standardized, high-fidelity and open-source datasets. In this paper, we introduce a large-scale 3GPP TR 38.901-compliant dataset for RIS-aided millimeter wave (mmWave) networks, that considers severe path loss, blockage sensitivity, and spatial channel sparsity make the RIS assistance more impactful. The dataset spans various canonical 3GPP deployment scenarios across 20 controlled variants, capturing diverse user densities, fading conditions, and blockage regimes. Uniquely, every sample includes oracle RIS phase configurations obtained via a globally optimal brute-force codebook search, providing gold-standard supervision labels that are absent from any existing public dataset. Rich multi-task annotations comprising full channel state information (CSI), per-link channel decomposition, optimal phase matrices, and channel quality index (CQI) labels support a broad range of machine learning paradigms and downstream tasks, including phase optimization, channel estimation, and interference management. As the primary benchmark task, we introduce a novel CSI-to-CQI mapping that frames RIS-aided link-quality prediction as a scalable scalar classification problem, thereby avoiding the exponential output complexity of the direct phase vector prediction. We have evaluated this mapping against state-of-the-art architectures under in-distribution, out-of-distribution, and real-world hardware measurement conditions. Our dataset provides a reproducible, extensible, and community-ready foundation to accelerate data-driven research in RIS-aided B5G networks.

</details>


### [279] [O-Funnel: Lossless Structural Capture and Requirement-Driven Extraction from Drifting, Heterogeneous Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39209)
*Osama Mustafa*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出 O-Funnel，将文档字段抽取中"描述值"与"定位值"两种职责解耦，通过转译成五构造器类型树并结合多证据融合定位字段，在真实与构造数据上显著优于手写正则解析器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上从多格式、模式漂移的文档中抽取固定字段依赖手写字节模式（正则），一旦键被重命名、值格式变化或先出现相似值就会失效。作者认为根本原因是结构性的：单一模式必须同时承担"描述值"和"在其上下文中定位值"两项任务。

Method: O-Funnel 将两项职责分离：先把任意 XML、JSON、CSV、HTML 或键值文本文档转译为仅含五种构造器的类型树，并由 oracle 把关，拒绝任何无法重建源文档的捕获；每个所需字段以树自身术语声明，并通过融合多种独立证据（键名、路径、值形状、同义词、键拼写、记录邻域、值画像）来定位，支持度最高的节点胜出；缺失字段会附带原因报告；未被任何需求认领的数据成为"残渣"，由 funnel 回溯到需求以学习新的键别名。实现为无依赖的 Python 库。

Result: 在 34,989 条真实 PubMed 记录上，O-Funnel 与手写解析器持平（F1 1.00）；在五元素模式重命名后，手写解析器正则降至 0.20，而 O-Funnel 保持 1.00 且每次捕获均验证完整；在隔离正则失效模式的构造测试集上，F1 从 0.43 提升到 1.00，自我改进后从 0.80 提升到 0.94；在留出模式匹配实例上无需训练即可与经典匹配器竞争。

Conclusion: 通过结构性地解耦"值的描述"与"值的定位"，并借助类型树、oracle 校验、多证据融合与残渣驱动的别名学习，O-Funnel 在模式漂移场景下比手写正则解析器更鲁棒，同时保持无依赖、易安装（pip install ofunnel）的工程可用性。

Abstract: Pulling a fixed set of fields out of documents that arrive in many formats and under drifting schemas is usually done with hand-written byte patterns, which break whenever a key is renamed, a value is reformatted, or a lookalike value appears first. We argue the cause is structural: one pattern must both describe the value and locate it among its surroundings. O-Funnel separates the two. It transcribes any XML, JSON, CSV, HTML or key-value text document into one typed tree over five constructors, gated by an oracle that rejects any capture that does not reconstruct its source. Each needed field is declared in the tree's own terms and located by fusing independent evidence (key, path, value shape, synonym, key spelling, record neighborhood, value profile), so the best-supported node wins and a missing field is reported with a reason. Data no requirement claims becomes residue that a funnel traces back to the requirements to learn new key aliases. On 34,989 real PubMed records, O-Funnel matches a hand-written parser (F1 1.00). After a five-element schema rename, the parser's regular expressions fall to 0.20 while O-Funnel stays at 1.00, with every capture verified complete.
