On constructed suites that isolate regex failure modes it raises F1 from 0.43 to 1.00, and from 0.80 to 0.94 after self-improvement; on held-out schema-matching instances it is competitive with classical matchers without training. O-Funnel is a dependency-free Python library (pip install ofunnel).

</details>


### [280] [Recommendation Systems for Exploratory Data Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39412)
*Anna Fariha*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出将推荐作为数据系统的核心能力，围绕单任务/多任务、单动作/多动作两个维度，规划了探索性数据任务（EDT）推荐系统的研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索性数据任务（EDT）被大量用户用于理解陌生数据、发现趋势与支持决策，但可选动作空间极其庞大，用户难以在成千上万种连接、转换和聚合操作中做出选择，导致"探索瘫痪"。由于EDT工作流相互关联，每个选择都会影响后续探索，次优选择会带来低效、遗漏洞察、确认偏差和覆盖不全等问题，因此需要智能推荐来高效引导用户。

Method: 论文提出EDT推荐系统的愿景，沿两个维度展开：单任务与多任务设置、单动作与多动作推荐。研究议程从推荐单个EDT动作，逐步推进到受限动作包与动作序列，最终到跨互联EDT的协同推荐。同时识别了融合多种上下文（用户、数据、任务、生态系统）、应对效率挑战以及跨任务协调的研究方向。

Result: 这是一篇愿景/立场类论文，未提供实验结果，而是给出了EDT推荐的研究路线图：从单个动作推荐，到受限动作包与序列推荐，再到跨互联EDT的协同推荐，并指明了需要考虑的上下文因素、效率问题与任务协调机制。

Conclusion: EDT推荐面临动作空间组合爆炸、动作依赖数据需高成本物化、以及跨相互依赖任务需协调动作包/序列等挑战。作者主张将推荐作为数据系统的核心能力，以主动引导用户走向有价值的探索动作，从而降低探索性数据任务的门槛。

Abstract: A large class of data-centric tasks is exploratory, where users iteratively steer workflows, refining subjective goals as new insights emerge. These Exploratory Data Tasks (EDTs) are performed by millions of users with varying levels of expertise to understand unfamiliar data, discover trends, and identify evidence that informs critical decision-making. However, a key challenge in EDTs is the enormous space of possible actions that one can take at each step: users struggle to choose among thousands of joins, transformations, and aggregations, causing "exploration paralysis". Because EDT workflows are interconnected, each choice impacts subsequent exploration, and suboptimal choices can lead to inefficiency, missed insights, confirmation bias, and incomplete coverage. This calls for intelligent recommendations that efficiently guide users toward optimal EDT actions.
  We envision recommendation as a core capability of data systems, proactively guiding users toward promising actions and thereby lowering the barrier to exploratory data tasks. EDT recommendation is challenging because the action space is combinatorial and actions are data-dependent, which require costly materialization. Moreover, recommendation often involves bundles or sequences of actions across interdependent tasks, requiring coordination across tasks. In this paper, we present our vision of EDT recommendation systems along two axes: single-task vs. multi-task settings and single-action vs. multi-action recommendations. We outline a research agenda that progresses from recommending individual EDT actions to constrained bundles and sequences of actions, and ultimately to coordinated recommendations across interconnected EDTs. We identify research directions for incorporating various contexts (user, data, task, and ecosystem), addressing efficiency challenges, and coordinating across tasks.

</details>


### [281] [Scalable Approximate Algorithm for Dynamic Densest Subhypergraphs with Solution-Guided Maintenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39778)
*Jingbang Chen,Chenhao Ma,Yingli Zhou*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出CAP算法，在超边插入和删除时显式维护(1+ε)-近似的最稠密子超图，通过候选解与端点分配引导局部修复，实现高效动态维护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超图可建模群体交互，寻找高度连通的群组是基础任务。但当交互不断到达和过期时，维护稠密群组需要频繁且昂贵的底层表示更新，现有方法效率不足。

Method: CAP显式维护(1+ε)-近似最稠密子超图。算法保留一个解候选及端点分配，用其约束最优密度。候选密度指导维护：仅当该界过大、无法保证所需近似时才触发修复。局部搜索重新分配负载或寻找更稠密候选，使解能跨多次更新保留。分析将维护工作量与搜索区域大小及密度损失关联，识别局部修复保持廉价的条s。

Result: 在真实超图上，相比所评估的动态基线方法，加速可达近四个数量级，同时支持频繁查询且精度高。在普通图上，与专门的动态图方法相比也具竞争力，在测试负载上加速约达64倍。

Conclusion: CAP为动态超图下近似最稠密子超图维护提供了可扩展且高效的方案，其理论分析解释了观测到的效率，并在超图与普通图上均表现优异。

Abstract: Hypergraphs model interactions involving groups of entities, and finding highly connected groups is a fundamental task in analyzing these data. When interactions arrive and expire, maintaining a dense group can require frequent and expensive changes to the underlying representation. We propose CAP, a scalable algorithm that explicitly maintains a $(1+ε)$-approximate densest subhypergraph under hyperedge insertions and deletions. The algorithm keeps a solution candidate together with an endpoint allocation that bounds the optimum density. The candidate's density guides maintenance: an update triggers repair only when this bound is too large to establish the required approximation. Local searches redistribute load or find a denser candidate, allowing CAP to retain useful solutions across many updates. We give an analysis that relates maintenance work to the size of the regions searched and the density lost by the maintained solution. It identifies conditions under which local repair remains inexpensive and provides a theoretical explanation for the observed efficiency. Experiments on real-world hypergraphs show speedups of up to nearly four orders of magnitude over the evaluated dynamic baselines, while supporting frequent queries and high accuracy. CAP also remains competitive with specialized dynamic graph methods on ordinary graphs, with speedups of up to approximately $64\times$ on the tested workloads.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [282] [TAGGRAPH: Tag-Augmented Graphs for Graph Retrieval of Agent Persistent Histories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38353)
*Yu-Su Chen,Yu-Jung Liang,Pengtao Xie*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于共享5W式对话记忆的受控评估框架，用于公平比较不同长期记忆系统。在LongMemEval-S上AdaptiveGraph取得0.844 MRR，但BM25（0.867）与OpenClaw（0.880）更强；在ATANT Core上局部图遍历优于扩散与BM25，而BM25在压力轮次领先。检索策略需结合记忆设置与强词法基线共同评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期记忆让LLM智能体能够回忆过往交互并在跨会话中保持一致，但记忆系统在表示、索引、检索和评估方面差异很大，难以直接比较，因此需要一个受控的评估框架来进行公平对比。

Method: 构建基于共享5W式对话记忆的受控评估框架；采用在同一基础图上遍历的局部图配置，AdaptiveGraph加入时间顺序边与个性化PageRank扩散；同时对相同抽取笔记使用BM25，并将OpenClaw作为原始输入的外部参照；在LongMemEval-S与ATANT Core上进行检索排序对比。

Result: 检索排序随记忆设置而变。LongMemEval-S上AdaptiveGraph为0.844 MRR（最强图配置），但BM25为0.867、OpenClaw为0.880；ATANT Core上局部图遍历优于扩散和BM25，而BM25在压力轮次领先。缩小LongMemEval-S存储规模未复现ATANT的扩散劣势，但最小测试存储仍大于ATANT Core，故存储规模无法排除；该劣势在宽松内容匹配标准下依然存在。词汇归一化与抽取质量显著影响图检索，前五名未命中中缺失抽取标签很常见。

Conclusion: 检索策略应与记忆设置结合评估，并与强词法基线对比；图检索效果高度依赖词汇归一化和抽取质量，存储规模等混淆因素仍需进一步控制。

Abstract: Long-term memory lets LLM agents recall past interactions and remain consistent across sessions, but memory systems are hard to compare because they often vary in representation, indexing, retrieval, and evaluation. We present a controlled evaluation framework based on shared 5W-style conversational memories. Localized graph configurations traverse a common base graph; AdaptiveGraph adds chronological edges and Personalized PageRank diffusion. We also evaluate BM25 over the same extracted notes and OpenClaw as a raw-input external reference. Retrieval rankings vary across memory settings. On LongMemEval-S, AdaptiveGraph is the strongest graph configuration at 0.844 MRR, but BM25 reaches 0.867 and OpenClaw 0.880. On ATANT Core, localized graph traversal outperforms diffusion and BM25, whereas BM25 leads the stress rounds. Reducing LongMemEval-S within the tested range does not reproduce the ATANT diffusion penalty, but the smallest tested store remains larger than ATANT Core, so store size cannot be ruled out. The penalty also persists under a permissive content-match criterion. Vocabulary normalization and extraction quality substantially affect graph retrieval, and missing extraction tags are common among top-five misses. Retrieval strategies should therefore be evaluated jointly with the memory setting and against strong lexical baselines.

</details>


### [283] [AdaM-Rec: Adaptive Modality Routing for Multimodal Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38455)
*Honghao Fu,Jiacheng Chen,Manxi Lin,Junjun Zheng,Xiangheng Kong,Yiwei Wang,Xin Yu,Miao Xu,Yuning Jiang,Yujun Cai*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出AdaM-Rec，一个基于LLM的自适应模态路由框架，可动态校准多模态推荐中对文本与多模态证据的依赖，性能优于SOTA基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态推荐系统多采用静态模态融合，假设文本与视觉信号相对重要性在各推荐场景中保持稳定。然而不同查询需求各异：有些需要细粒度视觉线索，有些更依赖文本或功能性语义。不加区分的融合会引入无用线索，损害推荐质量。

Method: 基于结构化自然语言表示（物品与用户偏好），通过代理召回任务估计模态可靠性：生成与实际查询粒度匹配、指向用户正向交互物品的伪查询作为可验证代理目标，评估哪种模态在类似场景中召回表现更好，并以智能体方式优化路由策略。随后按优化策略执行路由召回，用协同物品丰富结果，并根据查询与用户偏好的相关性对候选排序。

Result: 实验表明AdaM-Rec相较最先进基线取得强劲性能，验证了自适应控制模态依赖的有效性及更广泛潜力。

Conclusion: 自适应控制模态依赖在多模态推荐中有效且具潜力，为动态校准文本与多模态证据提供可行方案。

Abstract: While recent multimodal recommender systems have demonstrated the effectiveness of incorporating visual and textual information to improve downstream performance, most existing methods rely on static modality fusion, assuming that the relative importance of textual and visual signals remains stable across recommendation scenarios. This design may not fully account for an important variation across recommendation requests: some queries require fine-grained visual cues, whereas others are better served by textual or functional semantics, in which case indiscriminate modality fusion brings in uninformative cues and impairs recommendation quality. To address this, we propose AdaM-Rec, an LLM-based framework for adaptive modality routing in multimodal recommendation, which enables dynamic calibration of reliance on textual and multimodal evidence for user-specific queries. Built on structured natural-language representations of items and user preferences, it estimates modality reliability using proxy recall tasks. Specifically, it generates pseudo-queries that match the granularity of the actual query while pointing to the user's positively interacted items as verifiable proxy targets, evaluating which modality yields better recall performance in analogous scenarios and optimizing the routing strategy in an agentic manner. It then performs routed recall with optimized strategy, enriches results with collaborative items, and ranks candidates by their relevance to both the query and user preferences. Experiments demonstrate that AdaM-Rec delivers strong performance against state-of-the-art baselines, highlighting the effectiveness and broader potential of adaptive control over modality reliance in multimodal recommendation.

</details>


### [284] [Re-ranking and Late Interaction Drive Retrieval Quality: A Controlled Comparison of RAG Strategies for Scientific Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38473)
*Bhagyesh Rathi,Eshan Chawla,William B. Andreopoulos*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文对科学问答场景下的六种RAG检索策略进行受控对比，共享同一生成器、提示和评测协议，基于46万余篇arXiv论文构建合成问答数据集，并开源代码与数据，为RAG设计权衡提供开放测试平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG已成为LLM外部知识 grounding 的标准方法，但其检索流程设计空间庞大，各变体间的权衡尚未被充分理解，尤其在真实规模的专业领域语料上缺乏系统性对比。

Method: 对比六种检索策略：(i)经典top-k稠密检索；(ii)LLM查询改写；(iii)查询改写后LLM重排；(iv)基于RRF的多查询融合；(v)生成器自主决定是否检索的agentic工具调用流程；(vi)基于ColBERTv2的late-interaction检索。所有流程共享同一生成器(Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct)、提示和评测协议，五个单向量流程共享SPECTER2嵌入与Chroma向量库，均从463,971篇2024-2025年arXiv论文全库检索。同时发布由Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct生成的19,484条合成问题数据集。

Result: 构建并开源了可复现的大规模评测方案：19,484条合成问答数据集（生成成功9,742篇论文），所有策略在同一查询集上评测，采用LLM-as-a-judge多维度质量评估及直接gold-paper检索指标。

Conclusion: 形成了研究文献语料上RAG设计选择成本与质量权衡的开放测试平台，为忠实性、检索鲁棒性及agentic检索等未来工作奠定基础。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) is now the standard way to ground Large Language Models (LLMs) in external knowledge, yet the design space of retrieval pipelines is large and the trade-offs between variants are not well understood, especially on domain-specific corpora at realistic scale. In this work, we present a controlled comparison of six retrieval strategies for scientific question answering: (i) classic top-k dense retrieval, (ii) LLM-based query rephrasing, (iii) query rephrasing followed by LLM-based reranking, (iv) multi-query fusion via Reciprocal Rank Fusion (RRF), (v) an agentic tool-call pipeline in which the generator decides for itself whether to retrieve, and (vi) late-interaction retrieval with ColBERTv2. All six pipelines share the same generator (Meta-Llama/Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct), prompt, and evaluation protocol; the five single-vector pipelines additionally share SPECTER2 embeddings and a Chroma vector store; and all six retrieve from the full corpus of 463,971 arXiv papers dated 2024-2025. To support reproducible, large-scale evaluation, we also release a synthetic question dataset of 19,484 problem-statement and methodology questions generated by Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct from a random sample of 10,000 papers across academic domains (query generation succeeded for 9,742 of them), and every strategy is evaluated on this same query set. We describe the architecture and implementation of each pipeline, release the code and the synthetic question dataset, and evaluate each strategy with an LLM-as-a-judge protocol along multiple quality dimensions, together with direct gold-paper retrieval metrics. The result is an open testbed for studying the cost and quality trade-offs of RAG design choices on a research-literature corpus, and a basis for future work on faithfulness, retrieval robustness, and agentic retrieval.

</details>


### [285] [Exploring Forum Post Retrieval with Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38646)
*Yang Li,Yaguang Liu,Heng Liu,Shengbo Guo,Samson Komo,Jane Kou,Yulian Zhou,Gang Yang,Shubhojeet Sarkar,Gaurav Chakravorty,Yujie Liu,Haipeng Chen,Yonghuan Yang,Deepti Chheda,Yamin Wang,Mike Plumpe,Rish Tandon*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文探索在Facebook Forum这一新场景中应用生成式推荐(GR)，通过在数据与语义ID两个维度进行迁移，将3B指令微调语言模型用于直接从用户上下文生成SID，并系统消融关键设计选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Facebook Forum是面向Facebook Groups中中重度用户的独立应用，属于新上线的推荐场景，其自身交互数据过于稀疏，无法从零训练生成式推荐模型，因此需要研究如何借助迁移学习解决新场景冷启动问题。

Method: 在两个维度上进行迁移：一是训练数据从仅用Forum会话扩展到更广泛的Facebook Groups互动语料；二是复用从跨平台Facebook Feed数据中学到的层次化、基于前缀的语义ID(SID)，而非单独拟合Forum专属分词器。随后用3B参数的指令微调语言模型进行有监督微调，使其直接从用户上下文生成SID。并系统消融SID构建方式、用户历史的组成与长度、用户画像特征是否纳入等关键设计。

Result: 跨平台的SID可以成功迁移到新的推荐场景(Forum)；消融实验揭示了SID构建、用户历史组成与长度、用户画像特征等因素对实际效果的影响，并给出了在实践中最重要的设计选择。

Conclusion: 生成式推荐能够在数据稀疏的新社交推荐场景中落地，跨平台语义ID的迁移是可行且有效的，论文为在真实社交平台上部署GR提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Generative recommendation (GR) has emerged as an alternative to embedding-based retrieval, building on the success of generative models in language and vision. We are exploring GR on Facebook Forum, a standalone application for medium-to-heavy users of Facebook Groups. Because Forum is a new surface, its own interaction data are too sparse to train a GR model from scratch. We address this with transfer along two axes: we train on a broader corpus of Facebook Groups engagements rather than Forum sessions alone, and we reuse hierarchical, prefix-based semantic IDs (SIDs) learned from cross-platform Facebook Feed data instead of fitting a Forum-specific tokenizer. A 3B-parameter instruction-tuned language model is then supervised-fine-tuned to generate SIDs directly from user context. We systematically ablate the design choices that matter most in practice, including SID construction, the composition and length of user history, and the inclusion of user-profile features. Our results show that cross-platform SIDs transfer to a new recommendation surface, and offer practical guidance for teams deploying GR on real-world social platforms.

</details>


### [286] [PatchHolmes: Agentic Patch Retrieval via Listwise Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38807)
*Guanqun Yang,Yingming Zhou,Jiangrui Zheng,Shudong Hao,Xueqing Liu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: PatchHolmes 是一个两阶段补丁检索系统，第一阶段用混合检索器召回候选，第二阶段用智能体以 listwise 方式阅读候选项，在 GitHubAD 上显著超越 Favia 与 IRCoT，且无需微调或外部搜索 API。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流行情数据库中 60% 至 63% 的 CVE 缺少补丁链接，严重阻碍漏洞管理流程。已有工作多为 pointwise 方法，即对每个候选项独立打分，未能利用候选列表的整体语境，因此需要一种能够同时观察全部候选并进行选择性深入阅读的检索方案。

Method: 系统分两阶段：Phase 1 使用混合检索器生成候选列表；Phase 2 采用智能体检测循环，对 top-100 候选进行 listwise 阅读，并通过四种受限预算工具选择性读取 3 至 10 个提交，最终提交单一最佳提交。整个系统运行在冻结的开源权重模型上，基于本地 Git 仓库，无需微调或外部搜索 API。

Result: 在 GitHubAD 上，PatchHolmes 的 Recall@1 比 pointwise 二分类器 Favia 高 25.34%，比 retrieve-and-CoT 基线 IRCoT 高 31.40%，而每个 CVE 仅需一次智能体对话（Favia 需十次）。在候选集相同的条件下，智能体相比直接取检索器 top-1 提升 27.32% Recall@1；同一智能体原样迁移至 PatchFinder_top10，将 Recall@1 从 24.28% 提升至 39.86%。在 Qwen 系列内更换 LLM 主干，Recall@1 变化小于 1%；第二模型家族 gpt-oss 也远高于无智能体基线。

Conclusion: 性能增益主要来自 listwise 智能体循环，而非特定模型主干；该方法在冻结开源模型与本地仓库条件下即可实现高效、可迁移的补丁检索，为缺乏补丁链接的 CVE 提供了有力解决途径。

Abstract: Patch retrieval, the task of finding the commit that fixes a known vulnerability, is the foundation of vulnerability management workflows, yet 60% to 63% of CVEs in the major advisory databases lack a patch link. We present PatchHolmes, a two-phase patch retrieval system that pairs a hybrid first-stage retriever with an agentic second-stage inspection loop. Unlike pointwise prior work that scores each candidate independently, the Phase 2 agent reads the top-100 listwise: it sees the full candidate list at once and selectively reads 3 to 10 commits through four budgeted tools before submitting a single best commit. On GitHubAD, PatchHolmes beats the pointwise binary classifier Favia by 25.34% Recall@1 and the retrieve-and-CoT baseline IRCoT by 31.40%, at one agent conversation per CVE versus Favia's ten; with the candidate set held identical, the agent adds 27.32% Recall@1 over taking the retriever's top candidate, and the same agent, transferred unchanged to PatchFinder_top10, lifts Recall@1 from PatchFinder's own top-1 pick (24.28%) to 39.86%. Swapping the LLM backbone within the Qwen family changes Recall@1 by under 1%, and a second model family (gpt-oss) stays far above the no-agent floor, so the gain comes from the listwise agent loop; the entire system runs on a frozen open-weight model over a local Git repository, without fine-tuning or external search APIs.

</details>


### [287] [SkillSeek: Revisiting Agent Skill Retrieval at Marketplace Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38822)
*Guanqun Yang,Wenlong Zhang,Tian Shi,Ping Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出开源两阶段技能检索器 SkillSeek，用标准IR方案替代LLM中介检索循环，在 SkillsBench 上达到相当通过率且成本近乎为零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent Skills 生态中开源聚合技能已超23万，选择（检索）而非编写技能成为瓶颈。现有文献的主流做法是把选择外包给智能体本身，即LLM中介的检索循环：在决策循环内重写查询、精炼候选，导致每个任务都要消耗LLM token，成本高。

Method: SkillSeek 是基于标准信息检索配方构建的两阶段技能检索器：BGE-base 双编码器做第一阶段召回，小型交叉编码器做第二阶段重排，并通过 MCP 暴露为服务。在 89 任务的 SkillsBench 基准上，围绕技能池、骨干模型和方法构建 4×11 网格进行评估。

Result: SkillSeek 在几乎不增加成本的情况下达到与 Liu 等人 LLM 中介循环相当的观测通过率：纯 bm25 在四个设置中的三个已达到或超过其精炼循环的通过率，第四个设置的差距由小型交叉编码器弥补。第一阶段召回上限可解释这一模式。每试验总花费从 51.30 美元降至 27.54 美元（距无技能基线仅差五十美分以内）。

Conclusion: 在测试的 SkillsBench 任务与 OpenHands 运行框架下，标准IR配方可作为智能体技能检索的强默认方案；LLM中介的替代方案更适合确定性方法失效的场景。

Abstract: Anthropic's Agent Skills package reusable procedural know-how for an LLM agent into SKILL.md directories, and open-source aggregations have grown past 230,000 skills, making selection rather than authoring the bottleneck. The standing answer in the literature outsources selection to the agent itself: an LLM-mediated retrieval loop that rewrites queries and refines candidates inside the agent's decision loop, paying LLM tokens on every task. We present SkillSeek, an open-source two-stage skill retriever built from the standard IR recipe (a BGE-base bi-encoder feeding a small cross-encoder, exposed over MCP). Across a $4 \times 11$ grid of pool, backbone, and method on the 89-task SkillsBench benchmark, SkillSeek reaches observed parity with the LLM-mediated loop of Liu et al. at essentially no extra cost: plain bm25 alone records a pass rate at or above their refined loop on three of four settings, and a small cross-encoder covers the remaining difference on the fourth. A first-stage recall ceiling explains the pattern, and total per-trial spend drops from USD 51.30 to USD 27.54 (within fifty cents of the no-skill baseline). Under the SkillsBench tasks and OpenHands harness we tested, this positions the standard IR recipe as a strong default for agent-skill retrieval, with LLM-mediated alternatives a natural fit for cases where deterministic methods fall short.

</details>


### [288] [Text-Video Retrieval via Multi-Dimensional Saliency Assessment and Granularity-Aware Query Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38949)
*Shuquan Wei,Xi Chen,Xu Chen,Xiangyang Jia*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文提出MMTI方法，通过联合缓解视觉冗余并实现多粒度文本-视频交互，达成精确的多粒度语义对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本-视频检索方法多依赖单一维度的视觉重要性准则，忽视了视频多维时空特性；同时将文本编码为单一全局嵌入与视频对齐，压缩了时序事件与空间实体，加剧跨模态错位。

Method: MMTI包含两个核心模块：关键特征选择机制（KFS）通过联合评估多维显著性与可学习重要性分数，自适应识别并聚合信息量大的帧与图块，压缩稠密视觉特征；多粒度文本-视频交互模块（TVIM）采用动态门控机制，将文本查询分解为句子、帧、图块三级查询（SFP），实现多粒度对齐。

Result: 在四个标准基准上进行了大量实验，结果表明该方法优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 通过同时缓解视觉冗余并支持多粒度文本-视频交互，MMTI实现了不同粒度上的互补对齐，有效提升文本-视频检索性能。

Abstract: Text-video retrieval, which aims to bridge visual and textual modalities by learning a joint embedding space, has become a crucial task in multimodal intelligence. Despite extensive efforts to mitigate visual redundancy, previous methods typically rely on a single-aspect criterion to assess visual importance, overlooking the multifaceted spatiotemporal nature of video. In addition, encoding text into a single global embedding to align with videos compresses temporal events and spatial entities into a unified representation space, further aggravating cross-modal misalignment. To address these issues, we propose MMTI, a method that jointly mitigates visual redundancy and enables multi-grained text-video interaction to achieve accurate multi-grained semantic alignment. Specifically, a key feature selection (KFS) mechanism adaptively identifies and aggregates informative frames and patches by jointly evaluating multi-dimensional saliency and learnable importance scores, effectively compacting dense visual features and mitigating visual redundancy. Furthermore, our proposed multi-grained text-video interaction module (TVIM) employs a dynamic gating mechanism to decompose the text query into sentence, frame, and patch queries (SFP), enabling multi-grained text-video alignment. Complementary alignment at different granularities is thereby achieved. Extensive experiments on four standard benchmarks demonstrate that our method outperforms state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [289] [When LLM-Inferred User Context Adds Value in Production Streaming Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38999)
*Milad Sabouri,Neeraj Sharma,Sardar Hamidian,Shaghayegh Agah*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文在生产级流媒体平台上评估了语义用户画像策略，发现聚合画像与LLM生成画像的优劣取决于用户的消费模式：习惯型消费（约80%用户）中聚合画像更优，探索型用户中LLM生成画像更优，且LLM画像存在流行度吸引子效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统中的情境信息正从静态预定义变量转向从行为中推断的潜在表示。LLM能够将非结构化交互历史渲染为自然语言摘要，从而生成主题化用户情境。然而，这种生成式画像在何种条件下优于聚合画像，目前主要在领域层面得到有限刻画，缺乏细粒度分析。

Method: 在生产级流媒体平台上评估语义用户画像策略，针对全目录进行排序。采用2×2设计空间，交叉表示类型（聚合 vs LLM生成）与情境范围（整体历史 vs 注意力融合的短期与长期情境），并按用户消费模式进行分析。

Result: 两种表示类型的相对优劣取决于用户的消费模式。聚合画像在习惯型消费下持续更强（约占人口五分之四）；LLM生成画像对探索型用户更强，其后继交互在语义上偏离历史。此外观察到LLM生成画像存在流行度吸引子效应，略微提升列表内多样性，但大幅降低目录覆盖率和新颖性。

Conclusion: 情境感知系统可根据推断出的消费模式为不同用户选择画像策略，而非对所有用户统一使用单一表示。

Abstract: Contextual information in recommender systems is shifting from static, predefined variables toward latent representations inferred from behavior. Large language models support this shift by rendering an unstructured interaction history as a natural-language summary, which yields a thematic user context that can be encoded and used in place of an aggregate profile. The conditions under which such generated profiles outperform aggregate embeddings have received limited characterization mainly at the domain level. We evaluate semantic user-profiling strategies on a production streaming platform, ranking against the full catalog. The evaluation covers a 2*2 design space crossing representation type (aggregate or LLM-generated) with contextual scope (holistic history or attention-fused short-term and long-term contexts). The relative ordering of the two representation types is conditional on the user's consumption regime. Aggregate profiles are consistently stronger under habitual consumption, which characterizes approximately four-fifths of the population, while LLM-generated profiles are stronger for exploratory users whose subsequent interactions diverge semantically from their history. We also observe a popularity-attractor effect in LLM-generated profiles, which modestly raises within-list diversity while substantially lowering catalog coverage and reducing novelty. These results indicate that a context-aware system can select a profiling strategy from the inferred consumption regime rather than applying one representation to all users.

</details>


### [290] [RouteRec: Behavior-Guided Sparse Routing for Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39007)
*Junyeong Song,Jaemin Yoo*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出RouteRec，用会话行为线索作为MoE的路由依据，在宏观/中观/微观三个尺度分配专家，18个数据集-指标组合中12项第一、3项第二，平均排名1.61。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列推荐模型对所有会话用同一套参数化模块处理，忽视会话之间的行为差异；MoE虽能实现条件计算，但"由什么决定专家分配"这一问题尚未解决。

Method: 从会话历史中提取四类行为证据：交互节奏、物品组关注、重复与延续、流行度倾向；用这些线索在宏观、中观、微观三个范围路由计算：先由线索得分选出专家组，再在组内用当前主干状态细化专家选择。

Result: 在6个公开数据集、18个数据集-指标组合中，12项排名第一、3项第二，平均排名1.61，优于次优基线的4.11；进一步分析表明行为线索的贡献超出单纯增加容量，且路由模式与观察到的行为一致。

Conclusion: 以可观测的会话行为作为MoE路由准则，可有效提升序列推荐性能，并使专家分配具有可解释的行为对应关系。

Abstract: Sessionized interaction histories contain behavioral patterns that can improve sequential recommendation. However, existing models process all sessions through the same parameterized blocks, regardless of their behavioral differences. Mixture of Experts (MoE) enables conditional computation, but it leaves open what should guide expert allocation. We propose RouteRec, a sequential recommender that uses observed session behavior as the routing criterion. RouteRec summarizes four types of behavioral evidence from sessionized histories: interaction tempo, item-group focus, repetition and carryover, and popularity tendency. It uses these cues to route computation at macro, mid, and micro scopes. Cue-derived scores first select expert groups; within each selected group, the current backbone state then refines expert selection. Across six public datasets and 18 dataset-metric combinations, RouteRec ranks first in 12 and second in three, yielding the best overall average rank of 1.61 compared with 4.11 for the next-best baseline. Additional analyses suggest that the behavioral cues guide expert allocation beyond added capacity and produce routing patterns aligned with observed behavior. Our code is available at https://github.com/jy1559/RouteRec

</details>


### [291] [Routing Between Generative and Collaborative User Profiles: A Serving-Time Gate for Controllable Novelty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39043)
*Milad Sabouri,Neeraj Sharma,Sardar Hamidian,Shaghayegh Agah*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究了在推荐系统中选择性地调用由LLM生成的用户画像，通过一个服务时路由门控将用户分配给协同序列模型或LLM画像驱动的模型，从而在可控的精度损失下提升新颖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM虽能为推荐生成丰富的语义用户画像，但此类画像生成成本高，且未必适合对所有用户统一部署。因此作者希望探究LLM画像能否在生产推荐流程中被选择性地调用。

Method: 使用覆盖电影、电视剧和体育内容的真实流媒体数据集，训练一个服务时路由门控（routing gate），仅使用服务时特征，将每个用户分配给协同序列推荐模型或LLM画像驱动的推荐模型。通过路由阈值控制用户被送往生成式模型的激进度，形成可调的新颖性—相关性权衡。

Result: 在整体NDCG损失预算为5%时，学习到的门控将Novelty@10提升6.5%，同时路由12.5%的用户，在相当的精度成本下优于简单启发式和随机路由策略。此外，使用非生成式语义画像的结果表明，收益来自选择性路由而非LLM生成本身。

Conclusion: LLM生成的用户画像可作为协同推荐的、可控的补充手段，其价值关键在于选择性路由的机制，而非单纯的LLM生成能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) enable rich semantic user profiles for recommendation, but such profiles are more expensive to generate and are not necessarily desirable to deploy uniformly. We study whether LLM-generated profiles can instead be invoked selectively within a production recommendation pipeline. Using a real-world streaming dataset covering movies, TV shows, and sports content, we train a serving-time routing gate that assigns each user to either a collaborative sequential recommendation model or a recommendation model driven by an LLM-generated profile. The gate uses only serving-time features and learns to identify users for whom profile-based routing can increase Novelty@10 while preserving ranking relevance. A routing threshold controls how aggressively users are sent to the generative model, exposing a tunable novelty--relevance trade-off. At an overall NDCG-loss budget of 5\%, the learned gate increases Novelty@10 by 6.5\% while routing 12.5\% of users, outperforming simple heuristic and random routing policies at comparable relevance cost. These results show that LLM-generated user profiles can serve as a controllable complement to collaborative recommendation, while results with non-generative semantic profiles indicate that the benefit stems from selective routing rather than LLM generation alone.

</details>


### [292] [Learning Multiresolution Relevance for Hierarchical Generative Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39312)
*Weihao Shen,Wei Chen,Fuwei Zhang,Guojun Liu,Qingsong Hua,Wei Lin,Fuzhen Zhuang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出RARS（分辨率对齐相关性监督），将文档级相关性在多分辨率语义标识符（SID）层级上形式化为一致的条件分布，用细化级别分布监督共享查询表示，从而在自回归检索中优于标准全SID训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式检索中，同一查询的相关文档常共享粗粒度前缀、在细粒度分支上分化，且分支模式随查询变化。标准全SID监督将这些路径视为独立训练目标，未显式刻画相关性在逐层细化中的分配方式。

Method: 将多分辨率相关性建模为单一文档级相关性度量在SID层级上诱导的一致条件分布；RARS在训练中按前缀聚合文档相关性，训练前缀条件预测器在兄弟分支间分配相关性，所有携带相关性的子节点参与局部竞争，每个局部损失由其父节点到达的相关性质量加权；该目标用于训练查询编码器，推理时丢弃预测器，保持标准自回归检索。

Result: 在三个多语言ESCI区域设置上，相比匹配的全SID训练在自回归解码下取得一致性提升；在统一检索规则下优于分组软目标、解码器软目标和采样树监督；增益在替代标识符结构与相关性定义下依然保持。

Conclusion: 通过将相关性分配显式对齐到SID分辨率层级，RARS为生成式检索提供了一种更有效的监督方式，且不改变推理流程，具有较好的鲁棒性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Generative retrieval with semantic identifiers (SIDs) makes successive decisions over a document hierarchy. Relevant documents for the same query may share coarse prefixes and diverge at finer depths, with branching patterns varying across queries. These paths reveal how relevance is distributed across successive refinements, yet standard full-SID supervision treats them as separate training targets. To make this allocation explicit, we formulate multiresolution relevance as consistent conditional distributions induced by a single document-level relevance measure across the SID hierarchy. We introduce \textbf{RARS}, \textbf{R}esolution-\textbf{A}ligned \textbf{R}elevance \textbf{S}upervision, which uses the resulting refinement-level distributions to supervise a shared query representation. RARS aggregates document relevance over prefixes and trains a prefix-conditioned predictor to allocate relevance among sibling branches. All relevance-bearing children participate in local competition, and each local loss is weighted by the relevance mass reaching its parent. This objective trains the query encoder to capture both the coarse structure shared by relevant documents and their finer branch allocations. The predictor is discarded after training, preserving standard autoregressive retrieval at inference. Experiments on three multilingual ESCI locales show consistent improvements over matched full-SID training under autoregressive decoding. RARS also outperforms grouped soft-target, decoder soft-target, and sampled-tree supervision under a common retrieval rule. The gains persist across alternative identifier structures and relevance definitions. Code is available at: https://github.com/Nevaeh7/RARS

</details>


### [293] [Residual Trajectory Distillation for Generative Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39319)
*Weihao Shen,Wei Chen,Fuwei Zhang,Guojun Liu,Qingsong Hua,Wei Lin,Fuzhen Zhuang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出ResTD框架，将RQ构建语义ID时被丢弃的残差轨迹信息通过蒸馏方式引入生成式检索训练，在多语言电商检索上取得稳定提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式检索用离散语义ID（SID）表示物品并自回归生成检索。当SID由残差量化（RQ）构建时，标准训练只监督选中的码字，丢弃了产生它们的残差轨迹。相同硬码字可能来自对不同竞争码字的不同偏好，而残差轨迹还包含后续量化决策的信息。这导致硬SID监督把不同的量化行为坍缩为相同目标，浪费了索引阶段可用的信息。

Method: 提出ResTD（残差轨迹蒸馏）框架：把冻结的RQ索引器视为"过程教师"，将残差引起的码字偏好蒸馏到SID解码状态中。该监督恢复了被硬分配隐藏的区分信息，使较早的解码状态在生成对应SID后缀前就能捕获后续量化决策的信息，同时保持原有检索索引与推理流程不变。

Result: 在多语言电商检索实验中，相比强基线和匹配的训练对照组取得一致提升。受控比较显示，残差导出的目标优于所测试的仅码本软目标；表征探针进一步表明，未来码本偏好可从更早的解码状态中被更好地恢复。

Conclusion: ResTD能从SID构建中引入更丰富的信息到检索学习中，在不改变索引和推理的前提下提升效果，并可方便地扩展到生成式推荐场景。

Abstract: Generative retrieval has emerged as a general retrieval paradigm, representing items with discrete Semantic IDs (SIDs) and retrieving them through autoregressive identifier generation. When SIDs are constructed with residual quantization (RQ), standard retrieval training supervises only the selected codes and discards the residual trajectories that produce them. The same hard code can nevertheless arise from different preferences over competing codewords, while the residual trajectory also contains information about subsequent quantization decisions. As a result, hard SID supervision collapses distinct quantization behaviors into identical targets and leaves information available during indexing unused in retrieval training. We introduce ResTD, a Residual Trajectory Distillation framework that transfers this discarded indexing information into retrieval training. Treating the frozen RQ indexer as a process teacher, it distills residual-induced codeword preferences into SID-decoding states. This supervision recovers distinctions hidden by hard assignments and allows earlier decoder states to capture information about subsequent quantization decisions before the corresponding SID suffix is generated. In this way, richer information from SID construction is incorporated into retrieval learning while preserving the original retrieval index and inference procedure. Experiments on multilingual e-commerce retrieval show consistent improvements over strong baselines and matched training controls. Controlled comparisons show that residual-derived targets outperform the tested codebook-only soft targets. Representation probes further show that future codebook preferences become more recoverable from earlier decoder states. ResTD can also be readily extended beyond retrieval to generative recommendation. Code is available at: https://github.com/Nevaeh7/iclr2027_ResTD.git.

</details>


### [294] [Generative End-to-end Ad Retrieval at Douyin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39327)
*Shaowen Zeng,Yanhua Huang,Jiacheng Sun,Jiarui Liu,Qian Dai,Zhikai Yang,Hancheng Li,Boya Wu,Tuoyu Zhang,Yekui Chen,Xiang Sun*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出GEAR框架，通过联合优化tokenizer、生成器和重排器，解决生成式检索中的表示塌缩与物品碰撞问题，并已在抖音广告落地服务数亿日活用户。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式检索将推荐转化为离散物品token的生成，但扩展到真实推荐系统时面临两大瓶颈：一是连续分布漂移下物品tokenizer表示塌缩，阻碍端到端稳定适配；二是海量候选池导致不同物品共享相同token序列（物品碰撞），损害检索精度。二者相互耦合——扩大码本容量缓解碰撞会加剧塌缩。因此需要同时解决这两个问题。

Method: 提出端到端框架GEAR，联合优化tokenizer、生成器与重排器。针对表示塌缩，设计BasisVQ，用正交基对码本进行重参数化，实现全局梯度共享与潜在空间的刚性空间旋转，无需启发式即可稳定梯度动态；并进一步扩展为前缀感知的BasisRQ，在相同渐近时间复杂度下增强码本表达能力。针对物品碰撞，将上下文条件重排头集成到生成过程中，以极小计算开销区分碰撞物品。

Result: 统一了稳定tokenization与联合重排在端到端生成框架内，形成完全可微、可扩展的范式；在抖音广告服务数亿日活用户，并在大量在线A/B测试中取得显著经验提升。

Conclusion: GEAR通过BasisVQ/BasisRQ缓解表示塌缩、通过上下文条件重排头解决物品碰撞，实现了可微且可扩展的生成式检索范式，并在大规模真实系统中验证了有效性。

Abstract: Generative retrieval reformulates recommendation as the generation of discrete item tokens. However, scaling this paradigm to real-world recommender systems reveals two critical bottlenecks: 1) Representation collapse, where the item tokenizer converges to degenerate results under continuous distribution shifts, fundamentally hindering stable end-to-end adaptation. 2) Item collisions, where the massive candidate pool causes distinct items to share identical token sequences, compromising the final retrieval precision. Crucially, these bottlenecks are inherently coupled: expanding codebook capacity to mitigate collisions inevitably exacerbates collapse. To address them simultaneously, we propose GEAR, an end-to-end framework that jointly optimizes the tokenizer, generator, and reranker. To mitigate representation collapse, we introduce BasisVQ, which re-parameterizes the codebook via an orthogonal basis to enable global gradient sharing and rigid spatial rotation of the latent space, effectively stabilizing gradient dynamics without ad-hoc heuristics. We further extend it to prefix-aware BasisRQ, substantially enhancing the codebook's expressiveness with the same asymptotic time complexity. To resolve item collisions, GEAR integrates a context-conditioned reranking head into the generative process, efficiently disambiguating colliding items with minimal computational overhead. By unifying stable tokenization and joint reranking within an end-to-end generative framework, GEAR establishes a fully differentiable and scalable paradigm. It currently serves hundreds of millions of daily active users on Douyin Ads, yielding substantial empirical improvements in extensive online A/B tests.

</details>


### [295] [When the Label Ignores the Request: Auditing Policy-Selected Targets in Synthetic Conversational Music Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39696)
*Sanjeev Suresh*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 合成对话基准中官方标签常与用户明确的点歌请求矛盾，作者审计该问题并提出训练期补充目标的方法来缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 生成的合成对话已成为完整的会话推荐基准，其标签由模拟策略选出，便于大规模可复现评估，但这些标签只是"模拟用户实际请求"的代理。作者希望审计标签与真实请求之间的偏差，尤其是在用户明确点名某首歌曲的场景。

Method: 在 RecSys Challenge 2026 TalkPlay 基准上，仅依赖可见对话与目录元数据，对用户点名精确歌曲的回合进行审计，比较官方标签与用户请求是否一致；随后提出一种训练期补充方法：在一小部分训练回合中加入"经目录解析、满足请求"的目标，并与一个能识别相同请求但仅用官方标签训练的匹配对照组进行比较验证。

Result: 在审计的开发集回合中，有一半的官方标签与用户的精确点歌请求相矛盾。加入请求满足型目标后，在 43 个冲突回合上 nDCG@20 取得 53.3% 的相对提升，同时官方指标保持不变；匹配对照组验证了该增益来源于补充目标而非仅仅识别了请求。

Conclusion: 合成对话基准中的策略选择标签会系统性地偏离用户真实意图，尤其在点名精确歌曲这一现实音乐搜索的主导意图上；通过在训练期补充目录解析的请求满足目标，可在不损害官方指标的前提下显著改善冲突回合的推荐表现。

Abstract: Synthetic dialogues generated by LLM pipelines now serve as complete conversational-recommendation benchmarks: an LLM listener talks to an LLM recommender, and the track logged next in the conversation becomes the official label for each turn. These policy-selected labels make large-scale evaluation reproducible, but they are proxies for what the simulated user asked. We audit the one place where label and request are directly comparable: turns where the user asks for an exact song by name. In the RecSys Challenge 2026 TalkPlay benchmark, using visible dialogue and catalog metadata alone, we find that the official label contradicts the user's exact-song request in half of the audited development turns. This matters beyond one benchmark: naming the desired item is the dominant intent in real music search, where deployed systems avoid substituting an alternative for an exactly named item, on the premise that it costs satisfaction. A small training-time supplement closes most of the gap: adding catalog-resolved request-satisfying targets to a small fraction of training turns yields a 53.3% relative gain in nDCG@20 on the 43 conflict turns while leaving the official metric intact, verified against a matched control that detects the same requests but trains only on official labels.

</details>


### [296] [KUAISHOU Explorer LLM-Rec Challenge 2026: Reasoning Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39828)
*Jiangxia Cao,Hao Peng,Wenlong Xu,Jiaxin Deng,Zhixin Ling,Xingmei Wang,Kun Shang,Can Tang,Zhihuai Cai,Jun Du,Fang Su,Xiaojuan Liu,Yiling Li,Chenglong Yu,Chongling Rao,Haixuan Gao,Haitao Xu,Jian Liang,Ruiming Tang,Chenglong Chu,Guohong Mu,Honghui Bao,Hui Wang,Jialong Chen,Jiao Ou,Muhao Wei,Peng Zhang,Renpu Liu,Ruochen Yang,Shugui Liu,Xinqi Jin,Yan Sun,Yifan Wang,Yingzhi He,Yufei Ye,Yusen Huo,Tingkuo Wang,Jihong Zhang,Lanxi Zhu,Pengyuan Liu,Zhipeng Yi,Luankang Zhang,Hang Lv,Xuyang Zhi,Tianyu Li,Bintao Wu,Chuang Ou,Siyue Su,Ziyuan Wang,Yuliang Sun,Baiyan Che,Feiyang Xu,Shiwen Zhang,Shiteng Cao,Chongcong Jiang,Yuan Fang,Xiangwu Yang,Hao Deng,Zijian Du,Pengxun Wang,Xiaoming Wang,Shun Qin,Yingqi Song,Tianyi Li,Naixiao Peng,Chenyu Zhou,Qiliang Jiang,Quan Zheng,Cheng Jin,Siying Zeng,Hongjia Xu,Junwu Hu,Teng Fu,Zhengkang Mei,Haijun Yu,Kai Li,Shengyang Zhou,Zhijia Wei,Siyi Xiong,Bo Liu,Zichun Guo,Zhubin Han,Jinpeng Fu,Bingqian Liu,Yuyi Wang,Yu Liu,Qinghai Tan,Ruijie Zhou,Zhuohang Li,Zhijia Zhong,Xiangnan He,Jirong Wen,Min Zhang,Wenwu Ou,Peng Jiang,Han Li,Kaiqiao Zhan,Yanan Niu,Lantao Hu,Kun Gai*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了快手在生成式推荐方向的一系列工作（OneRec/OneRec-V2及后续OneRec-Think、OpenOneRec、OneReason），并宣布与SIGIR 2026合作举办KUAISHOU Explorer LLM-Rec Challenge 2026，主题为"推理生成式推荐"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐受到工业界和学术界广泛关注，在大语言模型发展浪潮下，希望构建下一代智能推荐系统。团队的前期探索发现，引入推理链（CoT）并不总能提升推荐性能，因此需要研究如何让推理真正有益于推荐。

Method: 基于Semantic ID的OneRec/OneRec-V2（自回归下一物品预测范式）；进一步提出OneRec-Think、OpenOneRec、OneReason，将物品语义ID与自然语言统一到同一表示空间；OneReason通过增强物品与语言的语义对齐、引入结构化模板监督进行兴趣推理、并应用先进强化学习技术。

Result: 这些模型已在生产环境中广泛部署，展示了自回归下一物品预测范式在工业推荐系统中的扩展潜力；但初步研究表明引入推理CoT并不总是提升推荐性能。

Conclusion: 作为构建推荐基础模型的前沿课题，该方向具有重要研究价值。为此，团队与SIGIR 2026社区共同组织了KUAISHOU Explorer LLM-Rec Challenge 2026: Reasoning Generative Recommendation，希望鼓励更多研究者共同探索。

Abstract: Generative recommendation, has been attracted a surge of attentions in industrial and academic research community, towards to build more smart system to build next-generation recommender. Under the significant developing wave of large language model, our team have been developed Semantic ID based OneRec/OneRec-V2. These models have been widely deployed in production and demonstrate the scaling potential of the autoregressive next-item prediction paradigm for industrial recommender systems. Building on the success of OneRec, we further explored a series of models, including OneRec-Think, OpenOneRec, and OneReason, that connect item Semantic IDs with natural language in a unified representation space and seek to unlock the potential of natural-language chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning for recommendation. However, our preliminary works found that introducing reasoning CoT does not always improve the recommendation performance. To address this issue, OneReason strengthens the semantic alignment between items and language, introduces structured template-based supervision for interest reasoning, and applies advanced reinforcement learning techniques to make reasoning more beneficial to recommendation. As a frontier topic to building recommendation foundation models, we believe this topic has significant research value and hope to encourage more researchers to explore it together. To this end, together with the SIGIR 2026 community, we organized the KUAISHOU Explorer LLM-Rec Challenge 2026: Reasoning Generative Recommendation.

</details>


### [297] [Decision-Oriented Recommendation Reranking: An Empirical Study of Jev](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40241)
*Hanjia Lyu,Yinglong Xia*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文对决策导向模型Jev在个性化推荐重排序任务中进行了受控实证研究，比较其与推荐专用模型及Qwen重排器在效果与延迟上的表现，发现Jev处于独特的质量-延迟工作区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在推荐重排序中展现出潜力，但存在推荐质量与服务效率之间的权衡。作者希望探究当重排序本质上是预定义候选项之间的结构化选择时，决策导向模型是否能提供有用的替代方案。

Method: 在多个Amazon Reviews领域和不同候选集规模下，对TypeSafe AI描述的"System One Model"——Jev进行控制性实证研究，并与推荐专用模型以及pointwise和listwise Qwen重排器进行对比，评估推荐效果和实际服务延迟。

Result: Jev在推荐效果上相对所评估的基线保持较强水平，其延迟增长比pointwise Qwen重排器更平缓，但实际服务延迟仍明显高于推荐专用模型。这些特性使Jev在不同候选规模和领域下处于独特的质量-延迟工作区间。

Conclusion: 这些发现为将决策导向模型应用于推荐及其他具有结构化输出空间的排序任务提供了进一步研究的动机。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown promise for recommendation reranking, but their use introduces an important tradeoff between recommendation quality and serving efficiency. We investigate whether a decision-oriented model provides a useful alternative when the reranking task is fundamentally a structured choice among predefined candidate items. Specifically, we conduct a controlled empirical study of Jev, described by TypeSafe AI as a ``System One Model,'' for personalized recommendation reranking and compare it with recommendation-specific models and pointwise and listwise Qwen rerankers across multiple Amazon Reviews domains and candidate-set sizes, evaluating both recommendation effectiveness and observed serving latency. Our results show that Jev maintains strong recommendation effectiveness relative to the evaluated baselines while exhibiting substantially more gradual latency growth than the pointwise Qwen rerankers, although its observed serving latency remains substantially higher than that of recommendation-specific models. Together, these characteristics place Jev in a distinct quality--latency operating regime across candidate sizes and domains. These findings motivate further investigation of decision-oriented models for recommendation and other ranking tasks with structured output spaces.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [298] [How Should Diffusion Language Models Edit Code?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38257)
*Xijia Tao,Ziru Liu,Shansan Gong,Jiacheng Ye,Kecheng Chen,Zirui Wu,Lin Zheng,Xinyu Fu,Rui Liu,Lingpeng Kong*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究将代码编辑分解为"定位—生成—保留"三项职责，通过四种编辑界面实验揭示掩码扩散语言模型存在"组合鸿沟"：给定正确编辑位置时模型能生成协调修改，但需自行预测位置时能力大幅丧失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码编辑需要模型决定改哪里、生成新内容并保留其余部分。作者想探究掩码扩散语言模型如何在这些职责间分配能力，以及为何强大的填充（infilling）能力未必能保证可靠的编辑表现。

Method: 在CanItEdit上比较四种编辑界面：整文件重写、搜索替换、先定位再填充、token级编辑。在固定生成模型与解码过程的前提下，仅改变可编辑区域，以分离"覆盖所有必要修改"与"边界精确定位"两种需求；并补充一个句子级Wiki编辑探针，检验该现象是否超出代码领域。

Result: 实验发现明显的组合鸿沟：提供正确编辑位置时扩散模型可生成协调的多处修改，但需自行预测位置时大部分能力丧失。接触完整原始代码有助于填充多个编辑区域，却不能解决选区定位难题。遗漏必要区域会阻止相应修改，而为确保覆盖而放宽区域会因强制重生成未变代码而显著降低成功率。Wiki探针复现了同样的定性鸿沟。

Conclusion: 仅靠强填充能力不足以保证可靠编辑：编辑界面必须同时暴露所有必要修改，并限制对未变代码的重新生成。定位与生成是两类相互独立的要求，界面设计需兼顾二者。

Abstract: Code editing requires a model to decide where to make changes, generate the new content, and preserve everything else. We study how masked diffusion language models divide these responsibilities across four editing interfaces: whole-file rewriting, search-and-replace, locate-then-infill, and token-level editing. Experiments on CanItEdit reveal a composition gap: diffusion models can generate coordinated changes when the correct edit locations are supplied, but much of this capability is lost when those locations must be predicted. Access to the intact original code helps the model fill multiple edit regions, yet does not resolve the difficulty of selecting those regions. By varying the editable regions while holding the generation model and decoding procedure fixed, we identify two distinct requirements for successful editing: covering every required change and placing precise boundaries around it. Missing a required region prevents the corresponding change, while widening regions to ensure coverage can sharply reduce success by requiring unchanged code to be regenerated. A sentence-level Wiki editing probe shows the same qualitative gap between supplied and predicted locations beyond code. These findings show why strong infilling capability alone does not ensure reliable editing: the interface must expose all required changes while limiting regeneration of unchanged code.

</details>


### [299] [Zero2Repo: Can Coding Agents Build Repositories from Scratch?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38269)
*Pei Yang,Tianyu Shi,Yuhang Yao,Wanyi Chen,Tongyun Yang,Dun Pei,Haonan Wang,Pengbin Feng,Guanxu Yu,Jingchun Huang,Zeyu Zhang,Shuhan Sun,Hao Li,Xiang Li,Jie Xiao,Xinyu Wang,Hanxin Chen,Daqi Li,Qi Jia,Hongshan Lin,Zhizhou Gu,Zijun Tian,Weizhi Du,Lynn Ai,Eric Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Zero2Repo是一个跨语言、全自动构建的"从零建库"编码智能体基准：由真实开源项目自动生成需求文档、接口契约与隐藏验收测试，评测显示最强智能体仅能解出10/11题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有"从零构建代码库"的基准大多局限于单一编程语言，且依赖人工策划任务，难以规模化和跨生态评估编码智能体的真实工程能力。

Method: 提出语言无关的自动出题流水线：将版本固定的真实开源项目转化为行为规格、可复现环境和隐藏验收测试；每个任务经执行校验（参考实现必须通过，对抗性验证拒绝错误实现）。评测时在隔离容器中运行生产级编码智能体，提交前不透露验收测试，仅当全部测试通过才给二元奖励，不使用LLM评审。

Result: 流水线与评测框架不依赖特定语言，覆盖Python、TypeScript、Go、C++等主流生态。即使在11个很可能被前沿模型在训练中见过的仓库任务上，最强智能体也仅解出10个；所有失败提交通过90–99%的隐藏测试。

Conclusion: 对两个最强智能体，67–100%的失败测试可归因于单一疏漏或规格中低频规则，而非缺失子系统，因此每次失败都是具体、可改进的目标；Zero2Repo为跨语言从零建库提供了可扩展的严格评测基准。

Abstract: Coding agents are increasingly asked to build software rather than patch it, yet benchmarks for from-scratch repository construction are mostly limited to a single language and depend on manually curated tasks. We introduce Zero2Repo, a benchmark in which an agent receives a product requirements document, an interface contract, and an empty workspace, and must deliver a complete repository in the project's native ecosystem. Tasks are produced by a language-agnostic authoring pipeline that converts real, version-pinned open-source projects into behavioral specifications, reproducible environments, and hidden acceptance tests. Each task is validated by execution: a reference implementation derived from the upstream project must pass, and adversarial validation must show that the tests reject incorrect implementations. Evaluation runs production coding agents in isolated containers, withholds the acceptance tests until an explicit submission, and assigns a binary reward only when every test passes, with no LLM judge. The pipeline and harness make no language-specific assumptions and apply to mainstream programming ecosystems; the current release contains Python, TypeScript, Go, and C++ tasks. Even on 11 tasks drawn from repositories that frontier models have very likely seen during training, the strongest agent solves only 10, and every failing submission passes 90-99% of the hidden tests; for the two strongest agents, 67-100% of failed tests trace to a single omission or a low-frequency rule stated in the specification rather than to a missing subsystem, so each failure is a concrete target for improvement.

</details>


### [300] [E2E-SWE: Benchmarking LLMs on Building Working Codebases from Scratch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38335)
*Hantian Ding,Chloe Bi,Jiacheng Zhu,John Yang,Matt Deitke,Pengcheng Yin,Zijian Wang,Rui Hou*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 E2E-SWE 基准，用于评估编码智能体能否从零构建完整可运行的软件仓库。该基准包含 186 个全仓库生成任务，覆盖 11 种编程语言，仅提供自然语言规格说明和空工作区。13 个前沿模型的 pass@1 在 11.7% 到 67.7% 之间，显示出较强的模型区分度和较大的提升空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码智能体的评估多聚焦于局部代码修改，而仓库级别的完整生成评估面临挑战：任务需考察系统级推理能力，同时必须保证所评估行为被精确规定且不依赖特定实现。为此需要一个新的基准来评测端到端的仓库构建能力。

Method: 构建 E2E-SWE 基准，含 186 个全仓库生成任务，跨 11 种编程语言。每个任务由软件工程师与 LLM 协作完成，开发隐藏测试套件及与实现无关的规格说明。任务还需经过迭代验证流程，由自主智能体通过静态检查和真实模型运行失败来审计并修复任务缺陷。

Result: 对 13 个前沿模型的评测显示，端到端仓库生成能力差异显著，pass@1 从 11.7% 到 67.7%，具备较强模型区分度并留有较大提升空间。智能体轨迹分析揭示了冗长且前置的推理模式，体现出从零构建可用代码库所需的规划与系统级推理能力。

Conclusion: E2E-SWE 为仓库级端到端代码生成提供了有效且具区分度的评测基准，表明当前模型在此任务上仍有较大进步空间，同时强调了规划与系统级推理在完整软件构建中的关键作用。

Abstract: Coding agents powered by large language models (LLMs) are evolving from making localized code changes to developing complete software repositories. However, evaluating repository-scale generation remains challenging: tasks must demand system-level reasoning while ensuring that all evaluated behaviors are precisely specified and independent of any particular implementation. We introduce E2E-SWE, a benchmark for evaluating whether coding agents can build complete, functional software repositories end to end. E2E-SWE contains 186 whole-repository generation tasks spanning 11 programming languages. Given only a natural-language specification and an empty workspace, an agent must implement a complete, installable project that satisfies a comprehensive suite of hidden tests. Each task is constructed by a software engineer in collaboration with an LLM; together, they develop the test suite and a corresponding implementation-independent specification. To ensure that tasks are well specified and practically solvable, we further subject them to an iterative verification process in which autonomous agents audit and repair task defects using static inspection and failures observed from real model rollouts. Evaluating 13 frontier models, we find substantial variation in end-to-end repository generation ability, with pass@1 ranging from 11.7% to 67.7%, providing strong model differentiation while leaving considerable headroom for future progress. Analysis of agent trajectories further reveals long, front-loaded reasoning patterns, highlighting the planning and system-level reasoning required to construct working codebases from scratch.

</details>


### [301] [OpenCollab: A Multi-Agent Coding Framework with Programmable Collaboration and Controllable Runtime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38345)
*Chun-Wah Hsu,Kai Gong,Yu Wu,Xianhe Chen,Mengyang Liu,Jie Li,Hanyu Li,Zhixuan Liu,Naisheng Tang,Jiaying Chi,Ziheng Fan,Xuning He,Xiaokang Yang,Xue Jiang,Yihong Dong*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 OpenCollab 多智能体编码框架，通过统一基础设施实现可编程协作与可控运行时评估，并定义 Adherence 指标量化声明组织是否被真正执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估多假设已配置的组织会被忠实执行，但现实并非如此；这种行为差距加上底层系统组件的差异，使得观察到的性能提升难以清晰归因。

Method: OpenCollab 统一组织设计，在共享运行时上施加实验控制，并通过细粒度事件流追踪执行过程；在此基础上定义 Adherence 指标，用以衡量声明组织是否真正落地。

Result: 实验显示，智能体在不同配置下协作方式差异显著，改变任一维度都会改变 Adherence，范围从 47.2% 到最高 97.2%；在多项智能体编码基准上，基于 OpenCollab 的双编码器工作流相比 Mini-SWE-agent、Codex CLI、Claude Code 等主流框架达到新的 SOTA，且单智能体配置在所有评估套件中使用的 token 最少。

Conclusion: OpenCollab 为多智能体系统提供了统一基础设施，支持便捷的可编程协作与受控的因果评估，并表明精心设计的组织可以超越强基线框架。

Abstract: Multi-agent coding systems are designed to tackle complex software engineering tasks through collaboration. However, existing evaluations typically assume configured organizations are followed faithfully, whereas reality differs. This behavioral gap, combined with differences in underlying system components, prevents clear attribution of observed gains. To this end, we introduce OpenCollab, a multi-agent coding framework that provides a unified infrastructure for programmable collaboration and controllable runtime. Specifically, OpenCollab unifies organization design, enforces experimental control on a shared runtime, and tracks execution through fine-grained event streams. On this basis, we define Adherence to quantify whether the declared organization is actually realized. Our experiments reveal that agents collaborate very differently across configurations: changing any single dimension shifts Adherence, from 47.2% to as high as 97.2%. Furthermore, extensive agentic coding benchmarks show that a two-coder workflow built on OpenCollab establishes new SOTA performance compared to the mainstream harnesses such as Mini-SWE-agent, Codex CLI, and Claude Code, showing that a well-designed organization can outperform strong existing harnesses, while OpenCollab's single-agent configuration uses the fewest tokens across all evaluated suites. OpenCollab establishes a unified multi-agent infrastructure for easy programmable collaboration and controlled causal evaluation.

</details>


### [302] [From Codebase to Culprit (C2C): Reducing the Search Space for Bugs with Semantic Retrieval and Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38402)
*Ankur Garg,Corey Yang-Smith,Rishav Rishav,Ahmad Abdellatif,Samira Ebrahimi Kahou*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: C2C 是一个从代码库到缺陷源的多粒度缺陷定位框架，融合语义检索与分层强化学习，逐步将调试搜索空间从文件缩小到函数再到代码行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有缺陷定位方法通常只在单一粒度上操作，缺乏跨粒度的一致性，导致搜索空间大、定位不精确，难以贴合开发者自顶向下的调试流程。

Method: 采用两阶段流程：第一阶段用基于缺陷报告文本（含栈追踪信息）的语义向量相似度检索，结合 CodeBERT 对比学习微调嵌入，进行面向召回率的缺陷候选召回；第二阶段在缩小后的搜索空间上使用分层强化学习，从文件到函数再到代码行逐级定位，保持跨决策的上下文一致性。

Result: 在真实 Java 和 Python 数据集上的实验表明，C2C 提升了检索精度与定位准确性；消融研究进一步显示分层分解、结构化学习信号与奖励塑形对多层级缺陷定位均有贡献。

Conclusion: C2C 通过语义检索与分层强化学习的结合，实现了跨文件、函数、代码行的一致多分辨率缺陷定位，优于单粒度方法。

Abstract: We introduce C2C (From Codebase to Culprit), a framework for precise bug localization that progressively reduces the debugging search space across multiple levels of granularity: files, functions, and lines of code. To mirror developer's natural top-down debugging workflows, C2C integrates semantic retrieval and Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning (HRL) in a two-stage process. First, it performs recall-oriented retrieval of buggy candidates via semantic vector similarity search using bug-report text, including available stack-trace information, against a database of embeddings, where the embeddings are fine-tuned via contrastive learning with CodeBERT. Building on this reduced search space, the HRL framework incrementally localizes bugs, reasoning from files to functions and ultimately to individual lines of code. Unlike prior approaches which operate at a single granularity, C2C enables multi-resolution localization while maintaining contextual consistency across decisions. Experiments on real-world Java and Python datasets demonstrate that C2C improves retrieval precision and localization accuracy. Ablation studies further highlight the contributions of hierarchical decomposition, structured learning signals, and reward shaping in advancing multi-level bug localization.

</details>


### [303] [MallocSan: A Memory Safety Tool for Native Closed-Source Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38499)
*Adel Belkhiri,Michel Dagenais,Maxime Lamothe*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: MallocSan是一种针对x86-64 Linux闭源应用的堆内存安全检测工具，无需源码、重编译或专用硬件，通过LD_PRELOAD拦截分配并用非规范指针触发陷阱，实现运行时补丁与按对象边界检查，性能显著优于Valgrind Memcheck。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: C/C++内存破坏错误是高危漏洞的主要来源，但现有确定性检测方法部署困难：编译期sanitizer需要源码和构建控制，硬件方案依赖特定平台，动态二进制翻译则带来数量级的性能开销。因此需要一种无需源码、无需重编译、不依赖专用硬件且性能可接受的堆检测方案。

Method: MallocSan通过LD_PRELOAD拦截内存分配，在被保护指针未使用的高位嵌入对象标识符；解引用产生的非规范指针会在出错指令处触发陷阱，MallocSan解码并在运行时patch该指令，使后续执行在用户态完成按对象边界检查；无法patch的站点回退到handler内模拟或单步执行，并可选profile-guided离线重写高频残留站点；同时将基于标识的检查扩展到向量gather/scatter指令，并支持策略范围内的覆盖。

Result: 在七个SPEC CPU 2017基准测试中，MallocSan在五项上优于Valgrind Memcheck，几何平均执行时间倍率为原生执行的4.82倍，而Memcheck为18.55倍，并在644.nab_s和pigz两个多线程负载上保持了良好的并行扩展性。在范围内的Juliet测试中，MallocSan检测出全部注入违规且对正常执行无误报，并在真实应用中发现堆缓冲区错误，包括LibTIFF的tiffcrop工具中已知的一字节越读。

Conclusion: MallocSan证明了无需源码、重编译或专用硬件即可对x86-64 Linux原生（含闭源）应用进行确定性堆内存安全检测，在保持可接受性能开销和并行扩展性的同时，具备高检测率和低误报率，并能发现真实软件中的内存错误。

Abstract: Memory-corruption errors remain a leading cause of high-impact vulnerabilities in C and C++ software. Deterministic detection, however, remains difficult to deploy: compiler-based sanitizers require source code and control of the build pipeline, hardware-assisted schemes depend on specific platforms, and dynamic binary translation can impose order-of-magnitude slowdowns. This paper presents MallocSan, a heap sanitizer for native, potentially closed-source x86-64 Linux applications that requires no source access, recompilation, or specialized hardware. MallocSan interposes on memory allocation through LD_PRELOAD and embeds an object identifier in the unused high bits of each protected pointer. Dereferencing the resulting noncanonical pointer faults at the offending instruction, which MallocSan decodes and patches at runtime so that subsequent executions perform per-object bounds checks entirely in userspace. Sites that cannot be patched fall back to in-handler emulation or single-stepping, while an optional profile-guided pass rewrites frequently executed residual sites offline. MallocSan also extends identity-based checking to vector gather/scatter instructions and supports policy-scoped coverage. Across seven SPEC CPU 2017 benchmarks, MallocSan outperforms Valgrind Memcheck on five, with a geometric-mean execution-time factor of 4.82x relative to native execution, compared with 18.55x for Memcheck, while preserving substantial parallel scaling on both evaluated multithreaded workloads: 644.nab_s and pigz. On the in-scope Juliet tests, MallocSan detects all seeded violations with no false reports on the good executions. It also detects heap-buffer errors in real-world applications, including a known one-byte overread in LibTIFF's tiffcrop utility.

</details>


### [304] [An Empirical Study of Architectural Shift from Traditional to AI-Enabled Simulink Controllers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38504)
*Hadiza Umar Yusuf,Khouloud Gaaloul*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过对62个真实Simulink控制器模型与13位从业者的实证研究，揭示了传统与AI赋能控制器架构之间的三大张力，指出AI控制器中安全约束从显式结构转为隐式训练产物，破坏了可追溯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI赋能组件正日益取代基于解析推导的控制律，但业界对传统控制架构与AI赋能控制架构之间究竟有何差异、又有何共性缺乏系统性理解，导致设计知识难以有效嵌入工程实践，阻碍了AI在信息物理系统（CPS）中的有效落地。

Method: 构建了由文献推导的分类体系，包含十个结构类别和九种功能角色；在此基础上对传统与AI赋能的Simulink控制器开展实证研究，分析了横跨8种控制器类型、10个应用领域的62个真实世界模型，并调研了13位从业者。

Result: 识别出三大架构张力：其一，子系统组织在所有控制器结构中均占主导，占控制器足迹的68%-72%，而核心控制逻辑仅占极小空间，且与范式无关；其二，AI赋能控制器高度依赖离散动力学和用户自定义抽象，这些类别在AI文献中基本缺失，暴露出所描述架构与所实现架构之间的鸿沟；其三，尽管从业者预期存在约束执行模块，但它们在AI赋能模型中大量消失。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了一种错位：安全机制从显式结构转变为隐式的训练期产物，从而破坏了可追溯性。这表明当前AI控制器的实现架构与文献描述及工程预期脱节，亟需建立系统化的架构理解以支撑CPS中安全可靠的AI应用。

Abstract: Effective AI adoption in cyber-physical systems (CPS) depends on embedding design knowledge into engineering practice. Yet as AI-enabled components increasingly replace analytically derived control laws, this occurs without a systematic understanding of how controller architectures differ or remain similar across paradigms. We address this gap with an empirical study of traditional and AI-enabled Simulink controllers, guided by a literature-derived taxonomy of ten structural categories and nine functional roles. The study analyzes 62 real-world models spanning 8 controller types and 10 application domains, and surveys 13 practitioners, identifying three architectural tensions. First, subsystem organization dominates all controller structures regardless of paradigm, occupying 68-72% of controller footprint, while core control logic occupies minimal space. Second, AI-enabled controllers rely heavily on discrete dynamics and user-defined abstraction, categories largely absent from AI literature, exposing a gap between described and implemented architectures. Third, constraint enforcement blocks largely disappear from AI-enabled models despite practitioner expectations. This reveals a misalignment where safety mechanisms shift from explicit structure to implicit training-time artifacts, breaking traceability.

</details>


### [305] [Adaptive-GEPA: Make Your Harness Fit Heterogeneous Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38762)
*Tianyu Chen,Yasi Zhang,Ruiyi Wang,Xinran Zhao,Taoran Li,Mingyuan Zhou*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 Adaptive-GEPA，在统一搜索预算下同时进化路由器和专家程序库，实现请求划分与求解的联合优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户向同一端点提交异构请求，其有效解法需要不同工具、推理模式和控制流。优化单个共享程序会把分工隐含在源码搜索中，而为每类请求单独优化程序则预先固定了分工，二者都不理想。

Method: Adaptive-GEPA 进化一个路由器与专家程序库；路由指令、专家描述与程序代码均为可由反馈编辑的可读文本；通过对齐各专家所处理的请求来合并分支，并连同程序继承描述。

Result: 在四个任务族的固定混合集上，Qwen3-8B 运行进化出四个专家，且未向路由器或反思模型提供族标签，其路由在全部 651 条测试请求上与任务划分一致；族平均测试分数（×100）从 52.6 升至 70.6，高于 GEPA 全程序适配器的 62.5 和 GRPO 的 54.0（名义预算 18,000 次计分调用，该计数不等于总计算量）。

Conclusion: 联合学习请求划分与求解可自动涌现专家分工并显著提升性能，但调用次数不等同于总算力，需谨慎比较成本。

Abstract: Reflective optimizers such as GEPA improve language model prompts from execution traces and evaluator feedback; full-program extensions can also rewrite tools and control flow. In practice, a user hands the same endpoint heterogeneous requests whose effective solutions require different tools, reasoning modes, and control flow. Optimizing one shared program leaves this division of work implicit in source-code search, while optimizing a separate program per request family fixes it beforehand.
  We introduce Adaptive-GEPA, which learns both how to divide requests and how to solve them. It evolves a router and a library of specialist programs under one search budget. The router's instructions, each specialist's description, and its program code are plain, human-readable text, edited from feedback. To combine branches, it aligns specialists by the requests they handle and inherits descriptions together with programs. On a fixed mixture of four task families, the reported Qwen3-8B run evolves four experts without supplying family labels to the router or reflection model; its routing matches the task partition on all 651 test requests. Its family-mean test score (x100) rises from 52.6 to 70.6, compared with 62.5 for GEPA's full-program adapter and 54.0 for GRPO at a nominal budget of 18,000 scored calls. These counts do not equate total compute. Figure 1 summarizes the learning curves, final test scores, and routing agreement.

</details>


### [306] [Toward Quantum Software Automation: A Quantum-Aware Harness for LLM-Guided Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38883)
*Lily Jiaxin Wan,Deming Chen,Klara Nahrstedt,Bo Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: QSA是一个量子感知的LLM引导进化搜索框架，用于自动化量子软件设计，通过三种量子特定引导降低验证成本并提供细粒度反馈，在IBM量子平台上显著提升多程序执行与错误缓解性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子软件对提升稀缺量子硬件的效率与可靠性至关重要，但其设计仍依赖手工启发式方法，往往次优且随硬件演进而过时。LLM引导的进化搜索虽有潜力自动探索复杂软件设计，但现有搜索框架缺乏量子特定支持：验证昂贵、反馈稀疏、异构量子程序需要不同优化目标。

Method: 提出QSA，一个面向LLM引导进化搜索的量子感知harness，为搜索提供三种量子特定引导：1）进化难度引导的核心集与近似评分以降低验证成本；2）静态与快照分析提供细粒度执行上下文；3）针对编译器pass和运行时策略的任务特定奖励。

Result: 在IBM Quantum平台的三个基准套件上评估：多程序执行中，QPU利用率较SOTA提升4.2%-9.5%，Hellinger保真度提升15.2%-19.5%；错误缓解中，缓解时间至少减少96.8%，同时保真度相当或更优。上述收益仅需6.9美元LLM API成本和11.3小时。

Conclusion: QSA通过引入量子特定引导，使LLM引导进化搜索能够高效自动化量子软件设计，在硬件效率和可靠性上取得显著提升且成本低廉，展示了量子感知搜索框架的实用价值。

Abstract: Quantum software is critical for improving the efficiency and reliability of scarce quantum hardware. However, its design still relies heavily on ad-hoc, handcrafted heuristics that are often suboptimal and quickly become obsolete as quantum hardware evolves. LLM-guided evolutionary search offers a promising way to automatically explore complex software designs, but existing search frameworks lack the quantum-specific support needed for efficient evolution: verification is expensive, feedback is sparse, and heterogeneous quantum programs require different optimization objectives. In this paper, we present QSA, a quantum-aware harness for LLM-guided evolutionary search toward automating quantum software design. QSA equips the search with three forms of quantum-specific guidance: an evolution-hardness-guided coreset and approximate scoring to reduce verification cost, static and snapshot analyses to provide fine-grained execution context, and task-specific rewards for compiler passes and runtime policies. We evaluate QSA on the IBM Quantum platform across three benchmark suites. For multiprogramming, QSA improves QPU utilization by 4.2%-9.5% and Hellinger fidelity by 15.2%-19.5% over the state of the art. For error mitigation, QSA reduces mitigation time by at least 96.8% while achieving comparable or better fidelity. These gains require only $6.9 in LLM API cost over 11.3 hours.

</details>


### [307] [Doing More with Less Tokens: Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning for Efficient Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38885)
*Haobin Li,Liang Jiang,Zhenyu Huang,Mouxing Yang,Xi Peng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出HERO，一个强化学习框架，用于训练token高效且解决率高的coding agents，通过轨迹级与回合级优化平衡解决率与token效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: coding agents在多轮环境交互中产生大量token开销，导致高成本与延迟；现有推理时方法（上下文操作、交互限制）虽提升token效率，却可能丢弃任务相关信息，难以平衡解决率与token效率，且缺乏对训练token高效agent的探索。

Method: 基于两个观察（Efficiency Variation：成功解决可用更少token；Entropy Correlation：无效行为与回合级熵相关），提出HERO强化学习框架，在策略优化中优先保证任务解决率，并在轨迹级和回合级鼓励高效推理模式。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified和SWE-bench Multilingual上的实验表明，与最先进的coding agents及强化学习方法相比，HERO在解决率与token效率之间取得了更优的权衡。

Conclusion: HERO为训练token高效的coding agents提供了通用范式，能够在保证解决性能的同时降低token开销，实现更好的效率与效果平衡。

Abstract: Recently, coding agents have emerged as a dominant paradigm for real-world software engineering (SWE) scenarios, which solve complex tasks through multi-turn interactions with development environments. However, frequent interactions with environments would inevitably introduce substantial token overhead, leading to high usage costs and latency. Although recent studies have explored reducing token usage by context manipulation and interaction limits at inference time, these approaches focus on improving token efficiency while overlooking the risk of discarding task-relevant information, thus struggling to balance the trade-off between resolution rate and token efficiency. In this paper, we study a more general paradigm without suffering from the limitation, i.e., training token-efficient coding agents with promising resolution performance, which is a highly-practical yet less-explored problem. To this end, we reveal two core observations in SWE scenarios: i) Efficiency Variation: successful resolution could be achieved with fewer tokens; ii) Entropy Correlation: unproductive behaviors are associated with turn-level entropy. Motivated by observations, we propose a novel reinforcement learning framework, dubbed HERO. Specifically, HERO prioritizes task resolution over token efficiency during policy optimization and encourages efficient reasoning patterns at both trajectory and turn levels. Extensive experiments on SWE-bench Verified and SWE-bench Multilingual demonstrate that HERO achieves a favorable trade-off between resolution rate and token efficiency compared with state-of-the-art coding agents and reinforcement learning methods.

</details>


### [308] [From Verification Failures to Reusable Guidance for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39022)
*Yuqing Zhai,Xiaohong Chen,Lingming Zhang,Sriram Vishwanath,Grigore Rosu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究将专家诊断验证失败的过程转化为可复用指导，结合K框架的可执行语言定义与一套规格构造、证明修复、充分性审计工具开展验证工作，在HumanEval上达到164/164成功，并通过KleverBench等测试评估其泛化性与局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体不仅需要证明程序满足规格，还需确认规格本身准确刻画了所需行为。因此，作者希望研究如何把专家对验证失败的诊断转化为可复用的指导，以支撑规格与证明的构建和复核。

Method: 方法结合K框架中的可执行语言定义，以及一组用于构造规格、修复证明和审计规格充分性的程序工具包。作者在HumanEval上开展人机引导的开发活动；构造12对经作者审核的干净/缺陷软件包，检验审计能否发现成功证明未解决的问题；并用KleverBench测试31个改变算子含义程序的规格与证明构建，与完整验收规则及同等长度通用建议进行比较。此外还开展人工复核的Optimism证明。

Result: 在HumanEval 164个Python任务上，经两次针对性修复后，以最终AI审计Pass判定衡量，达到164/164成功率。12对软件包全部通过K证明，且完成审计能识别全部缺陷并接受全部干净包。KleverBench比较中，与完整验收规则和同等长度通用建议相比，在两种模型与预算设置下结果不一。人工复核的Optimism证明在声明输入边界内、London语义与无界gas下，确立了六类操作的预期暂停回滚。

Conclusion: 研究展示了将验证失败诊断转化为可复用指导的可行性，并报告了面向“交付带可检验正确性论证程序”的智能体的进展、困难与经验；同时指出在资源限制下如何选择有用指导仍是后续工作方向。

Abstract: Coding agents need to establish that a program satisfies a specification and that the specification captures the requested behavior. We study how expert diagnosis of verification failures can become reusable guidance for this work. Our approach combines executable language definitions in the K framework with a kit of procedures for constructing specifications, repairing proofs, and auditing their adequacy. A human-guided development campaign on HumanEval, a benchmark of 164 Python programming tasks, achieves a 164/164 success rate with the semantics and the kit, measured by final AI audit Pass verdicts after two targeted repairs. To examine whether auditing detects problems that successful proofs leave unresolved, we construct 12 author-reviewed pairs of clean and defective packages. Every package passes its K proofs, and completed audits identify all defects and accept all clean packages. We then use KleverBench to test specification and proof construction for 31 programs with changed operator meanings. Comparisons with complete acceptance rules and equally long generic advice yield mixed results across two model and budget settings, motivating further work on selecting useful guidance within resource limits. Human-reviewed Optimism proofs establish expected pause reverts for six operations within declared input bounds under London semantics with unbounded gas. We report progress, difficulties, and lessons toward agents that deliver programs with checkable correctness arguments.

</details>


### [309] [Trustworthy Runtime Error Healing in Real-World Repositories: A Benchmark and Guardrail](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39086)
*Gou Tan,Pengfei Chen,Zhensu Sun,Jieke Shi,Junkai Chen,Ting Zhang,Weifeng Sun,Junda He,Shuai Liang,Chuanfu Zhang,Lwin Khin Shar,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出HealBench基准和HealGuard安全框架，将基于LLM的运行时错误修复推向真实代码库场景，兼顾修复有效性并首次系统性地解决执行LLM生成代码的安全隐患。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的运行时错误修复工作仅在小型竞赛程序上评估，且将LLM生成的代码直接植入运行中的进程所带来的安全隐患尚未被研究，距离真实代码库的实际应用存在明显差距。

Method: 构建HealBench基准，包含来自18个真实仓库的265个运行时错误，每个实例配有修补版本的参考执行，并提供统一框架让LLM智能体结合跨文件上下文与实时运行时状态进行修复；设计HealGuard，要求修复代码使用可分析的Python子集HealCore编写，并通过静态与动态污点分析检查修复改变的运行时状态是否会到达开发者保护的敏感操作。

Result: 评估了专用修复方法和三种通用编程智能体（配备三种主干LLM）：最佳设置能在38.11%的实例中恢复执行、28.68%通过目标测试，说明现有智能体已能修复相当比例的真实仓库级崩溃；但在通过测试的执行中，HealGuard标记出17.4%的实例其修复改变的状态可能到达受保护操作；在684个受控用例上，HealGuard能检出全部不安全案例，代价是68.42%的假阳性率。

Conclusion: LLM智能体已具备修复真实仓库级运行时错误的实用能力，但其中存在不可忽视的安全风险；HealGuard在保证高召回（检出全部不安全案例）的同时带来较高误报，揭示了安全与实用性之间的权衡，为运行时修复的实际部署提供了基准与安全机制基础。

Abstract: Runtime error healing lets a crashed program continue by generating code that repairs its live runtime state. Recent work shows that LLMs can generate such healing code, but it is evaluated only on small competition programs, and executing LLM-generated code inside a live process raises safety concerns that remain unaddressed. In this paper, we take LLM-based runtime healing toward practical use in real-world repositories. We first build HealBench, a benchmark of 265 runtime errors from 18 real-world repositories, each paired with a reference execution on the patched version. HealBench also provides a unified framework that lets LLM agents heal with cross-file context and live runtime state. We then design HealGuard, which requires healing code to be written in HealCore, an analyzable subset of Python, and uses static and dynamic taint analysis to check whether state changed by healing reaches operations protected by developers. We evaluate a dedicated healing method and three general coding agents with three backbone LLMs. The best setting resumes execution in 38.11% of instances and passes the target test in 28.68%, showing that existing agents can already heal a meaningful share of real repository-level crashes. However, among executions that pass, HealGuard flags 17.4% whose healing-changed state may reach a protected operation. On 684 controlled cases, HealGuard detects all unsafe cases, at the cost of a 68.42% false positive rate.

</details>


### [310] [EngramBench: A Capability-Grounded Benchmark for Skill-Evolution Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39284)
*Zhixuan Tan,Pengjie Gu,Zhao Li,Yihan Hu,Xu He,Dong Li,Jianye Hao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 EngramBench，一个以能力为基准、遵循"能力重叠但解法不重叠"公理的评测基准，用于区分智能体真正的能力抽象与历史训练数据的记忆泄漏，并揭示静态技能库对程序性记忆的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对大语言模型智能体技能演化的评测存在关键的"可识别性"问题：无法区分真正的能力抽象与机械式解法泄漏（即从历史训练数据中复制高度相似的代码）。这使评测结果的可信度受损，因此需要一个原则清晰、能剥离记忆污染的能力评测基准。

Method: 提出 EngramBench 基准，严格遵循"能力重叠、解法不重叠"的公理。该基准包含 30 个多样化学习任务与 13 个未见过的迁移任务，由 LLM 模拟用户驱动智能体进行交互式、长达数小时的多轮开发周期。研究在 48 条多小时的执行轨迹上进行广泛评测，并结合人类专家验证。

Result: 评测揭示了对程序性记忆的重要洞见：静态技能库无法神奇地跨越精确代码实现的"最后一公里"，该环节仍受基础模型自身推理能力的瓶颈制约；但技能库可作为不可或缺的"执行指南针"，通过使智能体避开代价高昂、消耗大量 token 的试错过程，真正的能力抽象能大幅削减冗余上下文膨胀，将整体编码时间减少超过 55%。

Conclusion: EngramBench 将评测范式从简单的模式匹配推进到对深层跨域能力迁移的可验证测量，为智能体技能演化研究提供了更严谨、能力导向的评估框架。

Abstract: While large language models have achieved remarkable success in isolated code generation, authentic software engineering requires sustained reasoning, complex state management, and continuous cross-domain abstraction. However, current evaluations of skill evolution in autonomous agents suffer from a critical identifiability problem: they structurally confound genuine capability abstraction with rote solution leakage (i.e., copying highly similar code from historical training data). To resolve this, we introduce EngramBench, a rigorous, capability-grounded benchmark governed by the strict axiom of capability overlap without solution overlap. Comprising 30 diverse learning tasks and 13 unseen transfer tasks, EngramBench challenges agents to navigate interactive, multi-hour development cycles driven by LLM-simulated users. Our extensive evaluation across 48 multi-hour execution trajectories -- corroborated by human-expert validation -- reveals a profound insight into procedural memory. We demonstrate that static skill banks do not magically bypass the "last mile" of exact code implementation, which remains bottlenecked by the base model's inherent reasoning limits. However, they serve as an indispensable execution compass. By navigating agents away from catastrophic, token-heavy trial-and-error, genuine capability abstraction slashes redundant context bloat and reduces overall coding time by over 55%. Ultimately, EngramBench shifts the evaluation paradigm from trivial pattern matching to the verifiable measurement of deep, cross-domain capability transfer.

</details>


### [311] [Self-Spec Verifiable Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39568)
*Jiaru Qian,Yihong Dong,Yongmin Li,Hao Zhu,Bin Gu,Ge Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出VeriCodeBench基准（400个C/Java/Rust/Python语言原生问题），在"自生成规格"协议下端到端评估LLM生成可形式化验证代码的能力，并推出CodeNova方法提升该能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM可验证代码生成基准存在两大局限：(I)主要分阶段评估规格与代码生成，且代码生成常以理想规格为条件，忽略了阶段性强是否等于端到端成功；(II)多聚焦单一面向证明的语言和数学结构化任务，对软件开发中常见任务覆盖不足。

Method: 提出VeriCodeBench基准，LLM在整个流程中仅依赖自身生成的规格与代码，包含400个覆盖C、Java、Rust、Python的语言原生问题，评估规格覆盖率、代码有效性与联合问题级成功率。并提出CodeNova，通过约束引导的规格生成显式化需求，并利用验证器反馈指导针对性实现修复。

Result: 实验显示自生成规格仍是主要瓶颈，且更复杂的规格未必带来更高验证成功率；CodeNova在所有评估指标上大幅提升性能，使Claude Sonnet 5在自生成规格协议下取得最强结果。

Conclusion: 自生成规格是可验证代码生成的关键瓶颈，VeriCodeBench提供了更贴近软件开发的多语言端到端评测，CodeNova验证了约束引导规格与验证反馈修复的有效性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) may generate unreliable code on corner cases missed by testing, while formal verification can provide machine-checkable guarantees. Recently, researchers have proposed several benchmarks to evaluate the capabilities of LLMs in generating formally verifiable code, where LLMs need to formulate formal specifications, generate the corresponding code, and verify its correctness. However, existing benchmarks have two key limitations: (I) They primarily evaluate specification and code generation stage-wise, with code generation typically conditioned on an oracle specification. This setup overlooks whether strong stage-wise performance translates into end-to-end success. (II)They mainly focus on a single proof-oriented language and mathematically structured tasks, offering limited coverage of tasks common in software development. In this paper, we introduce VeriCodeBench, a benchmark for self-spec verifiable code generation, where the LLM relies solely on its own generated specification and code throughout the entire process. VeriCodeBench contains 400 language-native problems across C, Java, Rust, and Python, covering practical concerns in software development. We evaluate specification coverage, code validity, and joint problem-level success. We further introduce CodeNova to enhance the capabilities of LLMs in self-spec verifiable code generation. CodeNova makes requirements explicit through constraint-guided specification and uses verifier feedback to guide targeted implementation repairs. Experimental results reveal that self-generated specifications remain a major bottleneck, while providing more sophisticated specifications may not necessarily lead to higher verification success rates. CodeNova substantially improves performance across all evaluation metrics, enabling Claude Sonnet 5 to achieve the strongest results under the self-spec protocol.

</details>


### [312] [COMPASS: Predicting the Relationship of Multiple Patches for Vulnerabilities with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39783)
*Yi Song,Dongchen Xie,Xiaoyuan Xie,He Zhang,Lin Xu,Chunying Zhou,Zhi Jin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究多补丁漏洞中补丁关系的自动识别问题，通过人工分析约1K个真实多补丁漏洞并访谈开发者，总结出六种补丁关系，提出基于大语言模型的COMPASS方法，在300个多补丁CVE基准上平均超越SOTA 85.04%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代软件高度依赖代码复用，上游漏洞修复无法自动传播到下游代码库，下游维护者需手动采用补丁。而单个漏洞常对应多个补丁，不同补丁关系意味着不同的采纳策略，这大大增加了下游补丁采纳的复杂性，因此需要自动识别补丁之间的关系。

Method: 作者先人工检查约1K个真实世界的多补丁漏洞并访谈有经验的开发者，总结出六种典型补丁关系：Merge、Mirror、Better Solution、Fixing-of-Fixing、Collaboration 和 Separation。基于这些观察提出COMPASS，利用大语言模型自动预测多补丁漏洞的关系。给定一个CVE作为输入，COMPASS采用四阶段流水线：(i) 识别补丁组并预扫描显式关系；(ii) 进行单个补丁分析；(iii) 通过层次引导提示推断关系实例；(iv) 验证推断结果的完整性与一致性。输出为补丁组内预测关系并以关系图可视化。

Result: 在包含300个多补丁CVE的基准上评估COMPASS，并与主流基于学习的方法和LLM基线进行比较。结果表明该方法具有强且一致的预测效果，平均超越SOTA 85.04%。作者还公开了在线查询网站以支持社区复用补丁关系知识。

Conclusion: COMPASS能够有效自动识别多补丁漏洞中的补丁关系，显著优于现有方法，并有望帮助下游维护者理解补丁关系、制定更合理的补丁采纳策略，同时通过公开网站促进补丁关系知识的社区复用。

Abstract: Modern software heavily relies on code reuse, so upstream vulnerability fixes do not automatically propagate to downstream codebases. Downstream maintainers must manually adopt patches to eliminate known risks. In practice, a single vulnerability often corresponds to multiple patches, which greatly complicates downstream patch adoption because different patch relationships imply different adoption strategies.
